### [1] [Distributed SDN-Based Communication Architecture for the Pods4Rail System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25711)
*Dingyang Liu,Dereje Mechal Molla,Léo Mendiboure,Marion Berbineau*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合软件定义网络和多接入边缘计算的分布式通信架构，用于未来多式联运交通系统，以低延迟、高可靠的方式协调自动驾驶车辆和移动基础设施，解决了传统集中式控制的延迟、可扩展性和适应性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来多式联运交通系统需要低延迟、高可靠的通信基础设施来协调自动驾驶车辆和移动基础设施，但传统集中式控制架构在高动态环境中存在延迟高、可扩展性有限、适应性差等问题。

Method: 提出一种集成SDN和MEC的分布式通信架构，采用层次化控制结构（区域控制器+边缘控制器），实现低延迟接口管理和边缘自治，定义了代表性场景的操作流程和控制逻辑。

Result: 控制器通信和流设置延迟低于现有文献报道的水平；架构支持区域策略协调与边缘级自治相结合，可在无中央依赖的情况下实现本地故障切换和自适应接口管理。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式架构通过SDN和MEC的结合以及层次化控制，能够有效满足多式联运交通系统对低延迟、高可靠和灵活可编程网络的需求。

Abstract: Future multimodal transportation systems require reliable, low-latency communication infrastructures to coordinate autonomous vehicles and moving infrastructure across rail and road networks. Traditional centralized control architectures struggle to meet these requirements in highly dynamic environments due to increased latency, limited scalability, and poor adaptability to changing network conditions. To address this, we propose a distributed communication architecture integrating Software-Defined Networking (SDN) and Multi-Access Edge Computing (MEC), that can create a flexible, programmable and lowlatency network. Results show controller communication and flow setup latency between edge SDN controllers and Pods are lower than reported in literature. The framework uses hierarchical control with regional and edge controllers to support low-latency interface management and edge autonomy. Operational workflows and control logic are defined as representative scenarios. The architecture combines regional policy coordination with edge-level autonomy, enabling local failover and adaptive interface management without central dependency.

</details>

### [2] [EvoFlock: evolved inverse design of multi-agent motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25280)
*Craig Reynolds*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动调整多智能体运动模型参数的方法，通过用户定义的目标函数和遗传算法进行逆向设计优化，以生成期望的群体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体运动模拟（如鸟群、人潮、车辆交通）中，模型参数众多且交互复杂非线性，手动调整参数繁琐且难以实现期望的群体行为变化。

Method: 采用逆向设计方法，通过用户定义的目标（适应度/损失）函数度量期望的群体行为，并使用遗传算法进行优化。

Result: 用于基本群集行为的目标函数奖励与邻居保持适当间距、以期望速度飞行以及避开障碍物。有趣的是，鸟群中生动的对齐行为似乎源于维持个体间适当间距。

Conclusion: 遗传算法结合目标函数能有效自动调参，且发现群体对齐行为可由间距维持这一简单规则涌现。

Abstract: This paper describes an automatic method for adjusting or tuning models of multi-agent motion. Simulating the motion of bird flocks, human crowds, vehicle traffic, and other multi-agent systems is a widely used technique. These simulations model the behavior of a single group member (bird, human, or vehicle). The group behaviors (flock, crowd, traffic) emerge from interactions between group members. These models typically have many numerical control parameters. Even if each parameter is intuitive in isolation, their interaction can be complex and nonlinear. It is challenging to determine which parameters to adjust for the desired change in group behavior. Changing one aspect of group behavior often causes other aspects to change, leading to a tedious process of incremental changes. This work takes an inverse design approach. The desired group behavior is measured with a user-defined objective(/fitness/loss) function and optimized with a genetic algorithm. The objective function used here for basic flocking rewards proper spacing with neighbors, flying near a desired speed, and avoiding obstacles. Interestingly, the vivid alignment seen in bird flocks appears to emerge from maintaining proper spacing between flockmates.

</details>

### [3] [Fifty Years of Specification Completeness: What Aviation Certification Tells AI Governance About Epoch Limits, Proof Surfaces, and the Structural Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25120)
*Christo Zietsman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文借鉴航空软件认证（DO-178C/DO-330）三十年来的三项结构性要求——结构化治理链接、上下文约束有效性、客观证据架构——指出当前AI治理文档（系统提示词、AGENTS.md文件等）普遍缺乏这些特性。作者证明这些要求可在文档层面独立于随机系统进行评估，并映射为三个结构性发现：治理文档有效性的纪元限制、作为再验证反馈机制的证明表面、以及AI治理工具中结构完备性要求的缺失。实证研究表明37%的AI治理文档低于结构质量阈值，而PromptQ的七原则框架在治理文档层面操作化了这些要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空领域三十年来强制执行的结构性治理要求在AI治理文档层面完全缺失，没有任何现有框架要求AI治理文档具备这些结构属性。尽管航空标准制定机构已承认其框架对AI系统失效，但这些要求可在文档层面（非系统层面）转移应用。

Method: 将DO-178C的可追溯性架构、DO-330的重新认证触发条件、DO-178C的客观证据要求映射到AI治理文档的三个结构性发现：纪元限制、证明表面、结构完备性缺失。辅以arXiv:2604.21090的实证研究（37%文档低于阈值）和PromptQ框架的操作化实现。

Result: 识别出三个结构性发现：1）治理文档有效性的纪元限制；2）证明表面作为再验证反馈机制；3）AI治理工具缺乏结构完备性要求。实证发现37%的AI治理文档低于结构性质量阈值。提出PromptQ七原则框架作为解决方案。

Conclusion: 航空业的结构性治理要求可在文档层面独立于随机系统应用，当前AI治理文档普遍不满足这些要求，亟需引入类似的结构化标准以确保AI治理的严谨性和可靠性。

Abstract: Aviation software certification has operationalised three structural requirements for governed software systems since 1992: structured governance linkage between governing specifications and operational evidence, context-bounded validity that triggers revalidation when operational context changes, and an objective evidence architecture that defines what proof means and what makes it sufficient. These requirements appear in DO-178C and DO-330 and are enforced through FAA and EASA certification.
  No existing framework requires these structural properties as intrinsic properties of individual AI governance documents. A system prompt, an AGENTS.md file, a governance policy, or a task envelope can be deployed without satisfying any of the three requirements aviation has enforced for three decades. Aviation is the most technically rigorous instance: its standard-setting bodies have acknowledged that their frameworks break down for AI systems, yet none requires these properties of individual governance documents.
  Aviation's structural requirements break down at the system level because AI systems are non-deterministic, but remain transferable at the document level: the governance artifact is a static artifact whose structural properties can be evaluated independently of the stochastic system it governs.
  The paper maps DO-178C's traceability architecture, DO-330's requalification triggers, and DO-178C's objective evidence requirements onto three structural findings: epoch limits on governance document validity, proof surfaces as the revalidation feedback mechanism, and the absence of structural completeness requirements in AI governance instruments. An empirical companion (arXiv:2604.21090) found that 37% of AI governance documents fall below the structural quality threshold. PromptQ's seven-principle framework operationalises these requirements at the governance document layer.

</details>


### [4] [LLM4MTLs: Automated Generation and Empirical Evaluation of Model Transformation Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25193)
*Bowen Jiang,Nathan Hagel,Haowei Cheng,Benedikt Jutz,Arne Lange,Weixing Zhang,Rahul Sharma,Ralf Reussner,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM4MTLs自动化工作流，系统探索少样本提示、语法提示和辅助方法包含等策略组合，用于提升LLM生成模型转换代码的语法和语义质量。在四种MTL和三种LLM上的评估表明：少样本提示一致提升语法质量，但语义正确性提升不均且依赖语言；语法提示需与少样本结合才有效；LLM选择影响部分语言的语法质量但语义影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型转换语言（MTL）的正确开发既需要语言特定知识又需要领域知识，极具挑战性。尽管LLM可用于MTL代码生成，但由于训练数据和可执行示例有限，LLM生成的代码往往存在语法无效或语义不可用问题，亟需系统性的提示策略优化方法。

Method: 提出LLM4MTLs自动化工作流，系统探索提示构建方式（少样本提示、语法提示、辅助方法包含的组合），构建覆盖四种MTL（ATL、ETL、QVTo和Reactions语言）的评估套件（含可执行参考脚本和手动编写测试套件），在三种LLM上进行评估，使用语法和语义度量指标。

Result: 少样本提示一致提升四种MTL的语法质量，但语义正确性提升不均衡且依赖具体语言；ATL的Pass@1在所有策略和模型下保持不变；语法提示与少样本结合能稳定代码生成，但单独使用可能无效甚至适得其反；辅助方法可作为补充放大器；LLM选择影响部分语言（特别是ETL和QVTo）的语法正确性和相似度，但对语义正确性影响有限。

Conclusion: 少样本提示主要改善表层语法而非深层转换语义；语法提示需与少样本示例协同使用；提示策略的效果高度依赖具体MTL和LLM组合，需根据目标语言定制化设计提示策略。

Abstract: Model transformation languages (MTLs) are domain-specific languages for transforming models conforming to a given metamodel into other models, including textual models such as source code. Developing correct model transformations is challenging, requiring both language-specific and domain knowledge, and motivating the use of large language models (LLMs) for MTL code generation. However, due to limited training data and executable examples, LLM-generated MTL code is often not syntactically valid or semantically usable out of the box. This paper presents LLM4MTLs, an automated workflow for constructing and comparing prompting strategies for LLM-generated MTL code, together with an evaluation suite and an empirical evaluation. The workflow systematically explores prompt constructions combining few-shot prompting, grammar prompting, and helper method inclusion, and evaluates them using syntactic and semantic metrics. We construct an evaluation suite spanning four MTLs (ATL, ETL, QVTo, and the Reactions language) with executable reference scripts and manually written test suites, and evaluate across three LLMs. We find that few-shot prompting consistently improves syntactic quality across all four MTLs while gains in semantic correctness are uneven and language-dependent. For ATL, Pass@1 remains unchanged across all strategies and models, indicating that few-shot prompting improves surface-level syntax more readily than deep transformation semantics. Grammar prompting stabilizes code generation when combined with few-shot examples, but in isolation it can be ineffective or even counterproductive for certain model-language combinations. Including helper methods as a complementary amplifier can also be beneficial. Finally, LLM choice influences syntactic correctness and similarity for certain MTLs, particularly ETL and QVTo, while its influence on semantic correctness remains limited.

</details>


### [5] [Tensor-Based Batch Fuzzing with Adaptive Perturbation Scaling for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25239)
*Guanqin Zhang,Yulei Sui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于规范感知的批量模糊测试框架，通过自适应扰动缩放和将输入约束嵌入网络为不可训练层，实现了单次批量处理多个规范实例，在三个基准测试上相比顺序基线方法实现了高达40倍的吞吐量提升和4倍的违规发现量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有覆盖引导的DNN顺序模糊测试框架存在两个主要局限：一是每轮迭代仅处理一个输入，测试吞吐量低；二是对所有输入维度应用统一扰动预算，无法精确探索异构输入空间。

Method: 提出了规范感知的批量模糊测试框架：(1) 自适应扰动缩放：根据规范定义的可行范围（上下界间隙）计算突变步长，支持全局各向同性和逐维度各向异性两种缩放方式；(2) 将输入约束和输出属性检查作为不可训练层嵌入网络，构建包装模型，实现单次批量推理处理B个规范实例。

Result: 在TrafficSigns、Cifar100和TinyImageNet三个基准测试的6个网络和400+规范上，张量基模糊测试相比顺序基线方法实现了高达40倍的吞吐量提升和4倍的违规发现量。

Conclusion: 规范感知的批量模糊测试框架通过自适应扰动缩放和批量处理机制，有效克服了传统顺序模糊测试的吞吐量瓶颈和探索精度不足问题，显著提升了DNN规范引导模糊测试的效率。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains such as autonomous driving and medical diagnosis, yet their opaque, high-dimensional parameter spaces make it difficult to systematically assess model reliability on unseen inputs. Existing coverage-guided sequential fuzzing frameworks for DNNs inherit a one-input-per-iteration design from traditional software fuzzing and apply uniform perturbation budgets across all input dimensions, limiting both testing throughput (i.e., inputs processed per unit time) and the precision of input-space exploration. We present a new specification-aware batch fuzzing framework with adaptive perturbation scaling that addresses both limitations. Rather than relying on a fixed global perturbation radius epsilon, our approach derives mutation step sizes from specification-defined feasible ranges (the gap between lower and upper bounds) using a shared scale factor. This scaling can be applied either as a global scalar (isotropic) or as per-dimension step sizes (anisotropic), keeping perturbations consistent with the underlying constraint structure. As a result, the fuzzer can explore input spaces with heterogeneous feature scales more effectively across all specifications in the batch. We embed input constraints and output property checks directly into the network as non-trainable layers, yielding a wrapped model that processes B specification instances in a single batched iteration, substantially improving fuzzing efficiency and counterexample exploration. We evaluate our framework extensively on three benchmarks, covering six networks and over 400 specifications across TrafficSigns, Cifar100, and TinyImageNet. Our tensor-based fuzzing achieves up to 40X higher throughput and 4X more violations than the sequential baseline under the same time budget, demonstrating significantly improved effectiveness in specification-guided fuzzing.

</details>


### [6] [How Do Developers Maintain and Evolve Their Agents' Instructions? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25257)
*Gianmario Voria,Alfonso Cannavale,Andrea De Lucia,Yutaro Kashiwa,Gemma Catolino,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了自主编码代理中的代理上下文文件（ACF）的演化过程，通过大规模仓库挖掘，建立了ACF变更分类体系，分析了不同类型变更与代码质量的关系及其在开发周期中的时间模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理在软件开发中越来越普及，但引入代理带来了治理、可追溯性和行为控制等挑战。代理上下文文件（ACF）作为指导代理的结构化指令机制已被广泛使用，但人们对这些文件如何维护、其演化与代码开发的关系知之甚少。

Method: 采用大规模仓库挖掘研究，结合包含ACF和代理生成提交的仓库。在提交级别重建ACF演化，使用定性方法对变更进行分类，并与代码质量指标进行关联分析，通过统计分析和假设检验评估不同维护类别的差异。

Result: 建立了基于软件维护理论的ACF变更分类体系，识别了不同类型ACF变更与代码质量结果之间的关联，揭示了ACF变更在开发生命周期中的时间模式。

Conclusion: 本研究为治理自主编码代理的ACF设计提供了经验基础，有助于理解代理驱动开发中指令文件的演化规律及其对代码质量的影响。

Abstract: Context. Autonomous coding agents are increasingly used in software development, shifting parts of the engineering process to AI assistance. While this automation brings clear benefits, it introduces challenges in governance, traceability, and control over agent behavior. Agent Context Files (ACFs) have emerged as a practical mechanism to guide agents through structured instructions, yet little is known about how these artifacts are maintained and how their evolution relates to code development. Objective. This paper plans to investigate the evolution of ACFs and their role in agent-driven development. Specifically, we (1) classify ACF changes through a taxonomy grounded in software maintenance theory, (2) analyze how different types of changes are associated with code quality outcomes, and (3) examine their temporal patterns across the development lifecycle. Method. We conduct a large-scale mining study combining repositories with ACFs and agent-generated commits. We reconstruct ACF evolution at the commit level, classify changes using a qualitative approach, and analyze their association with code quality metrics. Statistical analyses and hypotheses are used to evaluate differences across maintenance categories, to inform future design of ACFs for governing autonomous coding agents.

</details>


### [7] [Semantic Code Clone Detection: Are We There Yet?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25272)
*Zhiwei Xu,Weixian Deng,Xuyang Liu,Xiaolin Peng,Jiabao Gao,Tian Qiu,Hai Wan,Xibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了当前最先进的语义代码克隆检测器在基准测试环境之外的泛化能力。通过提出一个由八种变换算子组成的克隆算子框架，构造分布偏移但语义等价的Type-4克隆实例，评估了11种代表性检测器。结果发现所有方法在真实场景下性能显著下降，现有检测器严重依赖词汇和结构线索的捷径学习，而非鲁棒的语义理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语义代码克隆检测方法在基准数据集上表现优异，但尚不清楚这些高绩效是否真正反映了对程序语义等价性的捕捉能力，还是仅仅利用了数据集特定的模式。需要系统性研究这些方法的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了一个克隆算子框架，包含8种源自Type-2和Type-3克隆变体的变换算子，用于构造分布偏移但语义等价的Type-4克隆实例。在BigCloneBench数据集上评估了11种代表性检测器（涵盖基于token、树和图的方法）。

Result: 所有评估的方法在分布偏移的测试场景下均出现显著的性能下降；现有检测器主要依赖基于词汇和结构线索的捷径学习，而非鲁棒的语义理解。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的语义代码克隆检测器在真实场景中泛化能力有限，指出了未来研究的重要方向。

Abstract: Code clone detection has been extensively studied for decades, and recent approaches have begun reporting remarkably high performance for semantic (Type-4) clones on benchmark datasets. However, it remains unclear whether these results reflect a genuine ability to capture semantic equivalence between programs, or simply an ability to exploit dataset-specific patterns.
  In this paper, we present the first systematic empirical study investigating the generalizability of state-of-the-art (SOTA) semantic code clone detectors beyond benchmark evaluation settings. Inspired by the inherent inclusion relationship among clone types, we propose a clone operator framework consisting of eight transformation operators derived from Type-2 and Type-3 clone variations. Using these operators, we construct distribution-shifted yet semantically equivalent Type-4 clone instances and evaluate 11 representative detectors spanning token-based, tree-based, and graph-based paradigms on the real-world BigCloneBench dataset. Our results reveal substantial performance degradation across all evaluated approaches, despite their strong benchmark performance. Further analyses show that existing detectors heavily rely on shortcut learning based on lexical and structural cues rather than robust semantic understanding. Our findings suggest that current SOTA semantic code clone detectors exhibit limited generalizability in real-world scenarios, highlighting important avenues for future research.

</details>


### [8] [LibEvoBench: Probing Temporal Knowledge Stratification in Code Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25402)
*Daniele Cipollone,Sergey Titov,Maliheh Izadi,Egor Bogomolov,Arie van Deursen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 LibEvoBench 基准和 SEUS 指标，系统评估 LLM 在多版本 API 推理中的能力。实验表明，现有模型对 API 版本演化缺乏感知：即使指定目标版本也无改善，而提供相关文档能显著提升准确率。这揭示了当前训练范式在时序知识建模上的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型软件项目常依赖旧版本库，但 LLM 训练数据混杂多个时间段的代码，缺乏显式的版本感知机制，导致生成代码时出现"时代错位"错误——调用了错误版本的 API。

Method: 构建 LibEvoBench 多任务基准，涵盖多个广泛使用的 Python 库的多个版本；提出软件演化理解分数（SEUS）衡量模型在演化 API 上的一致性。

Result: 顶级模型对 API 版本演化几乎无感知：在演化 API 上性能显著下降，而稳定 API 在不同版本间表现一致。仅指定目标版本无帮助，提供相关文档能大幅提升准确率。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 训练范式缺乏时序基础的知识建模能力，需要探索新的方法使代码生成具备时间感知能力。

Abstract: Large software projects often depend on older versions of libraries, even as APIs continue to evolve across releases. This creates a challenge for LLMs: they must maintain knowledge of multiple API versions, not merely the latest or most common one. However, current LLMs are trained on temporally mixed corpora and lack explicit mechanisms for such version-specific reasoning, leading to anachronistic errors - calling APIs as they exist in a different library version. To systematically evaluate this phenomenon, we introduce LibEvoBench, a multi-task benchmark spanning multiple versions of widely used Python libraries, along with a new metric, the Software Evolution Understanding Score (SEUS), to measure models' consistency when working with evolving APIs. Our results show that state-of-the-art models are largely version-oblivious: performance degrades for evolving APIs, while for stable APIs it remains the same across versions. Moreover, simply specifying the target version provides no benefit, while relevant documentation significantly boosts models' accuracy. These findings highlight a systematic limitation of current training paradigms and motivate new approaches for temporally grounded knowledge in code generation.

</details>


### [9] [Project-wise Comparison of Software Birthmarks Using Weighted Partial Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25418)
*Nikolay Fedorov,Akito Monden,Hiroki Inayoshi,Haruaki Tamada,Masateru Tsunoda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对称聚合的项目级软件胎记比较框架，通过加权机制和部分相似性方法，有效解决了项目级代码复用检测中的部分复用和小模块偶然相似性两大挑战，实验表明该方法在35个Java项目上优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件胎记相似度度量通常在模块级（如源代码文件或类文件）进行，但实际软件复用常发生在项目级，且仅复用部分模块。这带来了两个关键挑战：(1) 部分复用——被复用的模块仅占项目的一小部分；(2) 小模块的偶然相似性——可能导致误报。现有方法缺乏针对项目级部分复用场景的有效方案。

Method: (1) 建立一个基于对称聚合模块级相似度的项目级胎记比较框架；(2) 提出加权方案，为较大模块赋予更高权重，减少小模块噪声匹配的影响；(3) 提出部分相似性方法，聚焦于前K个高度相似的模块对，实现部分复用的鲁棒检测。

Result: 在10个类别、35个开源Java项目上评估，以同一项目不同版本作为复用案例。使用软件胎记的两个互补属性——弹性和可信度，通过调和平均进行综合评估。结果表明所提方法持续优于现有方法，实现了鲁棒且稳定的项目级部分代码复用检测。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于对称聚合的项目级胎记比较框架，结合加权机制和部分相似性方法，能有效应对项目级部分代码复用检测中的挑战，在弹性和可信度的调和平均指标上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Software birthmarks provide a robust approach to detecting code plagiarism even under substantial modifications, while distinguishing independently developed software. Existing similarity measures are typically applied at the module level (e.g., source or class files). However, in practice, software reuse often occurs at the project level, where only a subset of modules may be reused. This setting introduces two key challenges: (1) partial reuse, where reused modules constitute only a small fraction of the project, and (2) incidental similarity from small modules, which can lead to false positives.
  In this paper, we establish a framework for project-wise birthmark comparison based on a symmetric aggregation of module-level similarities. On top of this framework, we propose two complementary mechanisms to address the above challenges. First, we introduce a weighting scheme that assigns higher importance to larger modules, reducing the influence of noisy matches from small modules. Second, we propose a partial similarity method that focuses on the top fraction of highly similar module pairs, enabling robust detection of partial reuse.
  We evaluate the proposed approach on 35 open-source Java projects across ten categories, where different versions of the same project are treated as reuse cases. The dataset and experimental artifacts are made publicly available to support reproducibility. Performance is assessed using two complementary properties of software birthmarks, resilience and credibility, combined via their harmonic mean. The results show that the proposed method consistently outperforms existing approaches, achieving robust and stable detection of partial code reuse at the project level.

</details>


### [10] [Unlocking Model Potentials Through Adaptive Multi-Agent Scaffolding for Efficient Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25514)
*Yang Chen,Aliya Ahmad,Yiheng Zhou,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出icat-agent，一种去中心化多智能体架构，通过事件驱动消息传递替代共享上下文，并利用基于评估指标的问题质量检查动态调整工作流程，在SWE-bench基准测试中显著优于现有方法，最高解决67.4%的SWE-bench Pro问题，同时降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体处理复杂软件工程任务时，共享上下文机制导致上下文退化与污染，难以有效解决模糊和不完整的复杂缺陷描述问题。

Method: 提出icat-agent去中心化多智能体框架，采用同步事件驱动消息传递替代共享上下文；引入基于评估指标的问题质量检查，针对高质量问题并行补丁生成与验证，低质量问题则先进行初步探索。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上提升3.6-8.4%，SWE-bench Pro上提升6.3-18.5%；平均成本比多智能体Claude Code基线降低$1.18/实例；icat-agent+GPT-5.4-xhigh达到67.4%的SWE-bench Pro解决率，比当前最优结果(59.10%)高出8.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的智能体框架（如icat-agent）能显著释放固定模型的潜在能力，同一模型在icat-agent下比现有框架解决更多问题。

Abstract: Resolving issues with ambiguous and incomplete descriptions, particularly concerning complex bugs, requires a sophisticated, long-horizon workflow. Agents must navigate codebases to locate the root cause, reproduce the failure, implement a fix, and validate the resulting patch. Inefficient context management, thereby, can lead to rapid context degradation and context poisoning, preventing successful resolution. We propose icat-agent, a decentralized, multi-agent scaffolding that replaces shared context with synchronous, event-based message passing. Utilizing a rubric-based issue quality check, icat-agent strategically pivots its workflow: it initiates parallel patching and validation for well-defined issues, while deploying preliminary exploration for low-quality ones. A comprehensive evaluation of icat-agent on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro demonstrates that it consistently outperforms prominent baselines across all difficulty levels, including SWE-agent, mini-SWE-agent, and Claude Code, while using the same underlying models, improving by 3.6-8.4% on SWE-bench Verified and 6.3-18.5% on SWE-bench Pro. icat-agent is also computationally efficient, reducing the average cost by $1.18 per instance compared with the multi-agent Claude Code baseline. Our findings reveal that a robust scaffold such as icat-agent unlocks substantial latent capability within a fixed model, with the same backbone resolving markedly more issues under icat-agent than under existing scaffolds. icat-agent +GPT-5.4-xhigh resolves 67.4% of SWE-bench Pro problems, outperforming the current best result on SWE-bench Pro (59.10%, mini-SWE-agent+GPT-5.4-xhigh) by 8.3 percentage points.

</details>


### [11] [The impact of artificial intelligence on enterprise software user roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25525)
*Isabel Unger,Elizangela Valarini,Martin Schrepp,Nina Hollender,Gabriela Rocha,Erik Bertram*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过专家访谈（n=20）和参与式工作坊（n=24），定性调查了AI如何改变SAP BTP平台上的软件开发角色与职责，发现运营任务自动化、人机协作增强、对Agentic AI依赖增加等显著变化，并指出现有用户角色框架（如BTP用户类型矩阵）需要适应这些转变，呼吁修订角色分类、建立新的治理监督职能、更新AI原生企业软件的设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正在重塑软件开发工作的本质，但对企业平台中用户角色、工作流和协作模式的具体影响尚缺乏深入理解，特别是针对SAP BTP这类企业级平台。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，结合专家访谈（n=20）和参与式工作坊（n=24），在SAP BTP平台上进行调查。

Result: 发现三大转变：运营任务日益自动化、人机协作不断扩展、对Agentic AI系统的依赖持续增长；现有角色框架（如BTP用户类型矩阵）需要适应性调整。

Conclusion: 劳动力格局正处于转型期，需要修订角色分类体系、建立新的治理和监督职能，并更新AI原生企业软件的设计方法。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is rapidly reshaping the nature of work in software development, transforming user roles, workflows, and collaboration patterns across enterprise platforms. This qualitative study investigates how AI alters professional responsibilities within the context of SAP's Business Technology Platform (BTP), combining expert interviews (n=20) and a participatory workshop (n=24). The results reveal substantial shifts in day-to-day tasks and roles in the development domain, characterized by increasing automation of operational tasks, expanding human-AI collaboration, and growing reliance on agentic AI systems. The study further identifies significant implications for existing user-role frameworks, such as the BTP User Type Matrix, which requires adaptation as the workforce is undergoing significant role specific changes. Collectively, these findings highlight a workforce landscape in transition and underscore the need for revised role taxonomies, new governance and oversight functions, and updated design approaches for AI-native enterprise software systems.

</details>


### [12] [Evaluating LLMs on Real-World Software Performance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25530)
*Ezgi Sarıkayak,Wenchao Gu,Hesham Ghonim,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Pro是一个基于仓库级别的软件性能优化基准，包含102个专家编写的优化任务，评估显示当前LLM在运行时和内存优化方面远不及专家水平（专家平均加速15.5倍，峰值内存减少171.3倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码优化基准过于简化，仅关注孤立函数或单一性能指标，忽略了真实代码库中执行时间与内存的权衡、测量噪声以及不同输入数据带来的变异性。

Method: 从开源项目中收集102个专家编写的优化，构建仓库级基准SWE-Pro，通过参数化测试在噪声感知条件下评估运行时、峰值内存和时间加权内存使用（TWMU）。

Result: 当前LLM的运行时优化收益可忽略不计，内存优化几乎不存在；专家在91.2%的任务中实现运行时改进，65.7%的任务中实现峰值内存改进，平均加速15.5倍，峰值内存减少171.3倍。

Conclusion: 当前LLM能力与专家级工程优化之间存在巨大差距，SWE-Pro为弥合这一差距提供了有效的评估基准。

Abstract: Software performance optimization is a notoriously complex and manual task. Despite the growing use of Large Language Models (LLMs) for code refinement, we still lack benchmarks that capture how optimization actually happens in real-world codebases. Existing frameworks often oversimplify the problem by focusing on isolated functions or a single performance metric, missing the critical trade-offs between execution time and memory footprint, the inherent noise of the measurement environment, and the variability introduced by different input data and execution conditions. We address this by introducing SWE-Pro, a repository-level benchmark derived from 102 expert-written optimizations from open-source projects. Unlike previous benchmarks, SWE-Pro pairs each task with parameterized tests to evaluate runtime, peak memory, and Time-Weighted Memory Usage (TWMU) across varying input data and execution conditions under noise-aware measurement conditions. Our evaluation shows that current LLMs struggle significantly: runtime gains are negligible, and memory optimizations are nearly non-existent. This stands in sharp contrast to expert implementations, which achieve an aggregate speedup of 15.5x and peak memory reduction of 171.3x over benchmark tasks. Expert-written improvements are observed in 91.2% of tasks for runtime and 65.7% for peak memory. Our findings expose a substantial gap between current LLM capabilities and the demands of expert-level engineering.

</details>


### [13] [On the Viability of Requirements Generation From Code: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25550)
*Alexander Korn,Jone Bartel,Max Unterbusch,Andreas Vogelsang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于LLM和RAG的智能方法从源代码生成需求数据集的可行性。研究发现，LLM无法可靠地生成未实现的需求、高质量需求以及合成需求坏味，且单个人类审核也不足以可靠检测需求坏味。结论是，目前利用LLM生成代码-需求数据集尚不可行，需要人类监督，尤其是质量保证方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程中的实证研究因缺乏将源代码与对应需求配对的充分数据集而受阻。利用大语言模型从现有代码库合成需求是一条诱人的途径，作者旨在探究这一方法的可行性，以创建能模拟现实并促进实证RE研究的数据集。

Method: 采用基于LLM和RAG支持的智能体方法，从源代码生成需求，通过人机协同验证需求的实现状态，并合成引入需求坏味和未实现的需求。通过实验评估该方法在三个关键维度上的表现：未实现需求生成的可靠性、需求质量、以及需求坏味合成的可靠性。

Result: LLM无法可靠地生成未实现的需求、高质量需求以及合成需求坏味。同时，无论是LLM还是单一人机协同方式，都无法可靠地检测需求坏味。

Conclusion: 利用LLM生成代码-需求数据集目前尚不可行，需要人类监督，尤其是质量保证方面。作者对此进行了批判性反思，并为研究者和实践者总结了经验教训。

Abstract: Empirical research in Requirements Engineering is hampered by a lack of adequate datasets that pair source code with corresponding requirements. A tempting route to addressing this lack is the use of Large Language Models to synthesize requirements from existing code bases. We investigate this question by evaluating an LLM-based and RAG-supported agentic approach that generates requirements from source code, verifies their implementation status relying on a human-in-the-loop, and synthetically introduces requirements smells and non-implemented requirements. Our goal was to create datasets that mimic reality and foster empirical RE research. However, during the study, various problems arose, leading to this experience report. Contrary to our initial hypotheses, LLMs were unable to (i) generate non-implemented requirements reliably, (ii) generate high quality requirements, and (iii) reliably introduce synthetic requirements smells. Furthermore, neither an LLM nor a single human-in-the-loop suffices to detect requirements smells reliably. These findings suggest that the generation of code-to-requirements datasets using LLMs is not yet viable and requires human supervision, especially for quality assurance. We critically reflect on our lessons learned and draw relevant conclusions for both researchers and practitioners.

</details>


### [14] [IntentTester: Intent-Driven Multi-agent Framework for Cross-Library Test Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25588)
*Yi Gao,Ziyuan Zhang,Xing Hu,Xiaohu Yang,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IntentTester是一个基于意图驱动的多智能体测试复用框架，通过将测试抽象为语言无关的测试描述语言（TDL），结合仓库图进行语义对齐，并利用LLM引导推理和迭代验证，实现跨库和跨语言的测试迁移。在9个开源项目上的评估显示，该框架生成2,776个语法正确的测试，正确率85%，执行成功率74%，远超基线方法（51%和43%），并发现了包括栈溢出、空指针解引用和解析不一致在内的新缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单元测试中蕴含的领域知识被锁定在独立项目中，难以在不同库之间复用。现有的基于结构代码映射（如API签名）的迁移技术在设计差异大或跨语言场景下往往失败，生成的迁移测试无法执行。

Method: 提出IntentTester多智能体框架，核心是意图驱动的测试复用：(1) 将测试抽象为语言无关的测试描述语言（Test Description Language, TDL）；(2) 在仓库图中与语义相关的实体和依赖关系进行对齐；(3) 通过LLM引导的推理和迭代验证合成可执行测试。

Result: 在9个开源项目（覆盖JSON、HTML、Time三个领域，Java和Python两种语言）上评估，IntentTester生成2,776个语法正确的测试，正确率85%（基线分别为51%和43%），其中2,410个执行成功，有效率为74%。同时发现了栈溢出、空指针解引用和解析不一致等新缺陷，部分已被维护者确认或修复。

Conclusion: 意图驱动的迁移将关注点从代码映射转向语义对齐，使得实际的跨库和跨语言测试复用成为可能，同时提升了测试质量并暴露了实现缺陷。

Abstract: Unit tests capture both functional checks and domain-specific knowledge, but this knowledge remains locked within individual projects and is rarely reused across libraries with overlapping functionality. Existing migration techniques based on structural code mappings (e.g., API signatures) often break down under divergent designs or cross-language settings, resulting in non-executable migrated tests. In this paper, we present IntentTester, a multi-agent framework for intent-driven test reuse. Instead of translating raw code, IntentTester abstracts tests into a language-agnostic Test Description Language (TDL), aligns them with semantically related entities and dependencies in a repository graph, and synthesizes executable tests through LLM-guided reasoning and iterative validation. This design enables cross-library and cross-language migration without manual intervention, producing migrated tests that existing structure-mapping approaches cannot achieve. We evaluate IntentTester on nine open-source projects across three domains (JSON, HTML, and Time) and two languages (Java and Python). IntentTester generates 2,776 syntactically correct tests with 85\% correctness; in comparison, the two baselines achieve 51\% and 43\%. Among them, 2,410 tests executed successfully, yielding a 74\% effectiveness rate. Beyond higher success rates, IntentTester also surfaced previously unknown defects including stack overflows, null dereferences, and parsing inconsistencies, several of which have been acknowledged or patched by maintainers. Our results show that intent-driven migration shifts the focus from code mappings to semantic alignment, allowing practical cross-library and cross-language test reuse while improving test quality and exposing implementation flaws.

</details>


### [15] [CodeChat-Eval: Evaluating Large Language Models in Multi-Turn Code Refinement Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25747)
*Guoxiang,Guo,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Neelofar Neelofar,Yuanyuan Qi,Aldeida Aleti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 现有基准测试忽略了多轮代码优化对话场景，无法评估LLM在优化过程中保持功能正确性的能力。本文提出CodeChat-Eval框架，通过动态指令选择算法构建多轮优化对话评估会话。实验发现，在多轮优化中，LLMs的功能正确性显著下降19.2%-69.2%，逻辑层面优化和新增修改请求导致最大下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试忽略多轮代码优化对话场景，无法评估LLM在多次优化后是否仍能保持代码的功能正确性（即仍能通过原始任务测试套件）。

Method: 提出CodeChat-Eval评估框架，使用动态指令选择算法从多轮代码优化对话中构建评估会话。

Result: 在开源和闭源LLM上观察到功能正确性显著下降，降幅从19.2%（GPT-5 Nano）到69.2%（Llama 3.1 8B）不等；逻辑层面优化和新增修改请求导致最大下降。

Conclusion: LLMs在多轮代码优化对话中难以维持功能正确性，需要超越单轮生成的、评估功能保持型优化的基准测试。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering to generate and refine code. In practice, developers often continue from an initial code generation request with follow-up refinement instructions, such as requests to improve style, restructure implementation, or change the execution strategy while preserving the intended behaviour. However, existing benchmarks generally omit this multi-turn code refinement dialogue setting and therefore cannot evaluate whether LLMs maintain functional correctness, i.e., whether the refined code still passes the test suite for the original task. To address this limitation, we introduce CodeChat-Eval, an evaluation framework that constructs evaluation sessions from multi-turn code refinement dialogues using a dynamic instruction selection algorithm. Our empirical study on open-weight and proprietary LLMs observes a statistically significant decrease ranging from 19.2% (GPT-5 Nano) to 69.2% (Llama 3.1 8B) in functional correctness over multi-turn refinement. The largest correctness drops are associated with logic-level refinements and additive change requests. These findings indicate that LLMs struggle to maintain functional correctness during multi-turn code refinement dialogues, and highlight the need for benchmarks that evaluate functionality-preserving refinement beyond single-turn generation.

</details>


### [16] [Automated Detection of Configuration-Specific Security Vulnerabilities via Patch Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25863)
*Felipe de Sant'Anna Paixão,Joanna C. S. Santos,Paulo Anselmo da Mota Silveira Neto,Daniel Sadoc Menasche,Gustavo Bittencourt Figueiredo,Eduardo Santana de Almeida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PatchLens，一种纯静态技术，通过将AST级补丁与源码存在条件对齐，并解析轻量级构建系统，自动恢复高度可配置C/C++系统中安全补丁对应的漏洞影响条件（VIC），在Linux内核、FFmpeg和PHP上评估表明VIC紧凑且仅少数漏洞影响全系统，而CVE文本几乎不包含配置选项信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度可配置C/C++系统中，安全补丁仅影响特定编译时变体组合，但现有漏洞描述（如CVE）几乎不包含配置选项信息，导致漏洞评估和修复缺乏变体感知能力，亟需自动提取漏洞影响的配置条件。

Method: 形式化定义VIC为布尔谓词，表示包含原始缺陷的所有变体；PatchLens通过对齐AST级补丁块与源码级存在条件，结合轻量级构建系统分析解析文件包含，纯静态地恢复VIC，无需编译任何系统变体。

Result: 在1,192个Linux内核、289个FFmpeg和100个PHP补丁上评估，VIC平均变量数分别为1.84、3.23和1.04；仅少数漏洞影响全系统（这些漏洞CVSS评分更高）；CVE文本对配置选项的召回率仅约1%。

Conclusion: PatchLens可扩展、可解释地支持CI中的变体感知分类、测试选择、定向采样与模糊测试及特征风险评分，并为自动化丰富CVE描述提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We study how security patches in highly configurable C/C++ systems map onto the space of compile-time variants. We formalize the Vulnerability Impact Condition (VIC) - a Boolean predicate over configuration options that denotes all variants that contained the original flaw - and introduce PatchLens, a purely static technique that recovers VICs by aligning AST-level patch hunks with source-level presence conditions and resolving file inclusion via lightweight build system analysis. Evaluating PatchLens on 1,192 Linux kernel, 289 FFmpeg, and 100 PHP patches, we compute precise, human-readable VICs without the need to compile any system variant. The resulting predicates are compact (avg. 1.84 variables for Linux, 3.23 for FFmpeg, 1.04 for PHP) and show that only a small fraction of vulnerabilities are system-wide, which carry higher CVSS scores; meanwhile, CVE texts almost never encode the required options ($\approx$ 1% average recall), motivating automated enrichment of CVE descriptions with VICs. PatchLens and the accompanying dataset enable immediate applications in CI (variant-aware triage and test selection), targeted sampling and fuzzing, and feature risk scoring, offering a scalable, explainable path to vulnerability assessment in highly configurable software.

</details>


### [17] [LLM-Based Discovery of Latent Requirements from Stakeholder Conversations: Preliminary Results from Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25867)
*Mithila Sivakumar,Martin Lochner,Shiva Nejati,Mehrdad Sabetzadeh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LENS方法，利用LLM从利益相关者访谈中提取显式需求并推断隐式需求，在工业网络安全场景的12份访谈记录中，显式需求提取F1达84.4%，隐式需求中75%被领域专家认为有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利益相关者访谈是需求获取的重要来源，但许多相关需求在对话中仍然是隐式的。现有方法主要关注识别显式陈述的需求，而忽略了隐式需求的挖掘机会。

Method: LENS利用LLM分析利益相关者访谈记录，通过结合利益相关者陈述与组织工具和基础设施的上下文信息进行推理，从而提取显式需求并推断额外的隐式需求。所有需求以用户故事形式呈现，并与转录摘录关联以确保可追溯性。

Result: 在工业网络安全场景的12份访谈记录中，LENS提取显式需求的平均F1分数为84.4%；平均75%的由LENS识别出的隐式需求被领域专家认为具有实用的自动化或节省时间的潜力。

Conclusion: LENS方法能够有效从访谈中提取显式需求，并能推断出有价值的隐式需求，弥补了现有方法在隐式需求发现方面的不足。

Abstract: Stakeholder interviews are an important source of information for requirements elicitation, yet many relevant requirements remain implicit in such conversations. Stakeholders frequently describe workflows, challenges, and operational practices without explicitly articulating the software capabilities that could address them. Recent work has considered the use of LLMs to analyze conversational data and extract requirements from stakeholder interviews. Existing approaches, however, primarily focus on identifying explicitly stated requirements, leaving implicit opportunities largely unexplored. In this paper, we present LENS (LLM-Enabled Needs Discovery from Stakeholder Interviews), an approach that analyzes stakeholder interview transcripts to both extract explicit requirements and infer additional latent requirements. LENS performs this inference by reasoning over stakeholder statements together with contextual information about organizational tools and infrastructure. Both extracted and inferred requirements are represented as user stories and linked to transcript excerpts to ensure traceability. We conduct a preliminary evaluation of LENS using twelve stakeholder interview transcripts collected in an industrial setting involving cybersecurity operations. We show that LENS achieves an average F1-score of 84.4% for extracting explicit requirements, while, on average, 75% of the latent requirements identified by LENS were perceived as providing useful automation or time-saving potential by domain experts.

</details>


### [18] [The Web4 Agent Economy: A Large-Scale Empirical Study of the Landscape, Challenges, and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25876)
*Yuhan Jin,Shuohan Wu,Chong Chen,Lingfeng Bao,Xiaohu Yang,Jiachi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对Web4（从Web3过渡而来，自治代理作为独立经济参与者）生态系统进行大规模实证研究。通过分析99,448条多链身份注册、3.17亿+交易日志、341个MCP项目源码和349个GitHub问题，发现自治代理已建立活跃的机器间支付经济（每日处理数百万交易），但基础设施仍不成熟，存在身份/授权实践、跨环境操作和支付互操作性等瓶颈，社区响应分布不均，支付互操作性是最持久的未解决瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管业界对Web4代理协议（如MCP、x402、EIP-8004）的兴趣日益增长，但真实的Web4代理生态系统在很大程度上仍未被探索。为填补这一空白，作者开展了首次大规模实证研究。

Method: 采用大规模实证研究方法，分析了99,448条多链身份注册、317,596,323条交易日志、341个MCP项目的源代码以及349个经过筛选的GitHub问题，从三个维度（部署使用现状、工程挑战、社区响应）进行系统分析。

Result: 自治代理已建立起高度活跃的机器对机器支付经济，每天处理数百万笔交易。但这种增长建立在脆弱的基础设施之上，存在身份/授权实践、跨环境操作和支付互操作性等关键问题。社区响应可见但不均匀，支付互操作性是最持久的未解决瓶颈。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了Web4代理经济的快速增长与其脆弱底层基础设施之间的关键差距，为构建更安全的Web4代理生态系统指明了未来方向。

Abstract: The Internet is transitioning from Web3 toward Web4, where autonomous agents serve as independent economic actors. These agents can now hold crypto wallets, execute on-chain trades, and pay for external API calls. This transition calls for a new infrastructure stack capable of supporting key agent operations, including agent-to-tool interaction, agent-to-agent payments, and verifiable agent identity, represented by emerging protocols such as the Model Context Protocol, x402, and EIP-8004. Despite growing industrial interest in these protocols, the real-world Web4 agent ecosystem remains largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we conduct the first large-scale empirical study of the Web4 ecosystem. Specifically, our study targets three interconnected questions: how Web4 agents are deployed and used in practice; what engineering challenges developers face when building Web4 agents; how current project communities respond to these challenges. To answer these questions, we analyze 99,448 multi-chain identity registrations, 317,596,323 transaction logs, the source code of 341 MCP projects, and 349 filtered GitHub issues. Our findings reveal that autonomous agents have established a highly active machine-to-machine payment economy, processing millions of daily transactions. However, this growth is built on immature infrastructure, including identity/authorization practice, cross-environment operation, and payment interoperability. Our follow-up analysis shows that community responses are visible but unevenly distributed across repositories, and payment interoperability remains the most persistent unresolved bottleneck. Overall, this study reveals a critical gap between the rapid growth of the Web4 agent economy and its fragile underlying infrastructure, highlighting future directions for building a more secure Web4 agent ecosystem.

</details>


### [19] [The Dependency Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25949)
*Ehsan Zabardast,Bhuwan Paudel,Javier Gonzalez-Huerta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对267个微服务系统的工业案例研究，发现微服务依赖网络呈现"黑洞"结构：依赖磁铁构成致密核心，外围服务逐渐卷入，形成类黑洞的三区域宏观结构。据此提出依赖黑洞理论，将网络映射为奇点、事件视界和吸积盘，并给出三个关于依赖集中如何出现和演化的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构虽承诺通过松散耦合实现独立演化，但大型系统中依赖关系高度集中于少数服务，现有理论对此缺乏解释框架。

Method: 对由267个微服务组成的产品进行探索性工业案例研究，三角验证编译时、运行时和任务三种依赖信号，并与从业者迭代验证解释。

Result: 发现依赖网络中反复出现的宏观结构类似黑洞：一个由依赖磁铁构成的致密核心、一个服务日益与核心纠缠的过渡区域，以及一个连接松散的外围区域。

Conclusion: 提出依赖黑洞理论，将网络映射到黑洞解剖结构（奇点、事件视界、吸积盘），并制定三个关于依赖集中如何出现和演化的假设，为推理依赖增长、识别风险服务以及指导治理干预提供解释性视角。

Abstract: Microservice architectures promise independent evolution through loose coupling, yet large systems often exhibit strong dependency concentration around a small set of services. In an exploratory industrial case study of a product composed of 267 microservices, we triangulated multiple dependency signals -- compile-time, run-time, and task dependencies -- and iteratively validated our interpretations with practitioners. We observed a recurring macro-structure in the dependency network that resembles a black hole: a dense core of dependency magnets, a transitional region of services increasingly entangled with the core, and an outer region of lightly connected services. Based on these observations, we propose the dependency black hole theory, mapping the network to the black hole anatomy of a singularity, an event horizon, and an accretion disk, and formulating three hypotheses about how dependency concentration emerges and evolves at scale. The theory provides an explanatory lens for reasoning about dependency growth, identifying services at risk of becoming dependency magnets, and motivating governance interventions. We outline practical implications and directions for longitudinal and multi-case validation.

</details>


### [20] [Helpful or Harmful? Evaluating LLM-Assisted Vulnerability Patching via a Human Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25973)
*Giulian Biolo,Michael Tezza,Yuanjun Gong,Fabio Massacci*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出通过平衡交叉设计实验，比较LLM辅助与人工调试在漏洞修复中的效果，并开发了集成隐藏幽灵测试的WebApp来验证补丁完整性。初步试点研究已开展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件漏洞修复需要专业知识，而开发者常缺乏此类技能；LLM辅助工具虽有潜力，但可能引入幻觉或不安全代码，导致表面修复通过功能测试但未通过安全验证，因此需要系统评估LLM辅助修复的实际效果。

Method: 采用平衡交叉设计（Balanced Crossover）的对照实验；开发集成隐藏幽灵测试（Ghost Tests）的WebApp以验证补丁完整性；实验包含训练和评估场景；评估指标包括修复速度、功能测试和安全测试的修复效果、以及参与者感知。

Result: 已完成小样本试点研究，为后续正式实验提供了参考。

Conclusion: 通过严谨的实验设计，该研究有望揭示LLM辅助漏洞修复相较于人工调试的真实能力与局限性，为安全实践提供实证依据。

Abstract: Software vulnerability remediation is a cognitively demanding task that requires specialized security expertise often lacking in general developers. In the meantime, Large Language Models (LLMs) assisted tools show potential in vulnerability detection, location, and repair tasks. [Hypothesis:] While LLM-assistance is hypothesized to accelerate patching, it also risks introducing hallucinations or insecure code, leading to a higher likelihood of generating superficial repairs that bypass the standard functionality checks but fail the security validation. [Objective:] We aim to present an empirical experiment, unveiling the capability of LLM-assisted vulnerability patching compared to manual debugging on human participants in real-world scenarios. [Method:] We plan to conduct a controlled experiment using a Balanced Crossover design. For that, we have developed a WebApp for code execution and integrated hidden Ghost Tests to verify patch integrity beyond visible functional requirements. The experiment involves training and evaluation scenarios. The remediation speed, remediation efficacy for both standard functionality tests and security tests, and participant perception will be evaluated. [Pilot Study:] A pilot experiment with a small sample of participants has been conducted, providing insights for the following study.

</details>


### [21] [EnerInfer: Energy-Aware On-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23001)
*Bohua Zou,Nian Liu,Binqi Sun,Matteo Mascherin,Debayan Roy,Yutao Liu,Yu Peng,Ning Jia,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出EnerInfer框架，通过降低NPU/内存频率的配置松弛来优化端侧LLM推理的能效和热管理，无需QoE损失，在手机、笔记本和开发板上分别提升高达65%、12%和24%的能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端侧LLM推理系统仅优化解码速度，忽略了降低频率以提升能效和减少发热的机会，且缺乏跨模型、引擎和平台的稳定最优配置。

Method: 采用去中心化、模型结构感知的预测和排名驱动的在线反馈机制，替代逐模型 profiling 和大量传感器；包括跨NPU/DDR频率的吞吐量和功耗预测、QoE满足的高效配置选择，以及轻量级有限时域热预测实现动态切换。

Result: 在真实LLM上，手机提升能效65%，笔记本12%，开发板24%，且无QoE违反。

Conclusion: 通过主动利用配置松弛并联合管理能效、吞吐量和热舒适度，EnerInfer有效解决了端侧LLM推理的能耗和热瓶颈。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is increasingly attractive for privacy-preserving, reliable, and cost-effective deployment, yet its energy and thermal costs remain a critical bottleneck. Existing systems primarily optimize for decoding speed, implicitly assuming that faster execution is always preferable. We show instead that on-device LLM inference often has exploitable configuration slack: modestly lowering NPU and memory frequencies preserves quality of experience (QoE) while substantially improving energy efficiency and reducing heat.
  Realizing this opportunity in production is challenging. The most energy-efficient NPU/DDR setting varies with the model, inference engine, platform, and runtime conditions, with no stable ranking across configurations. Commercial devices further lack component-level power sensing, and shell temperature evolves with request arrivals, response lengths, and thermal history. To address these challenges, we propose EnerInfer, the first on-device LLM inference framework that jointly manages energy efficiency, throughput, and thermal comfort for LLM workloads. EnerInfer replaces per-model profiling and sensor-heavy control with disaggregated, model-structure-aware prediction and ranking-driven online feedback. It predicts throughput and power for unseen LLMs across NPU/DDR frequency settings, selects QoE-satisfying efficient configurations under runtime interference, and uses lightweight limited-horizon thermal prediction to dynamically switch between energy-optimized and thermally constrained inference. Evaluations on real-world LLMs show that EnerInfer improves energy efficiency by up to 65%, 12%, and 24% on phones, a laptop, and a development board, respectively, without QoE violation.

</details>


### [22] [Toward Self-Evolution-Ready Workflow Harnesses: A Reversible Migration Path and Convertibility Taxonomy for Expert LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24598)
*Yimo Lin,Zhen Zhang,Yibin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Strangler Fig模式的可逆迁移路径，将静态的"LLM+脚本"工作流重构为可组合、类型化、可审计的阶段，并通过A/B/C三档可转换性分类法实现工作流就绪度诊断与路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的专家验证型"LLM+脚本"工作流虽然价值显著，但都是静态的——它们编码了宝贵的领域知识，却无法根据反馈动态调整执行。现有智能体研究主要关注绿地和合成基准测试，遗留工作流的迁移问题尚未解决。

Method: 提出了一种可逆的Strangler Fig迁移路径，将遗留工作流重构为可组合、类型化、可审计的阶段。核心是一个三档可转换性分类法（A/B/C），实现为系统编排中的路由阶段，用于诊断工作流的就绪度并进行路由。

Result: 提出了一个完整的迁移框架，包含可逆的Strangler Fig迁移路径和三档分类路由机制。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了遗留工作流与自适应智能体之间的鸿沟，为将静态的专家验证工作流逐步迁移为可动态适应执行的系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While expert-validated "LLM + script" workflows deliver significant value, they remain static: they encode hard-won domain knowledge yet fail to adapt execution based on feedback. Existing agent research predominantly targets greenfield agents and synthetic benchmarks, leaving the migration of active legacy workflows unresolved. To bridge this gap, we present a reversible, Strangler-Fig migration path that refactors legacy workflows into composable, typed, and auditable stages. Central to this framework is a three-tier convertibility taxonomy (A/B/C), implemented as a routing stage within the system harness, which diagnoses a workflow's readiness and routes it accordingly.

</details>


### [23] [MANGO: Automated Multi-Agent Test Oracle Generation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24815)
*Pablo Valle,Shaukat Ali,Aitor Arrieta,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MANGO是一个多智能体框架，能从自然语言描述中自动为VLA机器人生成细粒度测试预言，解决了传统符号预言成本高、可重用性差、仅能评估最终状态等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA机器人的测试依赖人工构建的符号预言，成本高、需领域知识、与特定任务环境紧耦合、可扩展性差，且仅提供最终状态评估，无法定位中间行为故障。

Method: MANGO框架包含三步：生成可复用的原子任务库→生成模拟器可执行的原子预言定义→通过分解复杂指令为有序原子动作序列生成细粒度预言。采用Generator、Assessor、Judge三个智能体协作迭代优化。

Result: 在LIBERO_10和RoboCasa Humanoid Tabletop基准上，MANGO生成的细粒度预言能检测与符号预言数量相当的故障，同时准确进行故障定位并提供更丰富的诊断信息。消融实验验证了组件贡献和初始任务集的影响。

Conclusion: MANGO证明了为VLA机器人自动化生成测试预言的可行性和有效性，在保持预言质量的同时提升了可扩展性和诊断能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are emerging robotic control systems that integrate perception, language understanding, and action generation in a unified architecture. Existing testing approaches for VLA-enabled robots rely on manually constructed symbolic test oracles that determine task success from final environment states. These oracles are costly to construct, require domain expertise, and are often tightly coupled to specific tasks and environments, limiting scalability and reuse. Furthermore, they provide only end-state assessments of task outcomes, offering limited insight into intermediate behavior and fault localization. To address these limitations, we introduce MANGO, a multi-agent framework that automatically generates fine-grained oracles from natural-language descriptions of robotic tasks. MANGO first generates a reusable library of atomic tasks, then generates simulator-grounded oracle definitions for each atomic task, and finally produces executable fine-grained oracles by decomposing complex instructions into ordered sequences of atomic actions and corresponding oracles. The framework uses collaborative Generator, Assessor, and Judge agents that iteratively refine generated artifacts through structured feedback. We evaluate MANGO on the LIBERO_10 and RoboCasa Humanoid Tabletop benchmarks. Results show that MANGO generates executable, fine-grained oracles that detect a similar number of failures as symbolic oracles while accurately localizing them and providing richer diagnostic information. Through ablation studies, we further analyzed component contributions and the effect of initial task set, while preserving oracle quality. Overall, the results show the feasibility and effectiveness of test oracle generation for VLA-enabled robots testing.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [24] [Type Checking Project Haystack Grids using JSON Schema and Pydantic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24891)
*Thomas Hirsch,Samina Kadkhoda Masoumali,Gerald Schweiger*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文针对Project Haystack本体在标签使用歧义、自动化验证有限及集成障碍等问题，提出了一套基于Python的工具链，包括Trio文件解析器和Pydantic模型与JSON Schema代码生成器，实现了静态类型检查和结构化验证，并开源发布以增强Haystack生态系统的可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Project Haystack作为广泛采用的建筑本体，存在标签语义模糊、缺乏自动化验证、以及依赖Haxall生态的自定义文件格式和领域特定语言造成的集成壁垒，亟需工具链来降低使用门槛并提升可靠性和互操作性。

Method: 提出了基于Python的工具链：(i) Haystack定义文件（Trio格式）解析器；(ii) 从解析规范生成Pydantic模型和JSON Schema定义的代码生成器，支持静态类型检查和结构验证。

Result: 生成的模型支持Python内的静态类型检查和Haystack网格的结构化验证，以及Python生态外的JSON表示的Schema验证；所有工具、模型和Schema均以开源许可发布。

Conclusion: 该工具链通过提供可编程的标准化验证手段，旨在强化Haystack生态系统，并开辟一条超越其当前技术边界的实际路径。

Abstract: Ontologies enable scalable energy services in buildings by supporting interoperability and automation. Project Haystack is a building ontology that is widely adopted due to its flexible, tag-based semantic model, openness, and extensibility, but suffers from ambiguous tag usage and limited automated validation. Although Project Haystack is formally open, its reliance on custom file formats and domain-specific languages that originate from the Haxall ecosystem creates a de facto barrier to integration. In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing a Python-based toolchain for Haystack. We present (i) a parser for Haystack definition files (Trio file format), and (ii) a code generator that derives Pydantic models and JSON Schema definitions from these parsed specifications. The resulting models enable static type checking and enable structural validation of Haystack grids within Python, as well as schema-based validation of JSON representations outside the Python ecosystem. All tools, generated models, and schemas are released publicly under an open-source license, with the goal of strengthening the Haystack ecosystem and opening a practical pathway beyond its current technical boundaries.

</details>


### [25] [Reading AI Model Compilation in MLIR Through the Lens of Formal Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25244)
*Javed Absar*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文论证编译器基础设施（如MLIR）的设计原则与形式理论（项重写系统、精化演算、抽象解释）之间存在对应关系，强调理解这些形式概念有助于厘清抽象设计的完备性、理想设计方向及实际折衷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着编码代理降低了实现成本，良好的设计和抽象成为主要关注点。当本质结构缺失时，限制来自抽象层面而非实现层面。因此需要知道如何良好设计底层抽象。

Method: 通过将MLIR中的具体设计原则（match-and-rewrite引擎、分层lowering、范围分析）分别映射到形式理论（项重写系统、精化演算、抽象解释），展示这些对应关系，并论证形式理论能为抽象设计提供精确的完备性讨论框架。

Result: 揭示了MLIR设计理念与形式理论之间的深层联系，指出形式理论能为抽象设计提供关于完备性、理想设计及实际折衷的精确讨论依据。

Conclusion: 熟悉这些形式概念有助于理解编译器抽象设计的完备性含义、理想设计方向以及实际折衷的出发点，对指导未来编译器基础设施设计具有重要价值。

Abstract: Compiler infrastructures such as MLIR rest on a set of design principles: IR abstractions, interfaces, match-and-rewrite, flow analysis, type conversion, staged lowering, and so on. These concepts have proven themselves in practice. Good designs typically arrive through engineering knowledge, intuition and experience. Many of them, however, have correspondences in formal theory. MLIR's match-and-rewrite engine has correspondence to a \emph{term-rewriting-system}~\cite{baadernipkow1998}; staged lowering has the structure of \emph{refinement calculus}~\cite{back1998}; and range analysis is grounded in \emph{abstract interpretation}~\cite{cousot1977,cousot1979}. Highlighting these correspondences is useful because each theory supplies vocabulary precise enough to discuss structural questions. Moreover, as coding agents lower the cost of implementation, good design and abstractions become the main concern~\cite{Lattner2026ClaudeCCompiler}. A coding agent can generate a pass, but it can only reason over the semantics the representation exposes. When essential structure is missing, the limitation is one of abstraction, not of implementation. The natural next question is how to design that substrate well. Well-chosen abstractions emerge from experience and intuition, but they often mirror concepts given a more precise treatment in formal theory. We argue that knowledge of these formal concepts clarifies what completeness means for a given abstraction, what the ideal design would be, and where practical trade-offs depart from it.

</details>

### [26] [Systematic Exploration of 4-Expert Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts via Automated Pipeline Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23739)
*Yashkumar R Lukhi,Harsh Rameshbhai Moradiya,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向LEMUR数据集的异构4专家混合专家（MoE4）架构自动大规模搜索管道。在28天RTX 4090上的搜索中，生成了4,463个候选模型，但发现了由itertools.combinations字母序枚举导致的严重覆盖偏差——整个探索空间（理论23,751种组合的4.8%）全部锚定在AirNet单一族上。在AirNet锚定范围内，ShuffleNet和MobileNetV3组合产生最高精度（平均0.632），FractalNet和MNASNet被识别为低效族。管道、分析产物和修正后的生成器已开源在NNGPT项目中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 替代手动设计异构MoE架构的低效方式，实现自动化的、系统性的大规模搜索，以发现高性能的4专家MoE组合。

Method: 构建确定性代码汇编生成器，从LEMUR数据库中系统组合基础架构族为MoE4集成，每个集成由卷积门控网络（含温度缩放）、mixup增强和余弦退火学习率调度控制。通过28天RTX 4090管道运行，生成4,463个候选模型（197批次，1,021个成功评估）。

Result: 发现了严重搜索空间覆盖偏差：因itertools.combinations的字母序枚举，整个搜索空间仅覆盖了理论23,751种可能组合的4.8%，且全部锚定在AirNet单一族。在锚定范围内，ShuffleNet+MobileNetV3组合达到最高平均精度0.632；FractalNet和MNASNet为低效族。

Conclusion: 识别了管道中的系统偏差根源并提出分层随机采样修复方案；发布了管道、分析产物和修正版生成器作为NNGPT开源项目的一部分。

Abstract: We present an automated large-scale search pipeline for heterogeneous 4-Expert Mixture-of-Experts (MoE4) architectures within the LEMUR neural network dataset ecosystem. Building on a hand-crafted heterogeneous MoE reference model, we replace manual design with a deterministic code-assembly generator that systematically combines base architecture families drawn from the LEMUR database into MoE4 ensembles, each governed by a convolutional gating network with temperature scaling, mixup augmentation, and cosine-annealed learning rate scheduling. Over a 28-day campaign on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the pipeline generated 4,463 candidate models across 197 batches, of which 1,021 were evaluated successfully. A critical finding emerged from the campaign: due to alphabetical enumeration via itertools.combinations, the entire explored search space (4.8% of the theoretical 23,751 possible 4-family combinations) is anchored to a single family, AirNet. We characterise this coverage bias precisely, identify the root cause in the generator, and propose a stratified random sampling fix. Within the AirNet anchored scope, ShuffleNet and MobileNetV3 consistently co-produce the highest-accuracy ensembles (mean accuracy up to 0.632), while FractalNet and MNASNet are identified as low-yield families warranting exclusion in future campaigns. The pipeline, analysis artefacts, and corrected generator are released as part of the open-source NNGPT project at https://github.com/ABrain-One/nn-gpt

</details>


### [27] [Weight-Space Geometry of Offline Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23740)
*Aleksandr Nikolich,Igor Kiselev,Vladimir Platonov,Karina Romanova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种离线强化学习损失（SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO、DPO）在相同数学推理数据上的机制差异，发现：SFT/RFT/RIFT的权重更新几乎共线（余弦相似度≥0.97）；DFT方向偏离最大；Offline GRPO在SFT方向上加正交分量；DPO处于近正交子空间且CKA骤降。DPO在GSM8K（93.5%）和AIME26（30.0%）上精度最高，但学习率比其他方法小10倍，差异部分源于超参数选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅用下游精度比较离线RL损失函数，未探究这些方法在权重更新机制层面是否真正不同，或是否收敛到相似的更新方向。

Method: 在Qwen3-4B基座模型上，使用注意力仅LoRA微调，基于相同数学推理轨迹，训练SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO和DPO六种方法，通过余弦相似度、主角子空间分析、线性模式连通性和CKA进行机制比较。

Result: (i) SFT/RFT/RIFT的权重delta几乎共线（余弦≥0.97，主角中位数~7°），GSM8K精度相当（87-88%）；(ii) DFT方向偏离最大；(iii) Offline GRPO添加了与SFT方向正交的分量（全局~67%，深层~86%）；(iv) DPO位于近正交子空间，存在模式连通性障碍，晚期层CKA降至~0.46。DPO精度最高（GSM8K 93.5%，AIME26 30.0%），但学习率小10倍。

Conclusion: 不同离线RL损失函数在权重更新上存在显著的机制差异，DPO的精度优势部分来自其独特的优化轨迹，但学习率差异使得这一结论需要进一步验证。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement-learning losses (RFT, RIFT, DFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) are widely used to distill reasoning from large teachers into smaller students, and are typically compared on downstream accuracy alone. We ask whether they are mechanistically distinct or converge to a similar weight update. Training six methods (SFT, RFT, DFT, RIFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) on identical math rollouts from a single base model (Qwen3-4B) with attention-only LoRA, we analyze the resulting deltas via cosine similarity, principal-angle subspace analysis, linear mode connectivity, and CKA. We observe: (i) SFT, RFT, and RIFT have nearly colinear weight deltas (cosine >= 0.97, top-1 principal angle ~7 deg median over 144 modules) and comparable GSM8K accuracy (87-88%, n=1319; pairwise McNemar p >= 0.15); (ii) DFT diverges further in direction than any reward-weighted method despite using the same data; (iii) Offline GRPO adds a substantial component orthogonal to the SFT direction (~67% globally, up to ~86% in late layers) while staying in the SFT loss basin; (iv) DPO sits in a near-orthogonal subspace, shows a mode-connectivity barrier, and collapses late-layer CKA to ~0.46. DPO also reaches the highest accuracy in our protocol on both GSM8K (93.5%, McNemar p < 10^-9 vs. each other method) and AIME26 (30.0% vs. 3.3-10.0%); its training uses a 10x smaller learning rate than the others (the standard convention), so the update-norm and accuracy gaps reflect loss-function and optimizer choices jointly, and a learning-rate-matched DPO comparison is left for future work.

</details>


### [28] [A Survey on Federated Causal Discovery and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23741)
*Xianjie Guo,Yuwei Wang,Guodu Xiang,Xiaoli Tang,Kui Yu,Han Yu,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦因果发现与因果推断领域，通过多维分类法组织现有方法，并建立了联邦因果推理的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推理对数据驱动决策至关重要，但可靠数据常分布在多个机构中，受隐私法规或通信限制无法集中。联邦学习虽能解决数据孤岛问题，但联邦因果发现与推断领域跨学科性强且缺乏系统性综述，阻碍研究者入门。

Method: 提出三维分类法组织联邦因果发现：方法学范式、联邦拓扑结构、结构范围；并考察时间动态、数据异质性、缺失数据、变量集不一致等实践维度。联邦因果推断按目标估计量（平均vs个体化/条件处理效应）和估计策略（经典加权到深度生成架构）分类。将两者形式化为统一联邦因果推理流水线的互补阶段。

Result: 构建了联邦因果发现的系统化分类体系，明确了联邦因果推断的方法分类，建立了联邦因果发现与推断的统一流水线框架，并识别了隐私、通信效率、理论保证和应用领域的共同关切与开放挑战。

Conclusion: 联邦因果发现与推断是互补的因果推理阶段，前者为后者提供有效效应估计所需的结构知识。该领域面临隐私、通信效率、理论保证等多方面共同挑战，未来研究需进一步探索开放问题。

Abstract: Causal reasoning, which encompasses the discovery of causal structures and the inference of causal effects, is fundamental to data-driven decision making. In practice, data for reliable causal analysis are often distributed across institutions and cannot be centralized due to privacy regulations or communication constraints. Federated learning (FL) addresses this by enabling collaborative analysis without raw data sharing, giving rise to the rapidly growing field of federated causal discovery (FCD) and inference (FCI). However, the interdisciplinary nature of this field and the absence of a comprehensive survey present barriers to entry for researchers. This paper bridges that gap by providing a systematic review through multi-dimensional taxonomies. Grounded in the three core design decisions underlying any FCD solution, namely how structures are learned, how data are partitioned, and what structural knowledge each party obtains, we organize FCD along three axes: methodological paradigm, federation topology, and structural scope. We further examine key practical dimensions, including temporal dynamics, data heterogeneity, missing data, and non-identical variable sets. For FCI, we categorize methods by target estimand (average versus individualized/conditional treatment effects) and by estimation strategy, from classical weighting methods to modern deep generative architectures. Unlike prior works that treat FCD and FCI separately, we formalize their connection as complementary stages of a unified federated causal reasoning pipeline, where FCD supplies the structural knowledge required for valid effect estimation in FCI. Finally, we highlight their shared concerns regarding privacy, communication efficiency, theoretical guarantees, and application domains, and conclude by identifying open challenges for future research.

</details>


### [29] [Low-power analogue neural networks with trainable nonlinear connections for continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23742)
*Ian T. Vidamour,Fernando Aguirre,Thomas J. Hayward,Matthew O. A. Ellis,Charles Swindells,Alexander McDonnell,Martin Trefzer,Finley Robins,Luca Manneschi,Susan Stepney,Tony Kenyon,Oliver J. Sutton,Jack C. Gartside,Ivan Y. Tyukin,Adnan Mehonic,Eleni Vasilaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将可训练非线性函数置于物理神经网络的连接上（受Kolmogorov-Arnold网络启发），利用模拟带通滤波器在FPAA上实现，在平滑连续值任务（如机器人运动学、连续控制、光伏MPPT）上以更少节点和连接达到优于MLP的性能，但在分类任务上无参数效率优势；训练好的网络可迁移至硬件（约35,000个连接），专用CMOS实现预计功耗约30微瓦，忆阻器仿真复现了相同行为，表明优势来源于连接上的可训练非线性而非特定器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理神经网络大多将非线性器件响应强制作为标量权重，未能充分利用连接的表达能力；受Kolmogorov-Arnold网络启发，作者希望在物理连接上放置可训练的非线性函数，使每个物理连接成为可学习的计算单元，从而提升网络效率。

Method: 在物理连接的连接上实现可训练的非线性函数，具体使用现场可编程模拟阵列（FPAA）上的模拟带通滤波器；将训练后的网络（约35,000个连接）迁移至硬件并量化保真度；还通过忆阻器仿真验证方法的普适性。

Result: 在平滑连续值任务（机器人运动学、连续控制、光伏最大功率点跟踪）上，该方法以远少于MLP的节点和连接达到更优性能；但在分类决策边界任务上无参数效率优势；专用CMOS实现预计功耗约30微瓦；忆阻器仿真复现相同行为，证明优势源于连接上的可训练非线性而非特定器件。

Conclusion: 将可训练非线性放置在物理神经网络的连接上能显著提升平滑连续值任务的参数效率，且优势与具体物理器件无关；该架构具备低功耗（约30微瓦）的硬件实现潜力，为物理神经网络的设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Physical neural networks promise low-power machine learning by computing directly with analogue device physics, but most architectures force nonlinear device responses to act as scalar weights. Inspired by Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, we place trainable nonlinear functions on the connections, making each physical connection a learnable computational element. Realising these functions as analogue band-pass filters on field-programmable analogue arrays, we find that the benefit is task-dependent and follows from the smoothness of the physical basis: the networks represent smooth, continuously valued targets, including robotic kinematics, continuous control, and photovoltaic maximum-power-point tracking, with far fewer nodes and connections than multilayer perceptrons, but offer no parameter-efficiency advantage on classification-like decision boundaries. Trained networks transfer to hardware across approximately 35,000 connections with quantified fidelity, and a dedicated CMOS implementation is projected to operate at approximately 30 microwatts. A memristive realisation reproduces the same behaviour in simulation, indicating that the advantage comes from placing trainable nonlinearity on connections, rather than from a particular device.

</details>


### [30] [Synergizing Physically Constrained MCMC and Chemical-Informed Gaussian Processes for Reaction Network Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23757)
*Runzhe Liu,Zihao Wang,Wenbo Yang,Shengyang Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PC-MCMC-CIGP，一个结合拓扑采样、守恒/热力学约束和化学信息高斯过程（CIGP）残差模型的灰盒工作流，用于从稀疏含噪化学时序数据中提取可解释的基元反应网络，并在H2+Br2基准和苯乙烯环氧化反应中验证了其优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏含噪化学时间序列数据中提取可解释的控制方程仍然困难，因为离散反应拓扑与连续动力学参数紧密耦合，现有方法难以区分真正的基元反应路径与欺骗性的唯象拟合。

Method: 提出PC-MCMC-CIGP灰盒工作流，整合了：（1）spike-and-slab拓扑采样；（2）硬守恒与热力学筛选；（3）化学信息高斯过程（CIGP）残差模型用于参数校准和实验设计。这不是单个MCMC或GP系列的方法创新，而是这些组件的物理约束集成与显式不确定性感知采集策略的结合。

Result: 在H2+Br2基准上，约束采样器成功区分了基元自由基路径与欺骗性唯象拟合；在苯乙烯环氧化反应中，CIGP优化循环相比GP-BO基线将最终产率提升了12.5%；10种子采集策略对比显示，PC-EI显著减少低产率BO建议，而EI类准则获得最强的最终产率性能。

Conclusion: PC-MCMC-CIGP为化学动力学中的机理发现与实验优化提供了可复现、物理约束强、不确定性感知的灰盒框架，不同采集策略在低产率避免与最终性能之间存在明确权衡。

Abstract: Extracting interpretable governing equations from sparse, noisy chemical time-series data remains difficult because discrete reaction topology and continuous kinetic parameters are tightly coupled. We present PC-MCMC-CIGP, a reproducible gray-box workflow that combines spike-and-slab topology sampling, hard conservation and thermodynamic screening, and a Chemical-Informed Gaussian Process (CIGP) residual model for parameter calibration and experimental design. The methodological contribution is not a new MCMC or GP family in isolation; rather, it is the integration of these components into a physically constrained workflow with explicit uncertainty-aware acquisition choices. On the H2 + Br2 benchmark, the constrained sampler distinguishes elementary radical pathways from deceptive phenomenological fits in our experiments. On styrene epoxidation, the CIGP optimization loop improves final yield by 12.5% over the reported GP-BO baseline. A new 10-seed acquisition study shows that EI, GWU, PC-EI, uncertainty sampling, discrepancy hunting, and random search have different trade-offs: PC-EI substantially reduces low-yield BO suggestions, while EI-style criteria give the strongest final-yield performance.

</details>


### [31] [Exploring Dualistic Meta-Learning to Enhance Domain Generalization in Open Set Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23758)
*Xiran Wang,Jian Zhang,Lei Qi,Yang Gao,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MEDIC的元学习策略，通过联合域-类匹配的隐式梯度匹配，解决开集域泛化中正负样本不平衡导致的过拒绝问题，在开集和闭集场景下均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开集域泛化方法使用一对多分类器时，正样本少负样本多的不平衡问题会导致决策边界偏向正样本，造成模型对分布外数据（包括来自未见域中已知类的数据）过度拒绝。

Method: 提出双元元学习策略MEDIC，通过联合域-类匹配，同时在域间和类间任务划分上进行隐式梯度匹配，学习对域和类都平衡的最优决策边界。

Result: MEDIC在开集场景下优于先前方法，同时保持了具有竞争力的闭集泛化能力。

Conclusion: MEDIC通过联合考虑域和类两个维度的任务划分，有效解决了开集域泛化中的类别不平衡问题，实现了在开集和闭集场景下的平衡泛化。

Abstract: Domain generalization learns from multiple source domains to generalize to unseen target domains. However, it often neglects the realistic case of label mismatch between source and target. Open set domain generalization is then proposed to recognize unseen classes in unseen domains. A simple approach trains one-vs-all classifiers to separate each class and detect outliers as unknown. Yet, the imbalance between few positive samples and many negative samples skews the decision boundary towards the positive ones, leading the model to over-reject out-of-distribution data, even from known classes in unseen domains. In this paper, we propose a novel meta-learning stategy called dualistic MEta-learning with joint DomaIn-Class matching (MEDIC), which considers implicit gradient matching towards inter-domain and inter-class task splits simultaneously to find optimal boundaries balanced for both domains and classes. Experimental results show that MEDIC not only outperforms prior methods in open set scenarios, but also maintains competitive close set generalization ability.

</details>


### [32] [One Ruler: A Same-Hands Re-Evaluation of Bivariate Causal Direction on Tuebingen, with a Parameter-Free Compression Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23767)
*Wietse Stienstra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Tübingen因果对数据集上各方法的报告准确率进行了复现评估，发现由于不同作者使用不同的子集、权重、模型选择和决策率，文献中的排名存在误导。作者采用统一严格协议（102对全部强制决策，无调参）重新评估所有方法，引入极简基线——排序条件压缩（bd2），准确率达74.7%。在此统一标准下，各方法聚类在70%出头，零参数压缩器与最强方法持平。文章揭示了报告指标虚高的机制（测试集模型选择、显著性门控弃权），并提供了预注册的证伪检验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中各方法在Tübingen因果对上的准确率是在各自不同的协议下测量得到的（不同的子集、权重、模型选择和决策率），这导致无法进行公平比较。作者认为这是错误的比较方式，需要进行统一协议下的"同手"复现评估。

Method: 作者在完全相同102对上运行所有方法，采用统一规则：无调参、强制决策每个因果对。引入极简基线"排序条件压缩"：将量化、排序、一阶差分后的数据输入bz2压缩器，零拟合参数。对SLOPE进行忠实复现（强制决策版），对RECI进行重跑。使用McNemar检验评估差异显著性，并计算压缩分数与混杂强度的关联p值。

Result: 基线方法达到74.7%加权准确率（p=3.7e-7）；在SLOPE使用的相同100对上，基线得分76.0%，与SLOPE强制决策版（77.2%）仅差1.2个点（McNemar p=0.39）。RECI重跑得到70.7%（在原始论文误差范围内），而非常被引用的77.5%（追踪到是抄错单元格）。SLOPE发表的82.4%是决定子集指标（仅在其显著性检验选择回答的对上得分81.7%）。统一标准下各方法聚类在低至中70%区间，零参数压缩器与最强方法持平。压缩分数大小是模型无关的混杂标志（p=2.8e-68）。

Conclusion: 文献中报告的高准确率存在虚高，原因包括测试集模型选择（在测试集上调参）和显著性门控弃权（只回答有把握的样本）。在统一严格协议下，所有方法表现接近，简单的零参数压缩基线即可达到与最复杂方法相当的性能。这提示因果推断基准评估需要标准化协议，并提醒社区关注报告指标的方法论偏差。

Abstract: Headline accuracies on the Tuebingen cause-effect pairs are routinely compared across papers even though each is measured under its authors' own protocol -- different pair subsets, weightings, model-selection, and decision rates. We argue this is the wrong comparison and run the right one: a same-hands re-evaluation in which every method is run by us on the identical 102 pairs, with one strict rule -- no tuning and a decision forced on every pair. As a clean reference point we introduce a deliberately minimal baseline: sorted-conditional compression, which feeds quantized, sorted, first-differenced data to an off-the-shelf compressor (bz2) and has zero fitted parameters. Under the common ruler the ranking differs sharply from the literature. Our baseline reaches 74.7% weighted accuracy (p = 3.7e-7); on the same 100 pairs that SLOPE is evaluated on it scores 76.0%, a 1.2-point gap below the authors' own forced-decision SLOPE (77.2%) that is well inside noise (McNemar p = 0.39). A faithful re-run of RECI lands at 70.7% -- inside the original authors' reported error bar, not the 77.5% often quoted (which we trace to a mis-copied cell). SLOPE's published 82.4% is a decided-subset figure: scoring the authors' own stored output only on the pairs its significance test chose to answer reproduces 81.7%. Under the common ruler the methods cluster in the low-to-mid 70s and the zero-parameter compressor ties the strongest of them. We document the mechanisms that inflate published figures (test-set model selection, significance-gated abstention) and contribute two further results: compression score magnitude is a model-free confounding flag (p = 2.8e-68), and a pre-registered falsification test fails in an instructive way that bounds the method's theoretical interpretation. Code, pre-registrations, and per-pair outputs are released.

</details>

*Fang Wu,Weihao Xuan,Jure Leskovec,Yejin Choi,Li Erran Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurfBind是一个直接基于分子表面表征进行表位预测的框架，通过Transformer架构集成几何与物理化学线索，在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表位预测方法依赖序列或主链结构，难以捕捉不连续的、由表面驱动的构象表位，亟需一种能够直接利用分子表面几何与物理化学信息的学习框架。

Abstract: Molecular surfaces encode the geometric and physicochemical patterns that determine antibody-antigen recognition, central to epitope prediction. However, existing methods rely on sequences or backbone structures and struggle to capture discontinuous, surface-driven epitopes. This study presents SurfBind, a surface-centric learning framework for epitope prediction that operates directly on molecular surface representations. SurfBind integrates geometric and physicochemical cues through a Transformer-based architecture with patch-level surface modeling, binder-aware cross-attention, and a hierarchical coarse-to-fine prediction paradigm. Experiments on challenging epitope identification benchmarks, including SAbDab and DB5.5, demonstrate that SurfBind achieves state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization across unseen antibodies and conformational states, highlighting the value of interaction-aware surface modeling for understanding the crucial mechanisms of protein-protein interactions.

</details>
