*Lukas Arzoumanidis,Lara Johannsen,Klara Middendorf,Annette Eicker,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多变量时间序列图神经网络（MTGNN）的方法，利用ERA5气象再分析数据将GRACE卫星观测的陆地水储量异常重建至1940年，在保持高精度的同时显著减少了所需预测变量数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRACE/GRACE-FO卫星提供的陆地水储量变化数据始于2002年，对于气候尺度的分析来说时间序列过短，亟需将其向后扩展以支持长期水文气候研究。

Method: 采用MTGNN架构，通过静态可解释的混合邻接矩阵（结合测地距离与气候时间序列的滞后相关性）编码空间依赖关系，学习每日ERA5气象强迫（降水、蒸散发、径流）与月度GRACE观测之间的映射关系。

Result: 网格级皮尔逊相关系数0.69，流域平均相关系数0.94，近乎零偏差；成功再现2015/16厄尔尼诺和2020/21拉尼娜事件的空间特征；在流域尺度上与现有方法竞争达到0.025以内的相关性，而所需预测变量仅为其他模型的1/2至1/10。

Conclusion: 基于图神经网络的该方法在减少预测变量的同时实现了具有竞争力的重建性能，所有模型在干旱区域均表现出特征性弱点。代码已开源。

Abstract: Terrestrial water storage (TWS) integrates snow, soil moisture, surface water, and groundwater and is a key indicator of how climate variability and human activity reshape the global water cycle. The GRACE and GRACE-FO satellite missions provide the only direct, globally consistent observations of TWS change, but their record only begins in 2002 which is too short for many climate-scale analyses. We present a deep learning application that reconstructs monthly GRACE-like TWS anomalies (TWSA) back to 1940 by learning the relationship between daily ERA5 meteorological forcing (precipitation, evapotranspiration, runoff) and monthly GRACE observations. In contrast to prior reconstruction approaches based on grid-cell-wise regression, CNNs, or LSTMs, we adapt a multi-variate time series graph neural network (MTGNN) architecture, which was originally developed for mobility and traffic forecasting on urban sensor networks to this satellite-geodesy task. Spatial dependencies are encoded in a static, interpretable hybrid adjacency matrix that combines geodesic proximity with lagged correlations of climatic time series, capturing both local hydrological coupling and large-scale teleconnections. The reconstruction achieves a grid-cell Pearson correlation of 0.69, a basin-mean correlation of 0.94, and a near-zero bias, and it reproduces the spatial fingerprints of the 2015/16 El Niño and 2020/21 La Niña events. A systematic comparison with established reconstruction approaches (GTWS-MLrec, RM-REC, GRAiCE) shows that the graph-based model is statistically competitive at basin scale, reaching a correlation within 0.025 of the best baseline while using only roughly half to a tenth of the predictors the other models require and revealing characteristic weaknesses in arid regions in all models. The complete implementation is publicly available at github.com/hcu-cml/MTGNN-TWS-Reconstruction-GRACE

</details>


### [35] [The Degeneracy Distillery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23838)
*T. Lucas Makinen,Deaglan J. Bartlett,Niall Jeffrey,Benjamin D. Wandelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"退化蒸馏器"的方法，通过Fisher信息矩阵的估计与平坦化，自动且符号化地检测和解析物理模型中的退化参数组合，从而减少下游神经后验估计所需的模拟预算（最多减少10倍），同时获得物理洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多个参数或标签产生相似数据时，存在退化（degeneracy）问题，这使标签预测和逆问题求解变得困难，因为机器学习算法和概率采样器都依赖数据的可区分性及其对参数的梯度。然而，识别退化可以帮助理解模型选择或数据生成过程。

Method: 提出退化蒸馏器方法，通过从参数-数据（或参数-模拟）对中估计Fisher信息矩阵，并对其进行平坦化处理，自动且符号化地检测和解析退化参数组合。该方法探索似然的信息几何，将退化表征为物理模型的内在属性，无需实际数据观测。最终发现的符号坐标变换能识别对数据产生独立影响的参数组合。

Result: 在合成和真实世界问题上的实验表明，该方法发现的坐标变换在全局期望上平坦化了Fisher信息（与仅在单点平坦化的后验方法相比），并显著减少了下游神经后验估计所需的模拟预算。在测试案例中，在匹配验证校准的条件下，后验估计所需模拟次数最多减少了10倍，同时获得了物理系统的洞察。

Conclusion: 该方法能自动检测并解析物理模型中的退化参数组合，通过符号坐标变换平坦化Fisher信息，大幅提升下游推断效率并提供物理可解释性。

Abstract: When two or more parameters or labels produce similar data, they are degenerate, or hard to distinguish. Degeneracies render both label prediction and inverse problems difficult, since both machine learning algorithms and probabilistic samplers rely on the distinguishability of data and its gradients with respect to parameters. However, identifying degeneracies in physical models or real-world datasets can be elucidating about the choice of model or the underlying process that produces the data. We present the degeneracy distillery, a method that (1) detects and (2) resolves degenerate parameter combinations (a) automatically and (b) symbolically, from parameter-data (or parameter-simulation) pairs alone, through estimation and flattening of the Fisher information matrix. By exploring the information geometry of the likelihood, we characterize degeneracies as an intrinsic property of the physical model, requiring no realised data observation. We demonstrate our approach on a range of synthetic and real-world problems, discovering symbolic coordinate transformations that identify the combinations of parameters of a model which yield independent effects on the data. The resulting coordinates flatten the Fisher information in expectation globally, in contrast to posterior-based methods that flatten only at a single point, and substantially reduce the simulation budget required for downstream neural posterior estimation. In test cases we require up to $10\times$ fewer simulations for posterior estimation at matched validation calibration whilst simultaneously gaining physical insight on the system.

</details>


### [36] [Machine Learning Modeling for Real-Time Melt Pool Monitoring in Laser Powder Bed Fusion Additive Manufacturing: A Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23851)
*Inioluwa Emmanuel,Zhuo Yang,Ho Yeung,Xinyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合预训练CNN特征与随机森林的混合方法，用于激光粉末床熔融增材制造中熔池图像的实时异常检测。在1200张镍基超合金625图像数据集上，混合方法（EfficientNetB0+随机森林）以亚毫秒级推理时间（1.15 ms）实现了F1分数0.9451、准确率0.9458、AUC 0.9904，优于纯深度学习方法，为数据有限的制造环境提供了高效稳健的实时监测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在激光粉末床熔融（LPBF）增材制造中，实现熔池图像的实时异常检测面临数据有限、硬件资源受限以及对推理延迟要求严苛等挑战。纯深度学习方法虽精度较高但推理时间长，难以满足工厂级部署需求；传统机器学习方法则特征表达能力不足。因此需要探索一种兼顾精度与效率的混合方法。

Method: 构建了包含1200张镍基超合金625熔池图像的平衡数据集，采用80/20训练-测试划分及90/10验证划分。对图像进行标准化缩放、归一化和标签保持的数据增强。对比了三种迁移学习架构（ResNet50、EfficientNetB0、MobileNetV2）和两种随机森林方法：一种基于EfficientNetB0特征嵌入（混合方法），一种基于原始像素特征（基线方法）。评估指标包括准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、AUC、训练时间、推理延迟及CPU/GPU使用率。

Result: 混合EfficientNetB0-plus-随机森林方法在测试集上取得最佳表现：F1分数0.9451，准确率0.9458，AUC 0.9904，每张图像推理时间仅1.15 ms（亚毫秒级）。相比之下，纯深度学习方法推理时间显著更长且准确率更低。

Conclusion: 将预训练CNN特征与经典集成学习方法相结合，为数据有限的LPBF增材制造环境提供了一种计算高效、鲁棒的实时熔池异常检测方案，具备工业部署的可行性。

Abstract: This work investigates the implementation of artificial intelligence and machine learning (AI/ML) for real-time monitoring in laser powder bed fusion (LPBF) additive manufacturing. We developed a binary image classification framework for distinguishing normal and abnormal melt pool images using a balanced dataset of 1,200 images collected from Nickel superalloy 625 on the NIST AMMT platform. The study evaluates accuracy and inference time based on control requirements and hardware limitations of open-architecture LPBF machines. We benchmark three transfer learning architectures (ResNet50, EfficientNetB0, and MobileNetV2) against two Random Forest approaches: one trained on EfficientNetB0 feature embeddings (hybrid) and one trained on raw pixel features (baseline). Images are stratified into 80/20 train-test splits, with a further 90/10 validation split on the training set, and undergo standardized resizing, normalization, and label-preserving data augmentation to emulate realistic process variability. Each model is evaluated using accuracy, precision, recall, F1 score, and area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), along with training time, inference latency, and CPU & GPU usage to capture deployability constraints relevant to factory-floor monitoring. The hybrid EfficientNetB0-plus-Random Forest approach achieves the best performance on the held-out test set, with an F1 score of 0.9451, accuracy of 0.9458, and AUC of 0.9904, while maintaining sub-millisecond per-image inference (1.15 ms). In contrast, purely deep learning models exhibit significantly higher inference times with lower accuracy. These results demonstrate that combining pre-trained convolutional features with classical ensemble methods provides a robust, computationally efficient route to real-time melt pool anomaly detection in data-limited additive manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [37] [Sesame: Structure-Aware Molecular Generation via Spatial Density-Map Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23856)
*Konstantin Yatsenko,Arvind Thiagarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sesame，一种基于扩散模型的分子生成模型，通过空间对偶变换模块（Spatial Pairformer）同时支持从头生成和基于片段的先导化合物优化，并在配体-蛋白质复合物数据集上训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子生成模型难以同时兼顾从头生成、蛋白质-配体相互作用理解以及从化学家提供的片段/支架出发进行先导优化（lead optimization）的能力，后者虽为药物设计核心操作却常被视为次要问题。

Method: 1) 提出空间对偶变换模块（Spatial Pairformer），将部分分子结构和周围蛋白质口袋都表示为连续空间密度图进行条件生成；2) 设计联合去噪扩散框架，同时对原子类型、键类型和位置进行去噪；3) 引入轨迹微调方案（trajectory finetuning），在模型自身的采样轨迹上训练以提升生成质量。

Result: 在大型配体-蛋白质复合物和纯配体数据集上训练，支持从头生成和片段条件先导优化两种模式。

Conclusion: Sesame通过统一的条件机制实现了多模态分子生成，为药物设计中的先导优化阶段提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Generative molecular models for drug design are a promising direction with much active research. In the next phase of computational drug design, such models will need to understand small molecule structure and protein-ligand interactions, and they will need to possess the machinery to generate molecules de novo. Incorporating each feature poses a critical challenge. Equally important, yet often treated as secondary, is the ability to grow a molecule from a partial starting point -- a scaffold or fragment supplied by a chemist -- which is the central operation of lead optimization. We present Sesame (Spatial Evoformer for a Structure-Aware Molecular Engine), a diffusion-based molecular generation model that leverages a novel spatial pairformer module to condition on partial molecular structure and the surrounding protein pocket, both expressed as continuous spatial density maps. This single conditioning mechanism supports both de novo generation and fragment-conditioned lead optimization, letting a medicinal chemist prune a hit to a scaffold and have Sesame grow it in productive ways. In addition to this module, we also introduce a diffusion framework for joint denoising of atom types, bond types, and positions, along with a trajectory finetuning scheme that trains on the model's own sampling rollouts to improve generation quality. Sesame is trained on a large corpus of ligand-only and protein-ligand datasets.

</details>


### [38] [Are Safety Guarantees in Neural Networks Safe? How to Compute Trustworthy Robustness Certifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23858)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对神经网络鲁棒性认证中最优体积计算不可行的问题，本文提出apothem度量，通过线性次调用验证器即可计算apothem最优认证，并引入对偶认证提供鲁棒性上界。实验表明，ParallelepipedoNN系统在最小边长指标上实现了至少两倍改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全面临对抗样本威胁，现有鲁棒性认证方法大多追求最大化认证体积，但近期研究表明在合理时间内计算体积最优认证是不可行的，亟需一种计算高效的替代度量。

Method: 提出apothem度量，设计基于oracle（NN验证器）的算法，可在输入域直径的线性调用次数内计算apothem最优认证。同时提出对偶认证，包含某一类别的所有实例，提供apothem最小的鲁棒性上界。实现ParallelepipedoNN系统。

Result: 理论证明：不存在基于oracle的体积最优算法（即使不计oracle成本）；apothem最优认证可在O(n)次oracle调用内实现。实验上，在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上，ParallelepipedoNN相比现有工作在最小边长指标上实现至少两倍提升。

Conclusion: apothem度量是体积最优认证的可行替代方案，在保证计算效率的同时显著提升了鲁棒性认证的最小边长性能，对偶认证为鲁棒性分析提供了新视角。

Abstract: A primary challenge in AI safety is the existence of adversarial examples -- slightly distorted inputs that cause a neural network (NN) to misclassify. To mitigate this problem, recent research focuses on the computation of robustness certifications, which, for a given input, determine the largest distortion the input may receive without breaking the network's prediction. Robustness certifications can be interpreted as an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle (multi-dimensional intervals). Most existing approaches focus on maximizing the certification's volume, but recent intractability results prohibit the computation of volume-optimal certifications in reasonable time. We introduce the apothem measure and show how to compute apothem-optimal certifications in a linear number of calls to a NN verifier (oracle) w.r.t. the input domain's diameter. Moreover, we prove that we cannot have a volume-optimal, oracle-based algorithm, even if we discard the oracle costs. Also, we introduce dual certifications -- an interval including all instances of a class -- thus providing apothem-minimum upper bounds to a robustness certification. Further, we present the ParallelepipedoNN system, which we evaluate on the standard MNIST and Fashion MNIST benchmarks. A preliminary comparison with existing work on the same datasets reveals at least two-fold improvement w.r.t. the minimum edge length.

</details>


### [39] [Exact Schur-Sylvester Dimensionality Reductions for Non-Smooth Stochastic Complexity and Manifold Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23867)
*Trenton Lau,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对非光滑估计器（如Lasso）的NML编码长度精确计算中的计算瓶颈，提出了一种基于块Schur补和Sylvester行列式恒等式的数学等价形式，将投影算子和体积因子的计算复杂度从O(N³)降至O(k³ + N²k)，实现了超过14,100倍的加速，且保持双精度数值等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PPMH采样器中，投影算子需要求逆(N+k)×(N+k)广义KKT矩阵，体积因子需要计算(N-k)×(N-k) Gram矩阵的行列式，两者均存在O(N³)的立方复杂度瓶颈，限制了正则非光滑估计器NML编码长度在高维数据上的精确计算。

Method: 利用块Schur补和Sylvester行列式恒等式，对PPMH采样器中每一步的投影算子和体积因子计算进行数学等价变换，将复杂度从O(N³)降至O(k³ + N²k)，并将该加速策略推广到稀疏SVM、Elastic Net和Group Lasso。

Result: 在高维数据集上的实证基准测试中，实现了超过14,100倍的恒定加速比，且保持双精度数值等价性，有效样本量（ESS）评估验证了采样器效率。

Conclusion: 该工作使得精确非光滑NML估计在大规模统计推断中变得高度可行，解决了长期存在的计算可扩展性难题。

Abstract: The exact computation of the Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) codelength for regular non-smooth estimators (e.g., Lasso) has been historically limited by the cubic scaling walls of manifold-constrained projection and volume integration. At each step of the geometric Propose-and-Project Metropolis--Hastings (PPMH) sampler, evaluating the projection operator requires inverting an $(N+k) \times (N+k)$ generalized KKT matrix, while calculating the volume factor requires the determinant of an $(N-k) \times (N-k)$ Gram matrix. This paper presents an exact, mathematically equivalent formulation that bypasses both bottlenecks by utilizing the block Schur complement and Sylvester's determinant identity. We prove that the computational complexity of both operations collapses from $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ to $\mathcal{O}(k^3 + N^2 k)$ per step. We generalize this reduction to Sparse Support Vector Machines (SVMs), Elastic Net, and Group Lasso. Finally, we provide a rigorous numerical stability analysis and evaluate the sampler's efficiency using the Effective Sample Size (ESS) per second. Our empirical benchmarks on high-dimensional datasets confirm a constant speedup exceeding $14{,}100\times$ while maintaining double-precision numerical equivalence, rendering exact non-smooth NML estimation highly tractable for large-scale statistical inference.

</details>


### [40] [EveLoad: Cognitive Workload Recognition from Event-Based Eye Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25177)
*Guorui Lu,Shaohua Guan,Zhen Xu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EveLoad，首个基于事件相机的眼动认知负荷数据集（20名受试者，6级负荷），采用N-back引导注视范式控制眼动位置，并提出时空事件编码学习方法，在受试者特定评估中达到96.36%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于帧的眼动追踪器在认知负荷监测中时间分辨率有限，且易受快速眼动干扰；同时以往研究多采用自由观看范式，模型可能学习到注视位置分布与负荷的关联而非真正的眼动特征。需要一种能捕捉细微眼动动态且不受注视位置混淆的传感方法。

Method: 1) 构建EveLoad数据集：使用事件相机在空间约束的N-back引导注视范式下采集20名健康受试者的眼动数据，标注6级认知负荷。2) 提出时空事件表示编码的深度学习框架进行认知负荷识别。

Result: 在受试者特定评估下平均准确率96.36%，混合随机分割评估下96.13%，实现6级认知负荷的高精度识别。

Conclusion: 基于事件相机的眼动信号为未来负荷感知康复系统提供了一种有效的传感途径，具有微秒级时间分辨率、高动态范围和低延迟的优势。

Abstract: Cognitive workload monitoring is important for adaptive rehabilitation and assistive interfaces, where task difficulty, pacing, and feedback should be adjusted according to the user's cognitive state to avoid overload and under-challenge. Emerging extended reality and robot-assisted rehabilitation environments provide controllable training tasks, but they require unobtrusive sensing methods that can capture rapid ocular dynamics during interaction. Existing eye-movement-based cognitive workload recognition methods mainly rely on frame-based eye trackers, which often suffer from limited temporal resolution and degraded robustness under rapid eye movements. In contrast, event cameras provide microsecond-level temporal resolution, high dynamic range and low latency, making them suitable for capturing fine-grained ocular dynamics. Many previous studies rely on free-viewing or similar paradigms, where gaze locations can vary across tasks. As a result, models may learn associations between gaze-location distributions and cognitive workload, rather than workload-related eye movement characteristics themselves. In this work, we introduce EveLoad, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first event-based eye-movement dataset with graded cognitive workload annotations, collected from 20 healthy participants under spatially constrained and task-driven conditions using a controlled N-back-guided fixation paradigm. Based on this dataset, we establish a benchmark for cognitive workload recognition with six workload levels and propose a learning framework that encodes spatiotemporal event representations. Experimental results show that our approach achieves an average subject-specific accuracy of 96.36% and 96.13% under mixed random split evaluation. These results suggest that event-based eye movements may provide a useful sensing pathway for future workload-aware rehabilitation.

</details>

Method: 在具有自然异构分布式客户的跨机构乳腺癌队列上，系统比较了Cox比例风险模型、DeepSurv和随机生存森林三种代表性生存模型，在集中式、本地和联邦训练三种范式下评估，并对梯度型模型测试了FedAvg、FedProx和FedAdam三种联邦优化策略。

Result: 联邦学习持续优于本地训练，接近甚至有时超越集中式性能；随机生存森林在异构客户间提供了最佳的区分度、校准度和鲁棒性平衡；性能依赖于客户分布的多样性；FedAvg和FedProx比FedAdam更强且更稳定。

Abstract: Survival analysis is central to clinical decision-making, yet reliable time-to-event models require large, diverse cohorts that are rarely available at a single institution, while privacy regulations restrict the centralization of patient data. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative by training shared models without exchanging raw data, but its effectiveness for survival modeling under realistic, heterogeneous conditions remains insufficiently understood. This paper presents a systematic, multi-model evaluation of federated survival analysis on a cross-institutional breast cancer cohort with naturally heterogeneous distributed clients. Three representative survival models, the Cox Proportional Hazards model, DeepSurv, and Random Survival Forest (RSF), are compared across centralized, local, and federated training, and three federated optimization strategies (FedAvg, FedProx, and FedAdam) are assessed for the gradient-based models. Results show that FL consistently outperforms local training and approaches, and occasionally exceeds, centralized performance, while RSF offers the best overall balance of discrimination, calibration, and robustness across heterogeneous clients. We further find that performance depends on the diversity of client distributions, and that FedAvg and FedProx are stronger and more stable than FedAdam. Based on these findings, we derive practical, decision-oriented guidelines mapping data, privacy, interpretability, and resource constraints to recommended model and training-paradigm choices for federated survival modeling in healthcare.

</details>

*Bihe Zhao,Michel Meintz,Juangui Xu,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出成员与生成推断（MGI）问题：区分样本是训练集成员还是模型自身生成。现有成员推断和归因方法均因依赖似然信号而失效。作者提出数据断路器（DCB）方法，结合自编码器和潜在生成器的互补信号，在图像自回归和扩散模型上有效区分两者，即使存在训练样本的近似重复或模型衍生场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型产生的样本与人类创建内容难以区分，现有成员推断方法将生成样本误分类为训练成员，而归因方法则将真实成员误分类为生成样本，两类方法均因依赖被训练样本和生成样本共同升高的似然信号而失效。需要一种新的方法来解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出数据断路器（DCB），一种三阶段方法：结合生成模型的自编码器和潜在生成器的互补信号，以区分训练成员与生成样本。

Result: DCB在多种生成模型（包括图像自回归模型和扩散模型）上一致地解决了成员推断和归因方法的缺陷；即使模型生成训练样本的近似重复时仍然有效；并且能泛化到在生成数据上训练的新模型的挑战性衍生场景。

Conclusion: DCB有效解决了成员与生成推断问题，克服了现有方法对似然信号的依赖缺陷，在多种生成模型及衍生场景下均表现出稳健性。

Abstract: As generative models increasingly produce samples that are indistinguishable from human-created content, it becomes difficult to determine whether a given data point was part of a model's natural training set or was generated by the model itself, especially when models memorize and reproduce training data. We formalize this challenge as Member vs Generated Inference (MGI): given a sample and a target generative model, infer whether the sample is a true training member or a generated output of that model. Focusing on image generation, we show that existing membership inference methods systematically misclassify generated samples as training members, while attribution-based methods often misclassify true members as generated. This failure arises because both approaches rely on likelihood-related signals that are similarly elevated for training examples and for the model's own outputs. To address MGI, we propose Data Circuit Breaker (DCB), a three-stage method that combines complementary signals from a generative model's autoencoder and latent generator to distinguish training members from generated samples. Across multiple generative models, including image autoregressive and diffusion models, DCB consistently addresses the shortcomings of membership inference and attribution methods, remains effective even when models reproduce near-duplicates of training samples, and generalizes to challenging model derivative settings in which new models are trained on generated data.

</details>


### [43] [GRACE: Gated Refinement for Accurate Causal Edge Discovery in High-Dimensional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23880)
*Mohammad Fesanghary,Abhinav Havaldar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRACE 是一种结合约束性与评分性方法的因果发现框架，通过 Hard Concrete 门控和 L0 正则化实现稳健的二值边选择，在合成和真实数据集上显著提升 F1 值并降低计算成本（比非线性 CI 测试快 75 倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代时间序列数据集包含大量交互变量，因果发现面临可扩展性挑战：基于约束的方法中的非线性条件独立检验在大规模数据上不可行，而基于评分的方法需要任意阈值来二值化连续边分数。

Method: 提出 GRACE，使用 Hard Concrete 门控与 L0 正则化优化约束性因果发现：每个候选边有独立门控，其值集中于 0 或 1 附近，产生清晰的双峰分离；先通过快速线性 CI 骨架获得高召回候选，再用单一门控模型通过学习哪些边真正改善预测来修剪假阳性，并自动适应问题维度和骨架密度进行正则化。

Result: 在合成基准上（涵盖无标度、ER、小世界拓扑，最高 d=100），GRACE 显著提升 F1 并保持高精度，优于注意力基和评分基方法；以 75 倍加速匹配或超越昂贵的非线性 CI 测试。在真实河流流量数据集上，时间自举变体恢复 Elbe 河 11 条因果边中的 9 条，仅 1 个假阳性（F1=0.86，AUROC=0.99），将骨架的 106 个假阳性减少 99%。

Conclusion: GRACE 通过门控机制有效弥合了约束性和评分性方法的差距，在保持统计严谨性的同时实现了可扩展的高效因果发现，尤其适用于存在混杂因子、传播延迟和分布漂移的真实场景。

Abstract: From climate teleconnections to gene regulation, modern time-series datasets encompass tens or hundreds of interacting variables, making causal discovery increasingly challenging. Constraint-based methods offer statistical rigor but their nonlinear CI tests are infeasible at scale, while score-based alternatives avoid CI testing but require arbitrary thresholds to binarize continuous edge scores. We propose GRACE ($\textbf{G}$ated $\textbf{R}$efinement for $\textbf{A}$ccurate $\textbf{C}$ausal $\textbf{E}$dge discovery), which refines constraint-based discovery using Hard Concrete gates with $L_0$ regularization: each candidate edge has an independent gate whose values concentrate near 0 or 1, yielding a clean bimodal separation that makes the binary decision robust, unlike the narrow, overlapping score distributions produced by $L_1$ and attention-based methods. A fast linear CI skeleton provides high-recall candidates; a single gated model then prunes false positives by learning which edges genuinely improve prediction, with automatic regularization adapted to problem dimensions and skeleton density. Systematic experiments on synthetic benchmarks, spanning diverse graph topologies (scale-free, Erdős-R'enyi, small-world) and dimensionalities up to $d=100$, show that GRACE substantially improves F1 over its base CI method while maintaining high precision, and outperforms attention-based and score-based alternatives. GRACE matches or exceeds expensive nonlinear CI tests at a fraction of the cost ($75\times$ faster). On a real-world river flow dataset, where rainfall confounders, variable propagation lags, and distributional shifts violate standard assumptions, a temporal bootstrap variant of GRACE recovers 9 of 11 causal edges along the Elbe River with only 1 false positive ($F_1 = 0.86$, AUROC${} = 0.99$), reducing the skeleton's 106 false positives by 99%.

</details>


### [44] [ARIA: Adaptive Region-Based Importance Allocation for Conditional Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23898)
*Loay Mualem,Vinh Tong,Samir Darouich,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ARIA框架，通过自适应分配训练资源到条件空间中误差较大的区域，提高条件扩散模型知识蒸馏的效果，尤其对未见和欠表示条件有明显提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件扩散模型的知识蒸馏存在瓶颈：教师-学生之间的噪声预测高度依赖于条件信号，导致知识难以泛化到训练分布之外的条件空间。当条件空间（如文本提示）极大时，生成所有条件对应的合成图像计算上不可行，现有方法虽通过切换条件扩展暴露范围，但未解决训练资源如何最优分配的问题。

Method: 提出ARIA框架，将条件空间划分为粗粒度区域，在线估计各区域上教师-学生的差异（discrepancy），根据差异大小自适应分配训练更新量——差异大的区域分配更多训练资源，同时保持原始蒸馏目标不变。

Result: 在多数架构和设置下，ARIA优于RC（Random Condition）基线方法，在未见和欠表示的条件区域上提升最为显著。同时提供了理论分析，证明在有限方差和漂移假设下，所提出的追踪机制能够跟随训练过程中演化的差异。

Conclusion: ARIA通过在条件空间粗粒度区域上自适应分配训练努力，有效提升了条件扩散模型蒸馏的泛化能力，尤其解决了条件空间稀疏问题带来的瓶颈。

Abstract: Distilling conditional diffusion models aims to transfer the behavior of a large teacher to a smaller student while preserving alignment across conditioning inputs. Unlike recognition tasks, knowledge distillation in conditional diffusion often struggles to transfer knowledge beyond the training distribution, since the predicted noise strongly depends on the conditioning signal. As a result, effective distillation requires exploring a large conditioning space. In practical settings, this creates a major bottleneck. Paired image-condition data may be limited, and generating synthetic images for every available condition is often computationally infeasible, while the pool of conditions, such as text prompts, can be extremely large. Recent work addresses this issue by switching conditions during training, exposing the student to a broader conditioning space without changing the distillation objective. Yet this raises a complementary question: once a large conditioning corpus is available, how should the training effort be allocated? In this work, we introduce ARIA, a framework that adaptively allocates training effort across coarse regions of the conditioning space. By maintaining online estimates of teacher-student discrepancy at the region level, ARIA focuses updates where misalignment persists while preserving the original distillation objective. Empirically, ARIA improves over RC across most architectures and settings, with the clearest gains observed in unseen and underrepresented regimes. We also provide a theoretical analysis showing that the proposed tracking mechanism follows the evolving discrepancy during training under bounded variance and drift assumptions.

</details>

*Armin Heydari,Torben Leowald*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将“LLM提案，验证器处置”范式从数学AI扩展到法律AI的架构，通过自动形式化、验证内核和证明轨迹解释生成，为法律计算提供可证明的正确性，并为开放纹理法律分析提供结构保障，同时在法律AI训练中通过确定性外部验证器闭环强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律AI缺乏形式化可验证的训练奖励信号，传统强化学习在法领域存在闭环缺口，需要一种能够提供可验证正确性保证的架构。

Result: 在德国法程序期限计算、美国宪法商业条款分析和跨司法管辖区制裁比例性三个场景上验证了架构的有效性。同时证明该架构在结构上有利于法律AI训练——确定性外部验证器提供可验证结果，从而闭环传统强化学习在法领域的缺口。

Conclusion: 该架构为法律计算组件提供了可证明的正确性，为开放纹理法律分析提供了结构保证（论证阶段完整性、阶段正确性、演绎链接有效性），并通过确定性外部验证器实现了法律AI训练中可验证强化学习信号。

Abstract: This article develops an architecture that creates a formally verifiable reward signal to train legal AI, adapting the LLM proposes, verifier disposes paradigm from mathematical AI to the distinctive demands of law. We present an architecture comprising LLM-driven autoformalization into a formal legal calculus extending Catala, a verification kernel, and explanation generation grounded in formal proof traces. For the computational components of law, the architecture provides provable correctness. For open-textured legal analysis, it provides structural guarantees: every required stage of the legal argument is addressed, argumentation is exercised at the correct stages and not omitted, and the deductive links between steps are valid. We demonstrate the architecture on procedural deadline calculations in German law, Commerce Clause analysis in U.S. constitutional law, and cross-jurisdictional sanction proportionality. We further show that the same architecture has a structural advantage for legal AI training: a deterministic external verifier supplies verifiable outcomes for legal problems and thereby closes the traditional reinforcement-learning loop gap in law.

</details>


### [46] [Catastrophic Compositional Generation: Why Vanilla Diffusion Models Fail to Extrapolate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23920)
*Duncan Soiffer,Chandler Squires,Yuan Guan,Jason Hartford,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了在特定设置下，标准条件扩散模型无法通过任何推理时技术高效地从组合定义的目标分布中采样，因为分数估计误差在目标分布分布外时会产生灾难性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合生成任务需要利用仅在部分条件上训练的生成模型，从组合定义的（如几何组合）目标分布中采样。作者质疑标准条件扩散模型能否高效完成此任务。

Method: 通过理论指导的泛化论证和精心设计的合成/真实数据实验，分析推理时技术（如Feynman-Kac校正）和分数估计误差对组合生成性能的影响。

Result: 发现分数估计误差在目标分布相对于源分布处于分布外时，对性能产生灾难性影响；即使Feynman-Kac校正等推理时技术能减少近似误差，也无法弥补此问题。

Conclusion: 标准条件扩散模型在特定组合生成设置下不可行，需要采用不同的方法来解决该任务。

Abstract: The task of compositional generation involves using a conditional generative model, trained only on a subset of the possible conditions, to produce samples from compositionally-defined target distributions such as a geometric combination of the source distributions. In this work, we argue that this task is often infeasible for vanilla conditional diffusion models: we conjecture that no inference-time technique can efficiently produce samples from the target distribution in certain well-motivated settings. This idea is supported by theory-guided generalization arguments and carefully-designed experiments on both synthetic and realistic data. In particular, while recent methods such as Feynman-Kac correction reduce inference-time approximation error, our results show that score estimation error has a more catastrophic effect on performance when the target distribution is out-of-distribution with respect to the sources, highlighting the need for a different approach to this task.

</details>


### [47] [KLip-PPO: A per-sample KL perspective on PPO-Clip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23932)
*Riccardo Colletti,Robin Holzinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了PPO中的裁剪替代目标与Kullback-Leibler惩罚形式在梯度上等价，揭示了裁剪替代目标本质上是一种逐样本自适应的KL惩罚，其系数随重要性比率和优势函数变化，并在MuJoCo基准上验证了两者训练曲线不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO算法在文献中存在裁剪替代目标和KL惩罚两种形式，被视为独立算法并拥有各自的梯度、超参数和实现，大量实证工作对二者进行比较，但缺乏对两者关系的理论理解。

Method: 从数学上推导裁剪替代目标的梯度与一个逐样本变系数的KL替代目标梯度完全等价，该系数以封闭形式依赖于重要性比率和优势函数；并在五个MuJoCo连续控制基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 裁剪替代目标的梯度可由一个逐样本变系数的KL替代目标精确复现；在五个MuJoCo基准上，两种损失函数产生无法区分的训练曲线。

Conclusion: PPO-Clipped的逐样本隐式惩罚是信任区域边界处的阶跃函数，该系数的形状是泛化算法的自然设计轴，为后续算法改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) is the standard policy-gradient algorithm for on-policy reinforcement learning. The literature presents it in two forms, a clipped surrogate that bounds the importance ratio between successive policies and a Kullback-Leibler penalty between them. These forms are treated as separate algorithms with their own gradients, their own hyperparameters, and their own reference implementations, and a sizeable body of empirical work compares them. We show that the gradient of the clipped surrogate is reproduced exactly by a Kullback-Leibler surrogate whose coefficient varies per sample, with closed-form dependence on the importance ratio and the advantage. The identity holds at every minibatch step and across the entire inner loop, and on five MuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks the two losses produce indistinguishable training curves. The reformulation exposes a structural feature of the clipped surrogate that the min notation hides. PPO-Clip's implicit per-sample penalty is a step function at the boundary of the trust region, and the shape of this coefficient is the natural design axis for generalising the algorithm. We sketch the resulting follow-up directions in the discussion.

</details>


### [48] [DREG: A Layer-Wise Jacobian Regularization as a General-Purpose Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23942)
*Rowan Martnishn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过960组实验系统评估了导数正则化（DREG）的效果，发现DREG在总体和干净准确率上表现最优，在噪声鲁棒性上仅次于谱归一化；在GELU激活函数和现代Transformer架构下表现最佳；在数据稀缺时优势最明显，支持其作为即插即用的几何归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模实证研究，隔离导数正则化（DREG）的贡献，回答其何时、何地及为何有效。

Method: 跨越4种激活函数、6种正则化方法、8个数据集和5个随机种子的960组全交叉因子实验，使用固定超参数λ=10^{-2.5}，无需逐数据集调参。

Result: 1) DREG在总体和干净准确率上最高（显著优于无正则化、权重衰减和IGPen，Wilcoxon p≤0.031），噪声鲁棒性排名第二（仅次于谱归一化）；2) 在GELU激活函数下全局最佳，尤其在混乱视觉和NLP基准上表现突出；3) 数据稀缺时优势最明显。

Conclusion: DREG通过将正则化压力集中在激活导数最大的层上，而非统一约束网络，实现了即插即用的几何归纳偏置效果，特别适用于具有非平凡Jacobian结构的神经网络。

Abstract: We present a large-scale empirical study isolating the contributions of the Derivative Regularization penalty (DREG). Across a fully-crossed factorial sweep of 960 experiments spanning 4 activations, 6 regularizers, 8 datasets, and 5 random seeds, we ask: when, where, and why does DREG work? Our results establish three principal findings. First, DREG achieves the highest overall and clean-regime accuracy among all regularizers evaluated (significantly so against the unregularized baseline, Weight Decay, and IGPen; Wilcoxon $p \leq 0.031$). It ranks second in noise robustness behind Spectral Normalization (SN) - the only two layer-wise regularizers in the study. Second, DREG is globally the best-performing regularizer under GELU, the default activation in modern transformer architectures, particularly on both messy vision and messy NLP benchmarks, suggesting direct applicability to frontier deep learning settings. Third, DREG's advantage over competing regularizers is most pronounced under data scarcity, consistent with its role as a geometric inductive bias that substitutes for the regularizing effect of data volume. Throughout, DREG is applied with a single fixed hyperparameter $λ= 10^{-2.5}$ and no per-dataset tuning, supporting its characterization as a plug-and-play regularizer for neural networks with nontrivial Jacobian structure. These findings are consistent with DREG's design: concentrating regularization pressure on layers where the activation derivative is largest, rather than constraining the network uniformly.

</details>


### [49] [Learning the Koopman Operator using Attention Free Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23957)
*Mohammed Nagdi,Evangelos-Marios Nikolados,Alexey Yermakov,Mars Gao,Nathan Kutz,Filippo Menolascina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补组件——注意力自由记忆块（AFT）和动态重编码策略，以提升Koopman自编码器在长时域预测中的鲁棒性，抑制潜变量流形漂移和误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman自编码器虽能实现潜空间线性预测，但长时域 rollout 常偏离已学习的流形，导致在切换系统、连续谱或强瞬态场景下产生相位和幅度误差。

Method: 1）AFT块：线性时间复杂度（相比多头注意力），利用历史潜变量的滑动窗口生成修正潜变量，参数量仅约3d²+T²（约30k）；2）动态重编码：通过EWMA、CUSUM、序贯双样本检验等轻量在线变点检测，识别潜变量漂移并投影回自编码器流形。

Result: 在Duffing振子、Repressilator、IRMA三个基准系统上，Koopman+AFT（可选重编码）相比Koopman自编码器、多头注意力、GRU和Transformer自编码器，在长达1000步的预测范围内持续降低误差累积，同时保持较低的推理延迟。

Conclusion: AFT和动态重编码的组合构建了一个快速、紧凑且能在长时域内稳定在流形上的预测器。

Abstract: Learning Koopman operators with autoencoders enables linear prediction in a latent space, but long-horizon rollouts often drift off the learned manifold, leading to phase and amplitude errors on systems with switching, continuous spectra, or strong transients. We introduce two complementary components that make Koopman predictors more robust. First, we add an attention-free latent memory (AFT) block that aggregates a short window of past latents to produce a corrected latent before each Koopman update. Unlike multi-head attention, AFT operates in linear time and adds only $\approx$30k parameters ($3d^2 + T^2$, fewer than matched multi-head attention), yet captures the local temporal context needed to suppress error divergence. Second, we propose dynamic re-encoding: lightweight, online change-point triggers (EWMA, CUSUM, and sequential two-sample tests) that detect latent drift and project predictions back onto the autoencoder manifold. Across three benchmark systems -- Duffing oscillator, Repressilator, IRMA -- our model consistently reduces error accumulation compared to a Koopman autoencoder and matched-capacity multi-head attention. We also compare against GRU and Transformer autoencoders, evaluated both from initial conditions and with a 50-step context, and find that Koopman+AFT (with optional re-encoding) attains markedly lower long-horizon error while maintaining lower inference latency. We report improvements over horizons up to 1000 steps, together with ablations over trigger policies. The result is a fast, compact predictor that stays on the learned manifold over long horizons.

</details>


### [50] [Certification of Machine Learning Models via Directional Sharpness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25004)
*Gefei Tan,Adria Gascon,Sarah Meiklejohn,Mariana Raykova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出方向锐度（directional sharpness）作为衡量模型泛化能力的新指标，相比现有指标与泛化相关性更强、识别泛化能力差的模型更可靠，且可在模型审计和零知识证明中高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型认证对于确保模型的可信度和质量至关重要，但直接认证泛化能力不可行（依赖未知数据且无法直接测量）；测试准确率等代理指标在训练过程受扰动时可能具有误导性，而sharpness等现有度量计算昂贵且在训练偏离预定流程时不可靠。

Method: 提出方向锐度（directional sharpness）这一新度量，用于在存在训练偏差的情况下高效且可靠地指示泛化能力。

Result: (1) 方向锐度与泛化的相关性比现有指标更强；(2) 方向锐度比现有指标更可靠地识别泛化能力差的模型；(3) 方向锐度可在模型审计场景（验证者可访问训练数据）中高效计算，并可通过零知识证明在不泄露训练数据的情况下认证质量。

Conclusion: 方向锐度是一种比现有指标更有效、更可靠的泛化能力指示器，且具有实用上的计算效率和数据隐私优势。

Abstract: In machine learning, model certification has been identified as an important method for gaining assurance about a model's trustworthiness and quality. A model's quality is largely determined by its ability to generalize, i.e., to perform well on data beyond what it was trained on. It is not possible to certify generalization directly, however, as it depends on unknown data and is not directly measurable. Proxies such as test accuracy can be misleading when the training process is perturbed (intentionally or accidentally), and metrics such as sharpness -- which has an empirically supported link to generalization -- are computationally expensive and can also serve as unreliable signals when training deviates from a prescribed procedure. In this work, we propose directional sharpness, a metric designed to efficiently and reliably indicate generalization despite potential training deviations. We provide empirical and analytical evidence that directional sharpness (1) correlates more strongly with generalization than existing metrics and (2) identifies models with poor generalization more reliably than existing metrics. Furthermore, directional sharpness is efficiently computable in model auditing settings, where the verifier has access to training data, and via zero-knowledge proofs that certify quality without revealing training data.

</details>


### [51] [Forget Without Compromise: Nexus Sampling for Streaming KV-Cache Eviction Under Fixed Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23961)
*Duc Duong,Hoang Anh Duy Le,Jianwen Xie,Anshumali Shrivastava,Zhaozhuo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Nexus Sampling 是一种免训练的 KV 缓存驱逐方法，通过 Nexus 评分（迭代注意力游走以发现桥接 token）结合加权蓄水池采样替代确定性 top-K 选择。理论上该方法在长期保留微妙重要 token 上优于 top-K，实验表明在 80% 缓存驱逐率下，LongBench 上接近密集注意力（<1% 差距），在检索密集型任务上超越 top-K 基线，且每序列缓存内存降低 10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存驱逐方法都采用每步直接注意力分数后跟确定性 top-K 选择的模板，这会导致低于阈值的 token 被永久擦除，而直接注意力无法从噪声中区分出的微妙重要 token 也会被永久丢失。

Method: 提出 Nexus Sampling，包含两个核心组件：(1) Nexus 评分——对直接注意力进行迭代游走以发现桥接 token；(2) 加权蓄水池采样——以包含概率保留 token，替代确定性 top-K 选择。

Result: 在 80% KV 缓存驱逐率下，Nexus Sampling 在 LongBench 上与密集注意力差距在 1% 以内；在检索密集型任务上超越 top-K 基线；每序列缓存内存降低高达 10 倍。

Conclusion: Nexus Sampling 在理论上证明了其对确定性 top-K 在长期保留微妙重要 token 方面的优势，实验验证了在高压缓存场景下的有效性，为长上下文和 agentic LLM 的连续推理提供了更鲁棒的缓存管理方案。

Abstract: Long-context and agentic LLM workloads push the KV cache past any fixed memory budget, forcing the inference stack to permanently evict tokens at every step of a continuous-inference stream. Existing methods all share the same template, a per-step direct-attention score followed by deterministic top-$K$ selection, which converts a single below-cutoff step into an irreversible verdict and permanently erases any subtly important token that direct attention cannot single out from noise. To address this challenge, we propose Nexus Sampling, a training-free eviction method that pairs Nexus scoring, an iterative walk over direct attention that surfaces bridge tokens, with weighted reservoir sampling, which retains tokens with inclusion probability in place of deterministic top-$K$. Theoretically, we show that Nexus Sampling dominates deterministic top-$K$ in long-run survival of subtly important tokens. Empirically, at 80% KV cache eviction, Nexus Sampling matches dense attention within 1% on LongBench while outperforming top-$K$ baselines on retrieval-heavy tasks, with up to 10x smaller per-sequence cache memory.

</details>


### [52] [Speculative Decoding at Temperature Zero: A Scoped Safety-Invariance Screen with a 48,072-Sample Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25097)
*Sahil Kadadekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出典型接受不变性筛查（TAIS），通过行为等价性检验框架，在零温度设置下验证投机解码中草稿模型不会导致安全评分输出出现可检测的偏差。在16683样本确认核心集及44066扩展样本上，测试的vLLM堆栈未发现安全性发散，最大Cohen's h仅为0.024，远低于常规微不足道效应阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码通过让草稿模型为验证模型生成token来加速推理，但引发了一个具体的安全问题：在零温度下，草稿侧行为是否会泄漏到安全评分输出中？

Method: 提出TAIS（典型接受不变性筛查）框架，要求配对的目标模型单独输出与投机解码输出在相同安全测试集上满足：字节级别证据、±3pp的TOST等价检验、每任务Cohen's h低于校准零截止值|h|<0.1。

Result: 零温度vLLM堆栈未发现可检测的安全发散；最大绝对Cohen's h为0.024（远低于常规微不足道效应下限）；27个TOST对比中25个在±3pp阈值通过；DPO对抗草稿与规范草稿在4006样本上字节一致；bf16改变36%-53%输出字节但未改变安全率。额外70B规模探测产生AdvBench拒绝率0.839（95%CI[0.809,0.864]）。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下（零温度、vLLM栈、特定框架），草稿模型行为不会导致安全评分输出的可检测偏差。但声明不适用于采样温度、未测试框架、未测试模型家族或树状投机变体（如EAGLE和Medusa）。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates inference by letting a draft model propose tokens for a target model to verify, raising a concrete safety question: at temperature zero, can draft-side behavior leak into safety-scored outputs? We answer with Typical-Acceptance Invariance Screen (TAIS), a behavioral-equivalence screen that pairs target-only and speculative outputs on the same safety battery and requires byte-identity evidence, TOST equivalence at +/-3pp, and per-task Cohen's h below a calibrated null cutoff of |h| < 0.1. Applied to a 16,783-sample confirmatory core plus 44,066 matched expansion samples (fp16/bf16 execution, canonical and DPO-adversarial drafts, GPTQ-4bit drafts, two seeds, and four safety benchmarks), the tested temperature-zero vLLM stacks show no detectable safety divergence under TAIS. The largest absolute Cohen's h on matched target-only versus speculative refusal is 0.024, roughly an order of magnitude below the conventional trivial-effect floor; 25 of 27 per-task TOST contrasts pass at the +/-3pp margin (the two non-pass contrasts are capability-domain Wald-CI edge cases at identical ceiling rates, not genuine non-equivalence); the DPO-adversarial draft produces byte-identical output to the canonical draft across 4,006 samples; and bf16 changes 36%-53% of output bytes without moving any per-task safety rate outside equivalence. A separate 4,006-sample 70B production-scale probe, which lacks a matched 70B target-only arm and is therefore not counted as a TAIS pass, produces AdvBench refusal 0.839 over 700 AdvBench completions with 95% Wilson CI [0.809, 0.864]. We make no claim about sampling temperatures, untested frameworks, untested model families, or tree-speculation variants such as EAGLE and Medusa.

</details>


### [53] [3D Masked Autoencoders are Robust Learners of Volumetric and Multimodal Cellular Representations for Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23964)
*Amirhossein Kardoost,Lion Gleiter,Tingying Peng,Carsten Marr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了荧光显微镜数据上的2D和3D掩码自编码器，证明3D MAE在单细胞下游任务中一致优于2D变体，结合跨模态对齐（ESM2蛋白质语言模型）和通道交叉注意力/频域正则化后在蛋白质交互和定位任务上达到最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 荧光显微镜数据本质上是三维的，但现有自监督学习方法通常使用2D投影，丢失了关键的空间信息。本文旨在系统验证原生3D建模相较于2D投影/切片方法在体积显微镜数据上的优势。

Method: 对比2D和3D掩码自编码器（MAE-2D vs MAE-3D），在匹配架构和训练协议下进行评估；引入视觉表示与预训练蛋白质语言模型ESM2的跨模态对齐；采用通道交叉注意力机制和频域正则化以充分利用3D空间上下文。

Result: MAE-3D在单细胞下游任务上一致优于2D最大投影和切片变体；蛋白-蛋白交互任务ROC-AUC达0.865（超前人+0.025）；蛋白定位任务AUC_micro达0.952，F1_micro达0.742（分别提升+0.003和+0.010）。

Conclusion: 原生3D建模和跨模态对齐对单细胞显微镜表示学习具有显著优势，通道交叉注意力和频域正则化是有效利用3D空间上下文的关键技术。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning in fluorescence microscopy often relies on 2D projections, despite the inherently three-dimensional nature of cells. We present a systematic comparison of 2D and 3D masked autoencoders (MAE-2D vs. MAE-3D) on volumetric microscopy data. Under matched architectures and training protocols, MAE-3D consistently outperforms 2D max-projection and slice-based variants on downstream single-cell tasks. We further align visual representations with a pretrained protein language model (ESM2) and show that cross-modal supervision yields larger gains for volumetric models. Channel cross-attention and frequency-domain regularization are critical for leveraging 3D spatial context. On a protein--protein interaction task, MAE-3D achieves a ROC--AUC of 0.865, outperforming prior methods by up to +0.025. For protein localization, our best 3D model attains state-of-the-art AUC$_{\text{micro}}$ (0.952) and F1$_{\text{micro}}$ (0.742), improving over previous approaches by +0.003 and +0.010 absolute, respectively. Overall, these results demonstrate the advantages of native 3D modeling and multimodal alignment for representation learning in single-cell microscopy.

</details>


### [54] [Leaking Circuit Secrets: Gradient Leakage Attacks on Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25589)
*Rupesh Raj Karn,Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了梯度泄漏攻击对电路设计和硬件安全任务中GNN模型的威胁，发现GAT等注意力机制加剧泄漏，GIN提供较强鲁棒性，现有防御技术仅在特定设置下有效且可能牺牲模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GNN在电路设计与分析中成为关键工具，其安全与隐私风险亟需关注，但梯度泄漏攻击这一实际威胁在该领域尚未被充分评估。

Method: 在标准网表基准（ISCAS'85, EPFL, TrustHub）上，对GraphSAGE、GCN、GIN、GAT等SOTA GNN模型进行梯度泄漏攻击评估，并测试差分隐私、梯度裁剪、安全聚合、量化压缩和对抗训练等防御技术。

Result: 梯度泄漏攻击可暴露门类型和硬件木马特征等敏感信息；GAT加剧泄漏，GIN提供较强鲁棒性；现有防御仅在特定设置下有效且可能降低模型性能。

Conclusion: 工作为隐私保护的GNN提供了关键见解，强调了开发更鲁棒高效防御机制的必要性。

Abstract: As graph neural networks (GNNs) become standard tools for critical tasks in circuit design and analysis, their security and privacy risks require careful attention. Here, we present the first comprehensive evaluation of gradient leakage attacks (GLAs) on GNNs in circuit-design and hardware-security tasks, a practical threat that has been largely overlooked. We assess state-of-the-art (SOTA) GNNs, including GraphSAGE, GCN, GIN, and GAT, trained on standard netlist benchmarks (ISCAS'85, EPFL, and TrustHub), for their fundamental vulnerability to GLAs. We find that GLAs can expose sensitive information, such as gate types and distinctive properties of hardware Trojans, which may assist adversaries in analyzing logic locking schemes or evading Trojan detection mechanisms. Our analysis shows that these risks are influenced by architectural features, with attention mechanisms (GAT) exacerbating leakage, while injective aggregation (GIN) provides comparatively stronger resilience. We further evaluate several SOTA defense techniques, including differential privacy, gradient clipping, secure aggregation, model compression with quantization, and adversarial training. We find that these techniques improve resilience only in specific settings and can also compromise model performance. Overall, our work provides key insights toward privacy-preserving GNNs and highlights the need for more robust and efficient defenses. We release our full methodology and artifacts.

</details>


### [55] [A Comparative Study of Bayesian Contextual Bandits for Real-Time Warehouse Sorter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23977)
*Tina Dongxu Li,Mouhacine Benosman,Ken Meszaros,Trevor Dardik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模仓库中自动物料处理系统（MHS）的分拣机分流控制问题，对比研究了三种混合机器学习框架（LR+GDO、XGB+BO、BCB），发现贝叶斯上下文赌博机（BCB）框架在奖励提升（2.03%）、推理延迟、在线学习能力等方面优于其他方法，适合实时控制优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分拣机分流系统依赖静态权重的成本函数，无法适应高度动态的系统上下文（如体积模式、拥堵水平、设备状态、上下游依赖），亟需一种能够实时优化分流决策的方法。

Method: 对比研究三种混合机器学习框架：线性回归+梯度下降优化（LR+GDO）、XGBoost+贝叶斯优化（XGB+BO）、贝叶斯上下文赌博机（BCB）。利用高保真物理感知仿真器解决冷启动问题，实现从离线到在线学习的安全过渡。

Result: 树模型（XGB+BO）预测精度略优，但BCB框架整体表现更佳：相比启发式基线实现2.03%的奖励提升，具备Bang-Bang控制理论支持的决断性时间最优策略、持续在线学习能力、探索与利用的战略平衡以及更短的推理延迟。

Conclusion: BCB框架在大规模仓库环境实时控制优化中展现出显著潜力，值得进一步研究以推动实际部署应用。

Abstract: Efficient sorter diversion control of automated material handling systems (MHS) is critical for optimizing operational efficiency in large-scale warehouse environments. In this study, we use an inbound receiving sorter at a high-volume e-commerce warehouse as our primary use case, where the sorter diversion system relies on cost functions with static weight configurations that fail to adapt to highly dynamic system contexts, such as volume mode, congestion level, equipment physical status, and upstream/downstream dependencies. To address this real-time sorter diversion optimization challenge, we conducted a comparative study of three candidate hybrid machine learning frameworks: Linear Regression with Gradient Descent Optimization (LR+GDO), XGBoost with Bayesian Optimization (XGB+BO), and Bayesian Contextual Bandits (BCB). Model training and evaluation were enabled by leveraging a high-fidelity physics-aware emulator to overcome the cold-start problem and allow a safe transition from offline to online learning. We performed comprehensive evaluations including reward model predictive accuracy, contextual sensitivity, action distribution, and projected reward uplift. Our results demonstrate that while tree-based reward models offer slightly better predictive power, the BCB framework achieved overall higher performance with 2.03% reward uplift over the heuristic baseline. Furthermore, BCB exhibits several superior characteristics, such as its decisive time-optimal policy backed by Bang-Bang control theory, continuous online learning capability, strategic balance between exploration and exploitation, and significantly shorter inference latency. These results demonstrate the potential of the BCB framework for real-time control optimization in large-scale warehouse environments, motivating further investigation toward operational deployment.

</details>


### [56] [TL++: Accuracy and Privacy Preserving Traversal Learning for Distributed Intelligent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25627)
*Erdenebileg Batbaatar,Young Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TL++ 是一种双模式遍历学习框架，通过构建虚拟批次实现集中式小批量梯度行为，支持基础模式（交换切割层激活/梯度）和安全模式（秘密共享切割层张量），在 CIFAR-10 和 BioGPT/PubMedQA 上显著优于联邦学习和分割学习基线，通信量降低 13.1 倍，精度提升超 12 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分布式智能系统在数据孤岛下训练的隐私与效率矛盾：联邦学习需反复交换完整模型且异构分区下性能差，分割学习虽减少通信但无法恢复集中式小批量梯度行为且可能暴露明文激活/梯度。

Method: 提出 TL++ 双模式遍历学习框架：基础模式交换切割层激活/梯度而非完整模型；安全模式通过编排器与辅助服务器秘密共享切割层张量，防止任一方观察明文张量（限于半诚实两服务器设置，标签和损失相关输出对编排器可见）。线性/仿射服务器路径可保持精确性，非线性操作需非线性 MPC 或近似。

Result: CIFAR-10 上 TL++ 基础模式 cut 1 准确率 91.41%（SD 0.19），安全模式 cut 3 准确率 90.93%（SD 0.17），超出最佳非 TL++ 基线 12 个百分点以上；基础模式每步通信减少 13.1 倍。PubMedQA 结果同样倾向 TL++。

Conclusion: TL++ 在接近集中式训练性能的同时，显著降低通信开销并提供切割层级别的秘密共享保护。

Abstract: Distributed intelligent systems increasingly need to train across data silos without centralizing raw data. Federated learning keeps data local but can suffer under heterogeneous partitions and requires repeated full-model exchange. Split learning reduces communication through cut-layer activations, but standard protocols generally do not recover centralized mini-batch gradient behavior and may expose activations and gradients in plaintext. We present TL++, a two-mode traversal-learning framework that constructs virtual batches across nodes to recover centralized mini-batch gradient behavior under explicit synchronization assumptions. Base mode exchanges cut-layer activations and gradients rather than full models. Secure mode secret-shares each cut-layer activation and gradient between an orchestrator and a non-colluding helper, preventing either server from observing plaintext cut-layer tensors. This protection is limited to a semi-honest two-server setting; labels and loss-related outputs remain visible to the orchestrator. In the lightweight secure path evaluated here, exactness requires a linear or affine server path, while nonlinear operations require nonlinear MPC or approximation. We formalize TL++, analyze communication and computation costs, and evaluate it against federated and split-learning baselines on CIFAR-10 and BioGPT/PubMedQA using full fine-tuning and LoRA. On CIFAR-10, TL++ base cut 1 and exact secure cut 3 achieve accuracies of 91.41% (SD 0.19) and 90.93% (SD 0.17), respectively, exceeding the strongest measured non-TL++ baseline by more than 12 percentage points. TL++ base cut 1 also reduces per-step communication by 13.1-fold relative to full-model synchronization. PubMedQA results similarly favor TL++. Overall, TL++ approaches centralized-training performance while reducing communication and providing activation-level secret sharing.

</details>

*Tina Dongxu Li,Mouhacine Benosman,Rajat Kumar,Kevin Tan,Ken Meszaros,Trevor Dardik*

Method: 提出算法无关的离线RL框架，包含历史信息增强状态表示、延迟影响动作空间抽象及联合上下游指标的奖励函数。使用三个SOTA离线RL算法（包括CQL）在去标识化历史运营日志上离线训练，并通过多方法评估策略性能：基于回归模型的即时奖励估计、长时域Fitted Q Evaluation以及基于Deep Koopman动力学的模型评估。

Conclusion: 离线强化学习在仓库吞吐控制优化中具有安全、可扩展的潜力，能够有效平衡吞吐效率与系统稳定性。

Abstract: We present an offline reinforcement learning (RL) framework for optimizing SLAM throughput control in a warehouse fulfillment environment. SLAM (Scan/Label/Apply/Manifest) throughput directly influences system congestion and operational efficiency. Our RL-based control approach dynamically recommends SLAM throughput settings that adaptively balance throughput maximization with downstream stability through intelligent adjustment of throttling behavior. We include a history-informed state representation, action space abstraction for delayed-impact control, and a reward function that captures both upstream and downstream operational metrics. Our approach is algorithm-agnostic, enabling integration of multiple offline RL methods under a unified architecture. We instantiate our framework with three state-of-the-art offline RL algorithms, and trained the models offline using de-identified historical operational logs from a large-scale warehouse. Policy performance is evaluated using a comprehensive multi-method strategy. These include model-free approaches including immediate reward estimation via regression models and long-horizon Fitted Q Evaluation (FQE), as well as model-based Deep Koopman dynamics evaluation. Empirical results reveal that the CQL policy consistently outperforms alternatives, improving system health by 22.97% and reducing average throttling duration by 3.18%. These findings demonstrate the potential of offline RL for safe and scalable warehouse throughput control optimization.

</details>


### [58] [Learning to Trigger: Reinforcement Learning at the Large Hadron Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23993)
*Zixin Ding,Shaghayegh Emami,Giovanna Salvi,Cecilia Tosciri,Abhijith Gandrakota,Jennifer Ngadiuba,Nhan Tran,Christian Herwig,David W. Miller,Yuxin Chen*

TL;DR: 本文首次将强化学习（RL）应用于大型强子对撞机（LHC）真实碰撞数据的实时触发阈值调优，通过GFPO-F和GFPO-FR两种变体算法，在模拟和真实数据上分别实现了48%和28%的容忍时间区间比例提升，并获得了最高2%的信号效率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高吞吐量科学设施（如LHC）依赖实时事件触发过滤，但现有触发菜单多静态且人工调优，随着探测器条件、堆积和背景组成随时间漂移而变得次优，亟需自适应动态调优方法。

Method: 将在线阈值调优建模为序贯决策问题，使用强化学习智能体读取流式速率和信号敏感特征摘要，动态更新触发阈值以最大化信号效率并跟踪目标背景率。将GFPO算法适配到流控制场景，提出GFPO-F和GFPO-FR两种变体，在训练中强制背景率可行性约束。

Result: 在模拟蒙特卡洛流上，H_T触发器和AD触发器的容忍时间区间比例分别提升48%和28%，容忍时间区间内信号效率累积增益最高达2%。在真实CMS Run 283408碰撞数据上（无需微调），H_T和AD触发器的容忍时间区间比例分别提升56%和28%，两种触发器均获得额外信号效率增益。

Conclusion: 这是首个在真实LHC碰撞数据上验证RL触发控制的演示，证明了基于强化学习的动态阈值调优在实际高能物理实验中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: High-throughput scientific facilities such as the Large Hadron Collider depend on real-time event filtering (\textit{triggering}) under tight constraints on bandwidth, latency, and storage. In practice, trigger menus are largely static and hand-tuned and can become suboptimal as detector conditions, pileup, and background composition drift over time. We cast online threshold tuning as a sequential decision-making problem: a reinforcement learning agent ingests streaming summaries of recent rates and signal-sensitive features and updates trigger thresholds to maximize signal efficiency while tracking a target background rate within a tolerance band. We adapt Group-Filtered Policy Optimization (GFPO) to streaming control and introduce two variants (GFPO-F, GFPO-FR) that enforce background rate feasibility during training. On a benchmark that emulates realistic collider operation, we study two representative triggers: a total transverse energy ($H_{T}$) trigger sensitive to pileup variation, and an anomaly-detection (AD) trigger based on reconstruction loss for rare or non-standard signatures. On Monte Carlo streams, our agent increases the fraction of in-tolerance time intervals by 48\% ($H_T$) and 28\% (AD), with a cumulative gain of up to 2\% in signal efficiency on those in-tolerance intervals. Transferring from simulation to \emph{real} collision data (CMS Run 283408), the same agent, without fine-tuning, achieves a 56\% ($H_T$) and 28\% (AD) in-tolerance improvement over baselines, with further signal-efficiency gain on both triggers. To our knowledge, this is the \emph{first} demonstration of RL-based trigger control on real Large Hadron Collider collision data. Code is available at https://github.com/Zixind/GFPO\_LHC.

</details>


### [59] [EMAgnet: Parameter-Space EMA Regularization for Policy Gradient Self-Play in Large Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23995)
*Tristan Maidment,JB Lanier,Chase McDonald,Nathan Tsang,Eugene Vinitsky,Roy Fox,Albert Wang,Wesley N. Kerr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMAgnet，使用指数移动平均策略参数作为正则化目标，替代传统均匀分布正则化，在两人零和博弈中相比PPO自对弈方法实现了更低的可利用性，尤其在含有严格占优策略的环境中表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正则化策略梯度方法（如PPO自对弈）使用均匀分布作为正则化目标，对所有动作一视同仁，忽视了不同动作的可行性差异，缺乏适应性。

Method: 提出EMAgnet，采用上一次迭代策略参数的指数移动平均（EMA）作为自适应正则化目标，使正则化目标随智能体策略改进而动态演化。

Result: 在大多数标准两人零和博弈基准及含有探索挑战和大量严格占优策略的修改基准中，相比采用线性和幂律退火调度的PPO自对弈均匀正则化方法，EMAgnet实现了更低的可利用性，且在含严格占优策略的游戏中表现一致更优。

Conclusion: EMAgnet的自适应正则化策略能更有效地引导策略学习，在复杂博弈环境中优于静态均匀正则化方法。

Abstract: Recent work has established that regularized policy gradient methods such as PPO, when used in self-play, can match or exceed specialized game-theoretic algorithms for solving two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games. The uniform distribution has emerged as a strong policy regularization target for this purpose, but it regularizes equally toward all actions regardless of their viability. We introduce EMAgnet, which instead regularizes toward an exponential moving average (EMA) of the last-iterate policy's parameters, providing an adaptive regularization target that evolves with the agent's improving strategy. We evaluate EMAgnet on both standard two-player zero-sum benchmarks and modified benchmarks with exploration challenges and large numbers of strictly dominated strategies. Relative to PPO self-play with uniform-magnet regularization under both linear and power-law annealing schedules, EMAgnet achieves lower exploitability in the majority of tested environments, with consistent performance gains across games containing strictly dominated strategies.

</details>


### [60] [Cyclic Denoising Reveals Ultrastable Memories in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24000)
*Rishabh Sharma,Stefano Martiniani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出循环去噪（cyclic denoising）作为一种针对图像扩散模型的提取攻击方法，通过在受控噪声幅度下重复前向和反向扩散，使采样陷入训练记忆图像的吸引子中，无需梯度、权重检查或提示词即可提取记忆图像，并展示了在Stable Diffusion v1.4和像素空间DDPM中的一致表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型提取攻击通常依赖大规模提示生成和事后相似性过滤（generate-and-filter方法），效率有限且需要先验知识。受无序固体中随机组织现象的启发，作者希望探索一种无需条件、无需梯度访问、无需训练数据先验知识的更直接有效的记忆提取方法。

Method: 提出循环去噪攻击：对样本进行受控噪声幅度的重复前向加噪和反向去噪循环，利用不同噪声幅度下的动力学行为差异——低幅循环产生平凡固定点或极限环，高幅循环诱发重排、盆地跳跃并陷入结构化记忆吸引子。该方法仅需采样器级控制，无梯度、无权重检查、无提示词、无字幕、无训练数据先验知识，且可无条件运行。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4和像素空间DDPM上观察到一致现象：最深吸引子具有超稳定性，能在近乎完全破坏后再生并持续数千个噪声-去噪循环；许多吸引子对应记忆的训练图像，包括库存照片、品牌水印和网络爬虫伪影；观察到类似屈服的转变、层次性部分吸收、提示稳定化的盆地以及跨初始条件的吸引子集普适性。

Conclusion: 循环去噪既是对生成景观的一种物理启发式探测工具，也是一种实用的记忆审计方法，对隐私保护、版权合规和模型指纹识别具有重要意义。

Abstract: We introduce cyclic denoising -- repeated forward and reverse diffusion at controlled noise amplitudes -- as an extraction attack for image diffusion models. Inspired by random organization in disordered solids, cyclic denoising exposes regions of the learned distribution that are largely inaccessible to standard sampling. The dynamics drive samples toward attractors with a broad stability spectrum. The deepest attractors are ultrastable: they regenerate after near-total corruption and persist through thousands of noising-denoising cycles. Many of these attractors correspond to memorized training images, including stock photographs, brand watermarks, and web-crawl artifacts. The attack requires only sampler-level control, with no gradients, weight inspection, prompts, captions, or prior knowledge of the training data. Unlike generate-and-filter attacks, which rely on large-scale prompted generation and post-hoc similarity or membership-inference filtering, our main protocol is fully unconditioned. We demonstrate the phenomenon in Stable Diffusion v1.4 and in a pixel-space DDPM, showing consistent behavior across latent- and pixel-space diffusion models. Across noise amplitudes, we observe a yielding-like transition: low-amplitude cycling produces trivial absorbing fixed points or limit cycles, while larger amplitudes induce rearrangements, basin hopping, and long-lived trapping in structured memorized attractor basins. We also observe hierarchical partial absorption, prompt-stabilized basins, and cross-initial-condition universality of the recovered attractor set. Our results therefore show that cyclic denoising is both a physics-inspired probe of generative landscapes and a practical tool for memorization auditing, with implications for privacy, copyright compliance, and model fingerprinting.

</details>


### [61] [Fast and Slow Variational Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24007)
*Subarnaduti Paul,Yohan Jung,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Siddharth Swaroop,Thomas Möllenhoff,Martin Mundt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVON优化器，通过VCL框架中的快速与慢速适应机制（合并过去后验作为先验）实现持续学习，在域增量学习、持续预训练和LLM微调中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度网络缺乏持续适应的内在机制，而生物系统中快速与慢速适应（平衡稳定性与可塑性）是一种自然机制，但如何将其融入常用优化器尚无共识。

Method: 基于VCL框架，将过去后验合并作为先验实现慢速适应（减缓知识漂移），再通过VCL更新实现快速权重更新；该过程无缝集成到IVON优化器中（形式与成本几乎与Adam相同），形成CoVON优化器。

Result: CoVON不仅一致地优于现有VCL优化器，还在域增量学习、持续预训练和大语言模型微调中表现优于其他权重正则化策略。

Conclusion: 通过VCL框架中的快慢适应机制（合并后验作为先验）可以简单有效地实现持续学习，CoVON优化器在多个持续学习场景中表现优越。

Abstract: Continual learning remains a major challenge for modern deep networks, partly because commonly used optimizers lack inherent mechanisms for continual adaptation. One such natural mechanism is fast and slow adaptation to balance stability and plasticity. This mechanism has deep roots in neuroscience and biology, but there is no consensus on how to best incorporate it in commonly used optimizers. Here, we show that this can be easily done via the VCL framework, where past posteriors are used as priors in the future. Our key idea is to incorporate slow adaptation via merging of past posteriors to slow down the drift in the knowledge as learning progresses. The merged posterior is then used as the prior in the VCL update to implement the fast-weight updates. These steps can be seamlessly implemented in the IVON optimizer, whose form and costs are nearly identical to that of Adam. We call this new optimizer the Continual IVON (CoVON) optimizer and show that it not only consistently improves over existing VCL optimizers, but also performs better than other weight-regularization strategies across domain-incremental learning, continual pre-training, and fine-tuning of large language models.

</details>


### [62] [You Don't Need to Run Every Eval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24020)
*Yuchen Zeng,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现84个前沿模型在133个基准上的得分矩阵近似秩为2（即模型的整体表现主要由两个因子决定），并基于此设计了BenchPress方法：通过秩-2矩阵补全技术，仅需5个基准即可预测模型在其他所有基准上的得分（误差<3.93分），极大减少了评估开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代模型发布前需要在40+基准上进行大量重复评估，但并非所有评估都是必要的。本文旨在探索能否通过少量基准预测模型在全部基准上的表现，从而大幅降低评估成本。

Method: 收集84个模型×133个基准的公共得分矩阵（23.3%填充），通过奇异值分解发现矩阵近似秩为2。设计BenchPress：基于logit空间的秩-2矩阵补全方法，并配有置信度层判断预测可靠性。进一步通过贪心搜索找出最优的5个基准子集用于预测全部得分。

Result: 秩-2假设成立：两因子可解释90%以上模型间差异；BenchPress恢复隐藏得分误差仅4.6分；5个基准（GPQA-D, HLE, Codeforces, MMLU-Pro, ARC-AGI-1）可恢复全部得分至3.93分误差；更经济的5基准集（GPQA-D, MMLU-Pro, Aider Polyglot, MATH-500, AIME 2026）误差4.55分。

Conclusion: 模型在众多基准上的表现本质上可由少数潜在因子决定，因此可以通过少量精心选择的基准高效预测完整评估画像。该方法可显著节省评估资源，并提供了交互式工具供社区使用。

Abstract: A modern model release reports scores on 40+ benchmarks and the same evaluations were run many more times before it: to track training progress, compare design choices, and select the checkpoint for the release. But do we need to run every eval? We compile a public score matrix of 84 frontier models on 133 benchmarks (2,604 cells, 23.3% filled) and find it is approximately rank-2: a model's scores across all 133 benchmarks are largely determined by just two numbers. We confirm this in two ways: scores hidden from the matrix are best recovered using two factors, and two factors already explain over 90% of the variation among models on the benchmarks they share. Building on this, we design BenchPress: a logit-space rank-2 matrix completion method that recovers held-out scores to within 4.6 points, and a confidence layer that says when each prediction can be trusted. Using BenchPress, we find a subset of five benchmarks {GPQA-D, HLE, Codeforces, MMLU-Pro, ARC-AGI-1} that can recover the rest of a model's public scorecard to within 3.93 points. For a tighter inference budget, a cheaper set {GPQA-D, MMLU-Pro, Aider Polyglot, MATH-500, AIME 2026} can predict a model's evals to within 4.55. We release the score matrix, the BenchPress code, and an interactive tool that predicts any model's score on any benchmark.

</details>


### [63] [Information-Theoretic Classifier-Free Guidance with Adaptive Schedule Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24025)
*Haobo Chen,Xiangxiang Xu,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的CFG调度优化框架，通过干净端点参考来指定一致性-覆盖率的权衡，并优化引导采样器产生的实际分布，在ImageNet-512和COCO上学习到了优于恒定引导的调度策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分类器自由引导（CFG）在提升条件一致性的同时会降低多样性和分布覆盖率，且不清楚如何在反向轨迹中控制这种一致性-覆盖率权衡，因为CFG诱导的分布并不是简单的固定时间倾斜分布。

Method: 提出信息论框架，使用干净端点参考指定期望的一致性-覆盖率权衡，优化引导采样器产生的实际分布；推导出轨迹级公式，通过样本和分数评估估计目标函数，避免显式密度估计。

Result: 在ImageNet-512（EDM-XXL）和COCO（SD-XL）上，学习到的调度策略取得了有竞争力或更优的权衡，并且能够选择性地在不同噪声级别分配引导强度。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效优化CFG调度，实现比恒定引导更好的一致性-覆盖率权衡，且引导强度的分配与噪声级别相关。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved strong performance in image, text-to-image, and video generation, where conditional generation is often controlled by classifier-free guidance (CFG). CFG improves condition consistency by increasing a guidance weight, but stronger guidance typically reduces diversity and distributional coverage. It remains unclear how this consistency-coverage trade-off should be controlled across the reverse trajectory, since the distribution induced by CFG is not simply the fixed-time tilted distribution given by the guided score field. To address this issue, we propose an information-theoretic framework for CFG schedule optimization. Our approach uses a clean endpoint reference to specify the desired consistency-coverage trade-off, while optimizing the actual distribution induced by the guided sampler toward this reference. We derive trajectory-level formulas to estimate the objective from samples and score evaluations, avoiding explicit density estimation. On ImageNet-512 with EDM-XXL and COCO with SD-XL, the learned schedules achieve competitive or improved trade-offs over constant guidance and allocate guidance selectively across noise levels.

</details>


### [64] [RoPE-Aware Bit Allocation for KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24033)
*Fengfeng Liang,Yuechen Zhang,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Block-GTQ是一种基于RoPE感知的键缓存量化位分配方法，通过将键缓存量化为块级位分配问题，利用能量分数为每个RoPE块贪婪分配整数位宽，在低比特量化下显著提升模型的长上下文检索、理解和推理能力，并实现高效打包缓存服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低比特KV缓存量化方法将每个缓存的键视为平面向量，忽略了RoPE下键对注意力logit的贡献可分解为位置相关的二维频率块之和这一结构特性，导致量化位分配不合理。

Method: Block-GTQ构建在TurboQuant-MSE(TQ-MSE)之上，为每层和每个KV头计算每个RoPE块的无标签能量分数，然后通过边际增益贪心分配整数位宽，实现RoPE感知的块级位分配。

Result: 在十模型诊断面板上，每层MAE降低32-80%；在K2V2下，Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct的六任务NIAH平均从70.6提升至97.4，LongBench-EN平均从36.87提升至53.31；在AIME 2024/2025上，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B在K3V2下得分51.7/37.5（fp16为54.2/37.9），而均匀TQ-MSE坍缩至0.0/0.0；K3V3实现3.24x KV缓存压缩，速度比fp16 FlashAttention2快1.34倍，峰值内存从56.31GB降至19.85GB。

Conclusion: Block-GTQ通过RoPE感知的块级位分配策略，在低比特量化下实现了接近fp16的推理质量，同时大幅降低内存占用并提升推理速度，验证了利用RoPE结构特性进行量化位分配的有效性。

Abstract: Existing low-bit KV-cache quantizers often treat each cached key as a flat vector. Under RoPE, however, a key's contribution to a future attention logit decomposes into a position-dependent sum over two-dimensional frequency blocks. This makes key-cache quantization a block-wise bit-allocation problem: high-energy RoPE blocks are more sensitive to quantization error and should receive more bits. We introduce Block-GTQ, a RoPE-aware bit allocator for key-cache quantization built on TurboQuant-MSE(TQ-MSE). For each layer and KV head, Block-GTQ computes a label-free energy score for each RoPE block and greedily allocates integer bit widths by marginal gain. Under matched K/V bit budgets, Block-GTQ better preserves RoPE query-key logits on a ten-model diagnostic panel, cutting per-layer MAE by 32-80% at 2 and 3 b/dim K-only quantization and winning all 367/367 layer comparisons against uniform TQ-MSE. These fidelity gains translate to stronger downstream long-context retrieval, understanding, and reasoning. At K2V2 on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Block-GTQ raises the six-task NIAH average from 70.6 to 97.4, and the LongBench-EN average from 36.87 to 53.31. On AIME 2024/2025 with DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, without an fp16 recent-key buffer, Block-GTQ at K3V2 scores 51.7/37.5, close to fp16's 54.2/37.9, whereas uniform TQ-MSE collapses to 0.0/0.0. We further implement a packed-cache serving path. On a single H800 GPU with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, packed K3V3 achieves 3.24x KV-cache compression with fp16-comparable quality, runs 1.34x faster than fp16 FlashAttention2 at 128K context, reduces peak memory from 56.31 GB to 19.85 GB, and remains feasible at 256K and 512K where fp16 OOMs. Code is available at https://github.com/JIA-Lab-research/blockgtq.

</details>


### [65] [Rapid FinFET Modelling Using an Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24046)
*Amit Sarkar Suman Sau,Swagata Mandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自编码器（AE）的机器学习框架，用于高效建模FinFET器件。通过BSIM-CMG校准生成I-V数据集，训练自编码器将完整I-V曲线压缩到低维潜空间，并显式引入VDS作为输入特征以捕捉偏置依赖变化。模型可重构完整I-V曲线并直接提取关键器件指标（VTH、SS、峰值gm），实现了数据驱动紧凑模型的高精度建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FinFET紧凑建模依赖物理方程和大量参数提取，流程复杂且耗时；数据驱动方法需要大量训练数据且泛化能力有限。本文旨在提出一种高效、数据高效的数据驱动建模框架，利用自编码器压缩I-V曲线，实现快速准确的器件表征与建模。

Method: 1) 使用BSIM-CMG校准模型生成ID-VG特性数据集；2) 训练自编码器将完整I-V曲线压缩至低维潜空间；3) 显式将VDS作为输入特征引入网络，增强模型对偏置依赖变化的捕捉能力；4) 从重构的I-V曲线中直接提取VTH、SS、峰值gm等关键器件指标。

Result: 自编码器模型能够高精度重构完整I-V曲线，并准确提取VTH、SS、峰值gm等关键器件参数，且仅需少量训练数据即可达到高精度。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的紧凑模型基于实际表征数据即可实现高精度建模，为快速器件表征、建模和电路级仿真提供了有力工具。

Abstract: This work presents a machine learning framework that leverages an autoencoder (AE) for the efficient modeling of FinFET. We first calibrated a BSIM-CMG model to generate a dataset of current-voltage (ID-VG) characteristics. This data was used to train an autoencoder that compresses full I-V curves into a low-dimensional latent space, which intrinsically encodes key device physics. A key innovation is the explicit incorporation of parameter such as drain to source voltage (VDS) as an input feature, enhancing the model ability to capture bias dependent variation. The trained model successfully reconstructs full I-V curves and directly extracts critical device metrics including threshold voltage (VTH), subthreshold slope (SS), and peak transconductance (gm). This approach demonstrates that data driven compact models, built from actual characterization data, can achieve high accuracy with minimal training data, providing a powerful tool for rapid device characterization, modelling and circuit level simulation.

</details>


### [66] [RAVEN: A Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network for Financial Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24062)
*Cheng He,Zhenyu Guan,Xijie Liang,Defu Lian,Jiajia Li,Enhong Chen,Patrick P. C. Lee,Geng Hu,Zehao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对金融时间序列中固定上下文窗口无法适配非平稳过程时变最优回溯长度的问题，提出RAVEN框架，通过分层嵌套窗口、累积重要性阈值裁剪、尺度专家路由和相关性感知加权，在股指对数收益率预测和基金销售预测上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳性、极低信噪比和机制依赖的时序依赖关系，现有SOTA模型使用固定上下文窗口，无法匹配非平稳价格过程中随时间变化的最优回溯长度，限制了预测性能。

Method: 提出RAVEN框架：(1) 构建分层嵌套连续窗口，按逆时间顺序对 patches 进行重要性评分；(2) 采用累积重要性阈值裁剪（CIT）推导嵌套前缀窗口，每个窗口路由到尺度特化专家；(3) 全局压缩表示（GCR）分支并行处理完整上下文，保持全局时序连贯性；(4) 引入相关性感知加权（CAW）对齐变长专家输出并惩罚余弦相似度，再进行聚合。

Result: 在HS300和S&P500的累计对数收益率预测上，Pearson相关系数分别提升9.2%和20.2%；基金销售预测上MSE降低18.2%；在四个PEMS交通基准的16项指标中取得14项最佳结果。

Conclusion: RAVEN通过数据自适应确定上下文窗口长度，有效解决了固定窗口与非平稳金融时序之间的不匹配问题，在金融预测和交通预测任务上均达到SOTA性能，验证了变上下文专家网络和相关性感知加权机制的有效性。

Abstract: Financial time series forecasting presents structural challenges absent from standard benchmarks. Log-returns are non-stationary, exhibit exceptionally low signal-to-noise (SNR) ratios, and are governed by regime-dependent temporal dependencies. We identify a key limitation of state-of-the-art (SOTA) time series models in financial settings. A fixed context window is mismatched to the time-varying optimal look-back of non-stationary price processes. We propose the Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network (RAVEN), a Mixture-of-Experts framework designed to adaptively determine the temporal context for each input sample. Instead of relying on a fixed look-back horizon, RAVEN constructs a hierarchy of nested contiguous windows whose lengths are determined by the data itself. Specifically, RAVEN scores patches by learned importance in reverse chronological order and applies the Cumulative Importance Thresholding (CIT) mechanism to derive nested prefix windows, each routed to a scale-specialized expert. A Global Compressed Representation (GCR) branch runs in parallel over the full context, preserving global temporal coherence that local experts cannot guarantee. Because the nested routing induces structured overlap among expert inputs, we introduce a Correlation-Aware Weighting (CAW) to align variable-length expert outputs and penalize pairwise cosine similarity prior to aggregation. Experiments on cumulative log-return prediction (HS300, S&P500) and fund sales forecasting demonstrate that RAVEN achieves SOTA performances, improves Pearson correlation by 9.2% on HS300 and 20.2% on S&P500, and reduces MSE by 18.2% on fund sales forecasting, while achieving the best results in 14 of 16 metrics on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [67] [Blockwise Policy-Drift Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24084)
*Liwen Zheng,Haiyun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分块策略漂移门控机制（Blockwise Policy-Drift Gating），通过计算行为学生与当前学生在采样令牌路径上的log概率偏移，聚合为固定分块（如64令牌）的均值归一化门控值，用于重加权同策略蒸馏（OPD）的位置损失。该方法在不改变教师目标、教师Top-K支持或rollout策略的情况下，在Qwen3数学推理基准上将sampled-token OPD的pass@8从0.4978提升至0.5160，识别出局部新旧策略漂移可作为复用rollout的有效控制信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏（OPD）在长程推理任务上存在脆弱性，尤其是sampled-token OPD方法。现有局部教师支持匹配虽然有效但不够轻量，需要一种更简洁的仅依赖学生自身的旧-当前策略漂移控制机制来提升OPD在rollout复用场景下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Blockwise Policy-Drift Gating方法：在固定长度分块（如64令牌）上，计算行为学生（rollout采样时策略）与当前学生之间的log概率偏移，聚合后做均值归一化，得到的门控值作为权重重新加权每个位置的OPD损失。门控值detached不参与梯度传播，不改变教师信号或采样策略。

Result: 在Qwen3数学推理基准（AIME24、AIME25、MATH500、AMC23）上，固定64令牌分块门控将sampled-token OPD的平均pass@8从0.4978提升至0.5160。在Teacher-TopK/LSM设置中，Block64取得了四个基准的最佳平均pass@8。

Conclusion: 局部新旧策略漂移可作为实用的控制信号用于复用rollout的OPD训练，分块级门控是一种简单有效的默认方法，能够提升求解率鲁棒性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student policy using teacher signals computed on trajectories sampled by the student itself. Recent work shows that sampled-token OPD can be fragile on long-horizon reasoning tasks and that local teacher-support matching is a simple and effective repair. This paper introduces blockwise policy-drift gating, a lightweight student-only old-current drift controller for OPD under rollout reuse. The method computes log-probability shifts between the behavior student and the current student on the sampled token path, aggregates these shifts over fixed blocks or spans, and uses the resulting detached, mean-normalized gates to reweight OPD position losses. It does not change teacher targets, teacher top-K supports, or the rollout policy. In a six-variant Qwen3 math reasoning benchmark with a uniform 200-step training budget for all trained variants, we use pass@8 as the primary problem-level solve-rate metric. Fixed 64-token block gating improves sampled-token OPD mean pass@8 from 0.4978 to 0.5160 across AIME24, AIME25, MATH500, and AMC23. On Teacher-TopK/LSM, Block64 gives the best four-benchmark mean pass@8 among trained students. The results identify local old-current policy drift as a practical control signal for reused OPD rollouts and motivate block-level gating as a simple default for improving solve-rate robustness.

</details>


### [68] [NeuroSonic: Conditional Flow Matching for EEG-to-Speech Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24087)
*Wenhao Gao,Yifan Wang,Yijia Ma,Carl Yang,Wen Li,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroSonic提出了一种基于条件流匹配的框架用于EEG到语音的重建，通过学习确定性概率流速度场将噪声污染的声学状态映射到干净语音，避免了随机采样，在CineBrain和EAV基准上显著优于GAN、扩散和均值流基线，感知质量提升最高达26.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信号本身具有弱、空间弥散、高度可变的特点，而语音具有连贯的声学轨迹和强谐波结构，二者之间的不匹配使得直接从EEG回归波形不稳定，随机多步生成方法对伪影依赖和受试者变异性敏感，亟需一种更稳定的重建框架。

Method: 提出NeuroSonic框架，采用条件流匹配（conditional flow-matching）学习一个确定性概率流速度场，该场在EEG条件下将噪声扰动的声学状态传输到干净语音；EEG和音频被嵌入共享的token空间，由时间条件门控Transformer参数化传输常微分方程（ODE），从而显式建模轨迹演化，避免迭代随机采样。

Result: 在CineBrain和EAV两个基准数据集上，NeuroSonic在分布真实性、频谱保真度和感知质量上均优于GAN、扩散模型和均值流基线，整体感知质量提升最高达26.3%；在伪影严重、条件变异最强的片段上性能差距最为显著。

Conclusion: 确定性条件传输为EEG驱动的语音重建提供了一种稳定有效的方案，能够有效应对EEG信号的弱性和变异性，大幅提升重建质量。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous speech from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) remains fundamentally challenging. EEG provides a weak, spatially diffuse, and highly variable measurement of distributed cortical activity, whereas speech is organized as a coherent acoustic trajectory with strong harmonic and temporal structure. The resulting mismatch makes waveform regression unstable and causes stochastic multi-step generation to be sensitive to artifact-dependent conditioning and subject variability. We introduce NeuroSonic, a conditional flow-matching framework for EEG-to-speech reconstruction. Instead of predicting waveforms directly or refining them through stochastic denoising, NeuroSonic learns a deterministic probability-flow velocity field that transports a noise-corrupted acoustic state toward clean speech under EEG conditioning. EEG and audio are embedded into a shared token space and processed by a time-conditioned gated Transformer that parameterizes the transport ordinary differential equation. This formulation models trajectory evolution explicitly while avoiding iterative stochastic sampling. We evaluate NeuroSonic on the CineBrain and EAV benchmarks under cross-subject evaluation.
