Across both datasets, the proposed method improves distributional realism, spectral fidelity, and perceptual quality over representative GAN-, diffusion-, and mean-flow baselines, with up to a 26.3\% gain in overall perceptual quality. The performance gap is most evident in artifact-heavy segments, where conditioning variability is strongest. These findings indicate that deterministic conditional transport provides a stable and effective formulation for EEG-driven speech reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/Y-Research-SBU/NeuroSonic/ .

</details>


### [69] [FedUP: One-Shot Federated Unlearning via Centroid-Guided Plug-in Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24113)
*Feihong Nan,Zhengyi Zhong,Pan Wang,Weidong Bao,Xiongtao Zhang,Quan Wen,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedUP是一个基于可插拔过滤器的单次联邦遗忘框架，通过冻结原始模型参数并在服务端使用差分隐私保护的类中心样本训练过滤器，实现秒级遗忘延迟，同时保持非目标知识完整性，支持可逆恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法面临两个核心矛盾：非目标知识损失与高请求延迟之间的两难困境。传统方法需要多轮客户端-服务器通信和复杂重训练，导致遗忘延迟高达数分钟，且难以在消除目标数据影响的同时保持模型原有性能。

Method: 提出FedUP框架，核心是设计轻量级可插拔过滤器作为"知识漏斗"。具体包括：1) 冻结原始模型参数，避免重训练；2) 在服务端使用差分隐私(DP)保护的类中心样本训练过滤器；3) 采用单次通信实现遗忘，无需多轮客户端交互；4) 架构天然支持可逆性，移除过滤器即可恢复遗忘知识。

Result: 在多种图像和文本任务上的实验表明，FedUP能有效减少非目标知识损失，实现优越的遗忘精度和效率，将遗忘延迟从分钟级降至秒级。

Conclusion: FedUP通过可插拔过滤器设计，成功解决了联邦遗忘中非目标知识损失与高延迟的矛盾，实现了高效、精确且可逆的联邦遗忘，代码已开源。

Abstract: Federated unlearning (FU) is critical for complying with legal mandates like the right to be forgotten in decentralized systems, yet current methods face a persistent dilemma between non-target knowledge loss and high request latency. To resolve these issues, we propose FedUP, a one-shot federated unlearning framework utilizing lightweight pluggable filters that act as a "knowledge funnel" to screen out target data while preserving original model performance. By freezing original model parameters and training filters at the server side using differentially private (DP)-protected class centroid samples, FedUP bypasses the need for multi-round client-server communication and complex retraining, reducing unlearning latency from minutes to mere seconds. Additionally, the framework's pluggable architecture ensures inherent reversibility, enabling the seamless restoration of forgotten knowledge by simply removing the filters. Extensive experiments on diverse image and text tasks demonstrate that FedUP effectively reduces non-target knowledge loss and achieves superior unlearning precision and efficiency across various scenarios. Code is available at: https://github.com/suows/FedUP-code.

</details>


### [70] [When Top-1 Fails: Calibrating LoRA Monitors for Masked Diffusion LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24119)
*Lucky Verma,Pratik Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DLM的PEFT微调中，top-1 argmax浓度作为坍塌预警完全失效（零精度），原因是预平衡饱和；而最大LoRA梯度范数作为参数侧信号，在LLaDA系列上达到精度0.68、F1=0.79，但需按模型族校准阈值，不能作为通用检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型（DLM）微调中，去噪时间置信度监控被用于廉价诊断，但其在PEFT（参数高效微调）场景下的有效性未经检验。本文旨在评估这些信号在LoRA微调中是否真正能作为模型坍塌的预警。

Method: 在三个DLM家族的816个LoRA/PEFT配置上，测试了top-1 argmax浓度作为坍塌预警的性能；随后提出使用最大LoRA梯度范数（参数侧梯度路由信号）替代，并在LLaDA系列上通过训练优化阈值进行评估。

Result: top-1 argmax浓度在所有配置下均触发预警但实际零坍塌，精度为零，原因是预平衡饱和（优化前浓度已高且对训练稳定性不敏感）。最大LoRA梯度范数在LLaDA系列上达到精度0.68、F1=0.79，优于全正类top-1基线，但自回归对照和跨模型族阈值失效将其限制为短期DLM-LoRA检测器。

Conclusion: 建议放弃top-1作为PEFT报警信号，改用训练早期最大梯度日志，并针对每个DLM模型族校准阈值，再进行路由检查。该方法不具备通用坍塌检测能力。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language model (DLM) fine-tuning inherits inexpensive diagnostics from denoising-time confidence monitors, but their PEFT-training meaning is untested. We test top-1 argmax concentration as a collapse warning. Across 816 LoRA/PEFT configurations from three DLM families, the warning fires for every configuration while logs record 0/816 actual collapses at the 200 step horizon, giving zero precision. The cause is pre-equilibrium saturation: top-1 concentration is already high before optimization and quickly becomes insensitive to final training stability. We then evaluate max LoRA gradient norm, a parameter-side signal that samples gradient routing rather than token concentration. On a pooled held-out LLaDA-family split, a train-optimized threshold identifies top-decile final-loss configurations with precision 0.68 and F1=0.79, above the all-positive top-1 baseline even at the lower split-bootstrap confidence bound. Autoregressive controls and cross-family threshold failures bound the result to short-horizon DLM-LoRA inspection rather than a universal collapse detector. Workflow: drop top-1 as a PEFT alarm, log max-gradient early in training, and calibrate thresholds per DLM family before routing runs for inspection.

</details>


### [71] [Holistic Data Scheduler for LLM Pre-training via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24133)
*Chenhao Dang,Jing Ma,Mingjie Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HDS（整体数据调度器）的在线数据混合框架，将数据调度建模为连续控制空间中的强化学习问题，利用SAC算法和包含数据质量、跨领域影响、权重范数的多目标奖励函数，在The Pile基准测试上以44%更少的训练迭代达到同等困惑度，并在MMLU 0-shot任务上提升7.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线数据混合（ODM）方法仅依赖单一优化视角，无法满足大语言模型预训练对动态数据组合的多维度需求。

Method: 将数据调度问题建模为连续控制空间中的强化学习问题，使用Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）算法进行策略探索，设计了包含数据驱动奖励（质量）、损失驱动奖励（跨领域影响）和模型驱动奖励（权重范数）的多目标整体奖励函数。

Result: 在The Pile基准上，HDS以44%更少的训练迭代达到次优方法的最终验证困惑度；在MMLU 0-shot任务上提升7.2%，并在其他基准上获得一致提升。

Conclusion: HDS通过多维度奖励函数的强化学习框架，有效提升了LLM预训练的训练效率和最终模型能力。

Abstract: The composition of training data, governed by the diversity of sources and their mixing strategy, is a cornerstone of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training. Online Data Mixing (ODM), the technique of adaptively adjusting data mixtures during training, has emerged as a promising direction to improve efficiency. However, existing methods are constrained by their reliance on a singular optimization perspective, which fundamentally overlooks the need for complex LLM pre-training to consider the dynamic data composition from multiple dimensions. To overcome this limitation, we introduce the Holistic Data Scheduler (HDS), a novel online data mixing framework. HDS formulates the data scheduling challenge as a reinforcement learning problem in a continuous control space and leverages the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm for its stability and sample efficiency in exploring the high-dimensional policy space. At the core of HDS lies a novel multi-objective, holistic reward function that integrates three critical perspectives: a data-driven reward for quality, a loss-driven reward capturing inter-domain influence, and a model-driven reward based on weight norms. To validate our design and determine its optimal configuration, we conducted systematic experiments on LLMs of various sizes. On The Pile benchmark, HDS reaches the final validation perplexity of the next best method with 44% fewer training iterations. Furthermore, it achieves a 7.2% improvement on the MMLU 0-shot task along with consistent gains on other benchmarks, showcasing its ability to enhance both training efficiency and final model capability.

</details>


### [72] [A Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation Sampler for Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24140)
*Feiyang Fu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离散流匹配（DFM）在有限函数评估次数下的高效采样问题，提出时间重新参数化累积强度外推（TR-CIE）采样器，通过调度基时间重新参数化和累积强度外推更新规则，在无需额外模型评估的情况下提升采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流匹配（DFM）在离散状态空间上的生成建模中具有优势，但现有采样方法（如τ-leaping）在有限函数评估次数（NFE）下效率不高，缺乏系统的优化策略。

Method: TR-CIE包含两个组件：(1) 基于噪声调度的调度基时间重新参数化，将时间网格按噪声调度重新缩放，吸收调度相关的增长项并缓解终端采样阶段的刚性；(2) 累积强度外推更新规则，通过重用上一步缓存的模型输出作为历史项，在非均匀时间网格上改进阶梯累积强度的近似。

Result: 在合成任务、文本生成和文本到图像基准测试中，TR-CIE在有限NFE条件下显著提升了采样质量。

Conclusion: TR-CIE采样器在每步仅需一次函数评估、不引入额外模型计算开销的前提下，有效改善了DFM在受限评估次数下的采样性能，理论分析保证了累积强度的局部近似误差有界并建立了收敛性结果。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching (DFM) provides a principled framework for generative modeling on discrete state spaces via continuous-time Markov chain dynamics. In practice, sampling for DFM commonly employs discretizations such as $τ$-leaping, yet efficient sampling methods under a limited number of function evaluations (NFE) remain less studied. To address this gap, we propose the Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation (TR-CIE) sampler, which aims to improve sampling quality when function evaluations are restricted. TR-CIE consists of two components. First, a schedule-based time reparameterization rescales the time grid according to the noise schedule. Under standard factorized DFM rate parameterizations, this transformation of variables absorbs the schedule-dependent growth term and mitigates stiffness near the terminal sampling stage. Second, we introduce a cumulative-intensity extrapolation updating rule. By reusing cached model outputs from the previous step as a history term, this improves the approximation of stepwise cumulative intensities on the resulting non-uniform time grid. We provide a theoretical analysis that bounds the local approximation error of cumulative intensities and establishes convergence results. The resulting sampler requires one NFE per step and introduces no additional model evaluations compared to the standard $τ$-leaping sampler. Extensive experiments on synthetic tasks, text generation, and text-to-image benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves sampling quality under limited NFE.

</details>


### [73] [AsyncOPD: How Stale Can On-Policy Distillation Be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24143)
*Wonjun Kang,Kevin Galim,Seunghyuk Oh,Minjun Kang,Sanghyun Park,Donghoon Kim,Minjae Lee,Minseo Kim,Rishabh Tiwari,Yuchen Zeng,Hyung Il Koo,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了异步在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的陈旧策略问题，发现KL散度方向影响陈旧数据的鲁棒性（前向KL更稳健，反向KL脆弱），提出并开源了AsyncOPD训练框架，通过多采样蒙特卡洛估计和反向KL信号重计算等策略，在保持精度的同时实现1.6-3.8倍的训练吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在大语言模型后训练中日益重要，但其面临与强化学习类似的在线策略系统瓶颈——对于推理任务，rollout生成时间往往主导训练时间。异步训练虽可缓解该瓶颈，但会引入陈旧策略数据。尽管异步RL中的陈旧数据问题已有研究，但在OPD中的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 1) 系统研究KL方向对陈旧数据鲁棒性的影响：前向KL对陈旧rollout更鲁棒，反向KL易受影响。2) 针对反向KL情况，测试异步RL稳定方法（无效），提出OPD特定替代方案：在learn端用当前学生模型重计算反向KL信号。3) 分析有限教师得分缓存带来的偏差-方差权衡，提出多采样蒙特卡洛估计。4) 基于上述发现构建并开源的AsyncOPD异步训练框架。

Result: AsyncOPD在严格同步训练基础上实现了1.6倍到3.8倍的训练吞吐量提升，同时达到可比的准确率。

Conclusion: 异步OPD中KL方向选择至关重要，前向KL天然抗陈旧数据；对于反向KL，重计算信号比异步RL的稳定方法更有效；多采样MC估计能在稀疏反向KL估计中平衡偏差-方差；异步OPD能在不牺牲精度的情况下大幅提升训练吞吐量。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts guided by teacher feedback and is becoming increasingly important for large language model (LLM) post-training. Like reinforcement learning (RL), however, OPD faces an on-policy systems bottleneck, as rollouts can dominate training time for reasoning workloads. Asynchronous training pipelines can alleviate this bottleneck by decoupling rollout generation from learner updates, but doing so introduces stale-policy data. While prior work has studied stale data in asynchronous RL, its effects in OPD remain underexplored. We present the first systematic study of staleness in asynchronous OPD, focusing on a practical setting where teacher feedback is implemented through local KL losses and full-vocabulary teacher logits are too expensive to store or transfer, necessitating finite teacher-score caches. We first show that KL direction changes the stale-data problem: teacher-weighted forward KL is more robust to stale rollouts, whereas student-weighted reverse KL is vulnerable. Second, for this vulnerable reverse-KL case, we study whether methods designed to stabilize asynchronous RL can mitigate OPD staleness. In our experiments, they do not improve over a simpler OPD-specific surrogate: recomputing the reverse-KL signal under the current student at learner time. Third, we analyze how finite teacher-score caches create a bias-variance tradeoff for sparse and sampled reverse-KL OPD estimators. This motivates multi-sample Monte Carlo (MC), which preserves MC correctability while reducing one-sample variance. Finally, we present and open-source AsyncOPD, a fully asynchronous OPD training pipeline built from these estimator choices. Experiments show that AsyncOPD improves training throughput by $1.6\times$ to $3.8\times$ over strict synchronous training while reaching comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [Lightweight Transformer Models for On-Device Fault Detection: A Benchmark Study on Resource-Constrained Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24173)
*Disha Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在三个公开数据集上系统比较了传统机器学习方法（随机森林、XGBoost、SVM、逻辑回归）与轻量级Transformer架构（DistilBERT、TinyBERT-6L、TinyBERT-4L、MobileBERT）在设备端故障检测中的性能、模型大小和延迟表现，并提出两阶段自适应推理流水线来优化资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上部署机器学习模型进行实时故障检测时，需要在准确率、延迟和模型大小之间进行权衡，但目前缺乏对这些方法在同类条件下的系统性比较。

Method: 在NASA C-MAPSS、SECOM、UCI AI4I 2020三个数据集上，对比8种模型（4种传统ML + 4种轻量级Transformer）的二分类故障检测性能，评估F1分数、AUC、模型大小和CPU推理延迟；进一步引入INT8动态量化和两阶段自适应推理流水线（小型量化模型作为分流器，仅2.1%的样本交由大型专家模型处理）。

Result: 在C-MAPSS数据集上，轻量级Transformer与传统ML在F1分数（87.8%）上持平，但模型大小高出100倍、延迟高出9000倍；TinyBERT-4L是最适合部署的Transformer模型（55 MB，18 ms延迟）；INT8量化减小25%体积且保持86.9% F1；自适应流水线在19.5 ms平均延迟下达到87.6% F1；在严重类别不平衡数据集上，所有方法均表现不佳。

Conclusion: 对于特征分离良好的传感器数据，传统ML方法在资源效率上显著优于轻量级Transformer；INT8量化与自适应推理可有效提升部署效率；严重类别不平衡仍是当前故障检测方法的基本局限。

Abstract: On-device fault detection enables real-time diagnostics without cloud dependency, but deploying machine learning models on resource-constrained hardware demands careful tradeoffs between accuracy, latency, and model size. We present a benchmark comparing traditional ML methods (Random Forest, XGBoost, SVM, Logistic Regression) against lightweight transformer architectures (DistilBERT, TinyBERT-6L, TinyBERT-4L, MobileBERT) for binary fault detection across three public datasets: NASA C-MAPSS turbofan degradation, SECOM semiconductor manufacturing, and UCI AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance. We evaluate classification performance (F1-score, AUC), model size, and CPU inference latency, and further assess INT8 dynamic quantization and a two-stage adaptive inference pipeline. Our results reveal that on well-separated sensor data (C-MAPSS), lightweight transformers match traditional ML at 87.8% F1 but at 100x the model size and 9000x the latency. TinyBERT-4L emerges as the most deployment-friendly transformer at 55 MB and 18 ms CPU latency. INT8 quantization reduces size by 25% while preserving 86.9% F1. Our adaptive pipeline, routing 97.9% of predictions through a quantized triage model and only 2.1% to a larger expert, achieves 87.6% F1 at 19.5 ms average latency. On severely imbalanced datasets (SECOM, UCI-PM), both traditional and transformer methods struggle significantly, highlighting fundamental limitations of current approaches for extreme class imbalance in fault detection. All code is publicly available.

</details>


### [75] [Project Ariadne: Prompt-Conditioned Route Generation for Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24184)
*Anton Morgunov,Victor S. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ariadne是一个解码器-only路线生成器，通过提示-补全序列同时表示目标分子、可选约束和合成路线，在多个基准测试上优于或媲美现有方法，且推理时间更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆合成规划方法要么依赖迭代搜索（效率低），要么直接生成路线但对不同规划规格需要训练单独模型，缺乏统一灵活的生成框架。

Method: 提出Ariadne，一个解码器-only的路线生成模型，将目标分子、可选约束（如路线深度、必需起始物料）和完整合成路线编码为一个提示-补全序列，通过自回归方式直接生成整个逆合成路线。

Result: 在RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160基准上，加入深度约束提示使Solv-0提升13.7点，加入必需叶子约束提示提升31.2点。在必需叶子约束下，Ariadne的Top-10和Solv-0优于DESP，且仅用24 GPU-分钟 vs 6.8 GPU-小时。与DMS Explorer XL相比，推理时间约为其一半。在仅目标分子基准上，路线留出重建表现最佳，但AiZynthFinder MCTS在多个Solv-0比较中仍更强。

Conclusion: 序列生成范式可从专家逆合成模型扩展到提示条件化的结构路线生成，但Tier-1-3路线检查器仍是模型实际应用于实验化学家的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Retrosynthetic planning seeks to connect a target molecule to commercially available starting materials through a multistep route. Classical planners construct such routes by iteratively applying single-step reaction models within a search procedure; constrained variants often require specialized algorithms or architectural changes. Direct route generation reframes retrosynthesis as sequence generation, but existing direct-generation methods still train separate models for different planning specifications. We introduce Ariadne, a decoder-only route generator that represents the target, optional constraints, and route in one prompt-completion sequence. On the RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160 benchmark family, one 24-layer checkpoint follows route-depth and required-starting-material prompts: adding the corresponding prompt fields raises Solv-0 by 13.7 points for depth constraints and 31.2 points for required-leaf constraints. Ariadne also improves over DESP, a bidirectional search planner, on required-leaf Top-10 and Solv-0 in 24 GPU-minutes versus 6.8 GPU-hours. On standard reconstruction, Ariadne is comparable to DMS Explorer XL at about half the reported inference time. Across additional target-only benchmarks, Ariadne's clearest gains are on route-holdout reconstruction, whereas AiZynthFinder MCTS remains stronger on several Solv-0 comparisons. These results extend sequence generation from specialist retrosynthesis models to prompt-conditioned structural route generation. We release the codebase and training scripts to support further work, but do not introduce Tier-1--3 route checkers; those remain the main bottleneck before models of this kind can become useful to experimental chemists.

</details>


### [76] [Managing Task Execution for Unknown Workloads in Batteryless IoT: A Hardware-Agnostic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24340)
*Samer Nasser,Henrique Duarte Moura,Ritesh Kumar Singh,Maarten Weyn,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种硬件无关的调度策略——无模型强化学习(RL)和近似预测(AP)——用于无电池能量采集IoT系统，通过物理仿真与真实数据评估，揭示了不同策略在任务吞吐量、生存-执行平衡和能量缺口管理方面的独特权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能量感知调度器依赖静态执行阈值或硬件特定预测量任务配置，难以应对不可预测的工作负载和能量波动，亟需硬件无关的通用动态调度策略。

Method: 提出两种黑盒调度方法：无模型强化学习(RL)代理和即时近似预测(AP)。在基于真实太阳能数据和动态LoRa传输协议构建的物理仿真框架中，与自适应任务速率方法(AsTAR)和优化静态阈值方法进行对比评估。

Result: AP方法提供轻量级、接近oracle的任务吞吐量；RL代理实现可调优的生存-执行平衡；AsTAR在长能量间隙中擅长任务执行节奏控制。大容量缓存设备可使用更简单的静态策略。

Conclusion: 不存在通用最优策略，不同调度方法各有权衡；先进调度策略对小能量缓存约束系统至关重要，而较大能量缓存的设备可依赖更简单的静态策略。

Abstract: In recent years, the Internet of Things (IoT) paradigm has been shifting toward batteryless, energy-harvesting architectures. Sustaining reliable operation in these systems requires intelligent management of highly volatile stored energy. As edge applications grow in complexity, traditional energy-aware schedulers struggle with unpredictable workloads due to their reliance on static execution thresholds or pre-measured, hardware-specific task profiles. To overcome this, we propose two novel, hardware-agnostic dynamic scheduling strategies treating applications as a "black box," requiring no prior energy information: a model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent and an on-the-fly Approximated Prediction (AP) method. We evaluate these methods against an adaptive task rate approach (AsTAR) and optimized static thresholds using a custom-built, physically accurate simulation framework driven by real-world solar data and dynamic LoRa transmission profiles. Rather than claiming universal superiority, our analysis exposes the distinct operational trade-offs of each method: the AP approach delivers lightweight, near-oracle task throughput; the RL agent provides tunable survival-execution balancing; and AsTAR excels at execution pacing across long energy gaps. Finally, we demonstrate that while these advanced strategies provide critical resilience for severely constrained systems with small capacitors, devices with larger energy buffers can efficiently rely on simpler, less computationally expensive static policies.

</details>


### [77] [Parallel Manifold Steering: Efficient Adaptation of Large Associative Memories via Residual Energy Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24396)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H-Res（分层残差引导），通过在不改变Transformer全局平衡或扩展序列长度的前提下，在激活流形上学习状态相关向量场来调制能量景观，从而解决大模型适应新任务时的“可塑性-稳定性”困境。该方法在关联检索任务上比全局权重修改提升26%，并消除了基于提示方法的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大Transformer模型作为密集关联记忆（DAM）工作，通过自注意力机制的高维吸引子动力学检索知识。然而，将这些冻结的记忆系统适应新任务面临根本的“可塑性-稳定性”困境：现有方法要么直接修改突触权重（如LoRA）导致灾难性干扰，要么用静态提示令牌堵塞检索缓冲区（如VPT）降低关联容量。

Method: H-Res将适应问题建模为激活流形上的控制问题，学习一个状态相关的向量场，将令牌轨迹引导到特定任务的吸引盆中。该方法在保持基础模型注意力熵的同时，通过分层残差引导调制能量景观，而不改变全局平衡或扩展序列长度。

Result: 实验证明，流形引导方法在关联检索任务上比全局权重修改（如LoRA）提升26%，消除了基于提示方法的计算开销，并能有效扩展到结构化领域（如VTAB）。

Conclusion: H-Res形式化地证明了该方法能保持基础模型的注意力熵并促进神经坍缩，为解决大模型适应中的可塑性-稳定性困境提供了一种高效、可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Large Transformer models function as Dense Associative Memories (DAMs), retrieving knowledge via high-dimensional attractor dynamics driven by the self-attention mechanism \citep{ramsauer2020hopfield, wu2024attention}. However, adapting these frozen memory systems to new tasks presents a fundamental ``Plasticity-Stability'' dilemma. Current methods either risk catastrophic interference by modifying synaptic weights directly (e.g., LoRA) \citep{hu2021lora} or degrade associative capacity by clogging the retrieval buffer with static prompt tokens (e.g., VPT) \citep{jia2022vpt}. In this work, we propose \textbf{H-Res} (Hierarchical Residual Steering), a mechanism that modulates the effective energy landscape of the Transformer without altering its global equilibrium or expanding its sequence length. By formulating adaptation as a control problem on the activation manifold \citep{chen2018neuralode}, H-Res learns a state-dependent vector field that steers token trajectories into task-specific basins of attraction. We formally prove that H-Res preserves the attention entropy of the foundation model and facilitates Neural Collapse \citep{papyan2020prevalence}. Empirically, Manifold Steering outperforms global weight modification by 26\% on associative retrieval tasks and eliminates the computational overhead of prompt-based methods, scaling effectively to structured domains \citep{zha2023vtab}.

</details>


### [78] [Natural Identifiers for Privacy and Data Audits in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24408)
*Lorenzo Rossi,Bartłomiej Marek,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自然标识符（NIDs）——即训练数据中天然存在的结构化随机字符串（如加密哈希、短链接），无需重新训练即可实现已训练模型的差分隐私事后审计，并能在无需私有非成员保留数据集的情况下进行数据集推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM隐私审计方法存在两大局限：一是需要插入特制的金丝雀数据并重新训练模型，无法审计已训练模型；二是数据集推断需要难以获取的同分布私有非成员保留数据集。这些限制使得大规模事后审计变得不可行。

Method: 利用LLM训练数据中天然存在的结构化随机字符串（NIDs，如加密哈希和缩短URL），其格式特性允许生成无限数量的同分布随机字符串，作为替代金丝雀用于差分隐私审计，并作为同分布保留数据用于数据集推断。

Result: 实验验证表明，使用NIDs能够在不重新训练的情况下实现已训练模型的差分隐私事后审计，并能对包含NIDs的任何嫌疑数据集进行数据集推断，无需私有非成员保留数据集。

Conclusion: NIDs为LLM隐私审计提供了一种新颖、可扩展的解决方案，克服了现有方法对重新训练和私有保留数据集的依赖，使得大规模事后审计成为可能。

Abstract: Assessing the privacy of large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges. In particular, most existing methods for auditing differential privacy require the insertion of specially crafted canary data during training, making them impractical for auditing already-trained models without costly retraining. Additionally, dataset inference, which audits whether a suspect dataset was used to train a model, is infeasible without access to a private non-member held-out dataset. Yet, such held-out datasets are often unavailable or difficult to construct for real-world cases since they have to be from the same distribution (IID) as the suspect data. These limitations severely hinder the ability to conduct scalable, post-hoc audits. To enable such audits, this work introduces natural identifiers (NIDs) as a novel solution to the above-mentioned challenges. NIDs are structured random strings, such as cryptographic hashes and shortened URLs, naturally occurring in common LLM training datasets. Their format enables the generation of unlimited additional random strings from the same distribution, which can act as alternative canaries for audits and as same-distribution held-out data for dataset inference. Our evaluation highlights that indeed, using NIDs, we can facilitate post-hoc differential privacy auditing without any retraining and enable dataset inference for any suspect dataset containing NIDs without the need for a private non-member held-out dataset.

</details>


### [79] [Data Augmentation: A Fourier Analysis Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24418)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于傅里叶分析和有限群表示论的理论框架，证明在广泛的学习问题中，基于随机采样子集的部分数据增强可以达到与完全增强相同的最优极小化风险率，同时证明严格子集无法实现精确不变性约束。研究为部分增强在计算与统计之间的权衡提供了统一理论视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强是一种简单且模型无关的利用已知不变性的方法，但当群规模较大时，完全增强的计算成本难以承受。核心问题是：部分数据增强能否在泛化和样本复杂度上达到与完全增强相同的统计收益？

Method: 利用有限群的傅里叶分析和表示论，构建了分析部分数据增强统计性质的一般性理论框架；通过随机采样子集进行部分增强，并与完全增强进行极小化风险率比较。

Result: 部分数据增强（基于随机采样子集）可达到与完全增强相同的最优极小化风险率，近似误差随子集规模增大而消失；同时证明了精确不变性需要整个群的平均，严格子集无法实现。

Conclusion: 部分增强可以在计算可行的情况下保留完全增强的统计收益，为学习对称性中统计最优性与计算可扩展性之间的矛盾提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a simple and model-agnostic approach for exploiting known invariances in learning problems. Given a group acting on the input space, one augments the training set with transformed copies of each sample. Because it exploits symmetries without modifying the underlying learning algorithm, data augmentation can be applied broadly across learning methods. However, this universality comes at a computational cost: when the group is large, full group-sized augmentation quickly becomes computationally infeasible. This raises a fundamental question: Can partial data augmentation achieve the same statistical benefits as full augmentation in terms of generalization and sample complexity? We develop a general framework for investigating this question using Fourier analysis and the representation theory of finite groups. We show that, for a broad class of classical learning problems, partial data augmentation based on a randomly sampled subset of group elements achieves the same minimax rates as full augmentation, up to an approximation error that vanishes as the subset size increases. Our results provide a theoretical explanation for why partial augmentation can retain the statistical benefits of full augmentation despite enforcing symmetry only approximately, and shed light on a recently raised question in learning with symmetries: whether statistically optimal learning under general group invariances can be achieved using computationally scalable methods. Moreover, we prove a complementary impossibility result: enforcing exact invariance via data augmentation requires averaging over the entire group, and cannot be achieved by any strict subset when the hypothesis space is sufficiently expressive. Together, these results provide a unified perspective on full and partial data augmentation, as well as exact and approximate symmetry enforcement.

</details>


### [80] [An LLM-based Two-Stage Transformer Framework for Cross-Domain Bearing Fault Diagnosis with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24459)
*Jinghan Wang,Feng Cheng,Wentao Wu,Hang Li,Gaoliang Peng,Tianchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架，采用轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer处理振动信号，通过原型嵌入和分类器自适应机制，在仅需10%标注目标数据的情况下实现92.61%的平均故障诊断准确率，比现有最优方法提升17.24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轴承故障诊断方法在面对数据集异质性、工况变化和有限标注数据同时出现时效果不佳，且现有方法孤立处理这些问题并依赖隐式特征对齐，限制了在并发挑战下的有效性。

Method: 提出知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架：(1) 采用轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer（带因果自注意力）从振动信号中分层提取特征；(2) 多源预训练获得通用表征，预训练编码器权重和故障原型嵌入作为知识载体；(3) 原型知识调制实现目标域适配；(4) 分类器自适应实现跨异质故障类别的无缝迁移。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，仅用10%标注目标数据即达到92.61%平均准确率，超越现有最优方法17.24个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业4.0环境中经济高效的预测性维护提供了实践路径，有效解决了数据集异质性、工况变化和有限标注数据并发存在的挑战。

Abstract: Bearing fault diagnosis faces critical challenges when dataset heterogeneity, operating condition variations, and limited labeled data occur simultaneously in industrial environments. Existing approaches address these issues in isolation and rely on implicit feature alignment, limiting effectiveness under concurrent challenges. This paper proposes a knowledge-guided two-stage transfer learning framework that employs a lightweight GPT-2-style Transformer with causal self-attention for hierarchical feature extraction from vibration signals, establishing explicit pathways where pre-trained encoder weights and fault prototype embeddings serve as knowledge carriers from multi-source pre-training to target adaptation. The framework addresses the dual-shift challenge through multi-source learning for generalizable representations, prototype-based knowledge modulation for target adaptation, and taxonomy-adaptive classification for seamless transfer across heterogeneous fault categories. Experimental validation on four real-world datasets demonstrates 92.61% average accuracy with only 10% labeled target data, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 17.24 percentage points, establishing a practical pathway toward cost-effective predictive maintenance in Industry 4.0 applications.

</details>


### [81] [A Fair Evaluation of Graph Foundation Models for Node Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24509)
*Oleg Platonov,Gleb Bazhenov,Dmitry Eremeev,Liudmila Prokhorenkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对9种面向节点属性预测的图基础模型(GFM)进行了公平且严格的重新评估，发现只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新GFM在预测性能上超越了调优后的GNN基线，但推理成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型(GFM)在节点属性预测任务上缺乏统一的评估标准，不同工作采用差异化的评估方式，导致无法可靠比较各类GFM及与其他模型之间的性能。

Method: 对9种近期提出的节点属性预测GFM进行公平且严格的重新评估，并与强图神经网络(GNN)基线进行对比。

Result: 在这些GFM中，只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新模型在预测性能上优于调优后的GNN，但推理成本更高。

Conclusion: 当前大多数GFM在节点属性预测任务上并未超越调优后的GNN基线，GFM领域仍需更统一的评估标准和更高效的方法。

Abstract: Due to the wide use of graph-structured data in different fields of industry and science, the development of Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) has recently attracted a lot of attention. While many different types of models are called GFMs, particular interest has been paid to GFMs designed for node property prediction tasks, which is one of the most popular settings in Graph ML with lots of real-world applications from fraud detection in financial and social networks to recommendation systems for e-commerce and user-generated content platforms. While a number of GFMs for this task have been recently proposed, the field has not converged to a unified evaluation setting, and different works evaluate their models in widely different ways, preventing reliable comparison of GFMs with each other and with other types of models. In this work, we conduct a fair and rigorous reevaluation of 9 recent GFMs for node property prediction, comparing them to strong Graph Neural Network (GNN) baselines. We find that, among these GFMs, only the most recent ones based on the Prior-data Fitted Networks paradigm outperform well-tuned GNNs in predictive performance, although at a higher inference cost.

</details>


### [82] [Reasoning as Attractor Dynamics: Latent Memory Retrieval via Gibbs-Weighted Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24543)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从集体计算视角重新审视LLM，将其视为高维稠密联想记忆模型，提出基于吉布斯测度和谱熵的检索机制，通过多路径采样和能量加权近似平衡分布，使推理过程如同系统"松弛"至吸引子盆地，从而减少幻觉。在Phi-3.5上GSM8K提升5.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将LLM视为自回归生成器，忽略了其内在的集体计算特性。作者试图从物理学的能量景观视角重新建模推理过程，将正确推理链视为平坦吸引子盆地，幻觉视为尖锐不稳定局部极小值，并利用这一几何特性设计更鲁棒的推理机制。

Method: 提出基于吉布斯测度的检索机制：对轨迹的谱熵进行度量，采样多条推理路径，按逆能量加权（P ∝ e^{-βE}），近似联想记忆的平衡分布。通过"松弛"过程使系统落入稳健的吸引子盆地，而非贪心逐词预测。

Result: 在Microsoft Phi-3.5上，GSM8K准确率从84.7%提升至90.1%，提升5.38个百分点。

Conclusion: 推理更适合被建模为系统动态沉降到吸引子盆地的过程，而非贪心的逐词预测。物理启发的推理机制能有效提升数学推理的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are traditionally viewed as autoregressive generators. However, from the perspective of collective computation, they function as high-dimensional Dense Associative Memories that store complex reasoning patterns as latent attractors. In this work, we investigate the energy landscape of mathematical reasoning. We posit that correct reasoning chains correspond to deep, wide attractor basins ("flat minima") in the model's output distribution, whereas hallucinations manifest as sharp, unstable local minima. To exploit this geometry, we introduce a retrieval mechanism based on a Gibbs measure of the trajectory's spectral entropy. By sampling multiple reasoning paths and weighting them by their inverse energy ($P \propto e^{-βE}$), we approximate the equilibrium distribution of the associative memory, effectively ``relaxing'' the system into a robust solution. Empirically, this physics-inspired mechanism improves Microsoft Phi-3.5 performance on GSM8K by 5.38\% (84.7\% $\to$ 90.1\%), demonstrating that inference is better modeled as a dynamic settling process into an attractor basin rather than greedy next-token prediction.

</details>


### [83] [QC-SMOTE: Quality-Controlled SMOTE for Imbalanced Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24625)
*Parth Upman,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QC-SMOTE，一种质量控制过采样框架，通过复合邻域可信度评分评估少数类样本可靠性，并采用IPQ引导的best-of-K策略生成合成样本，在30个不平衡数据集上取得了最优的平均AUC-ROC和Macro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别不平衡导致SMOTE等现有方法在噪声或类重叠区域生成低质量合成样本，影响分类性能。

Method: 构建复合邻域可信度评分（结合局部密度、安全水平和与多数类的隔离度）；采用IPQ引导的best-of-K策略，评估中间点纯度及多数类清除；根据样本可靠性和边界信息量分配生成数量；根据重叠-不平衡程度自适应调整插值范围和选择标准；当邻域纯度低于自适应阈值时用原始少数类副本替换低质量合成样本。

Result: 在30个不平衡数据集上的重复分层交叉验证实验中，QC-SMOTE在所有对比过采样方法中取得了最强的平均AUC-ROC和Macro F1，在中度和严重不平衡场景下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 质量感知和几何自适应的合成采样对于鲁棒的不平衡分类至关重要，QC-SMOTE通过优雅降级机制有效应对噪声区域的过采样退化问题。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a significant challenge in classification, where existing methods such as SMOTE often generate low-quality synthetic samples in regions with noise or class overlap. We propose QC-SMOTE, a quality-controlled oversampling framework that estimates minority sample reliability using a composite neighbourhood trustworthiness score combining local density, safe-level, and isolation from the majority class. Synthetic candidates are generated using an IPQ-guided best-of-K strategy that evaluates midpoint purity and, when required, majority clearance, with allocation guided by sample reliability and boundary informativeness. Generation behaviour adapts across overlap--imbalance regimes, adjusting interpolation range and selection criteria to match local data geometry. Low-quality synthetic samples are replaced with original minority duplicates when neighbourhood purity falls below an adaptive threshold, providing graceful degradation by reverting to duplication in severely noisy regions. Experiments on 30 imbalanced datasets using repeated stratified cross-validation show that QC-SMOTE achieves the strongest average AUC-ROC and Macro F1 among the compared oversampling methods, with particularly clear gains under moderate and severe imbalance. These results demonstrate the importance of quality-aware, geometry-adaptive synthetic sampling for robust imbalanced classification.

</details>


### [84] [FlowPipe: LLM-Enhanced Conditional Generative Flow Networks for Data Preparation Pipeline Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24679)
*Kunyu Ni,Lei Cao,Jie He,Xiaotong Zhang,Jianfeng Jin,Junyu Dong,Yanwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowPipe是一个基于条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）的统一框架，用于自动化机器学习数据预处理pipeline的构建。它通过轨迹平衡目标、基于FiLM的深度语义调制（注入LLM先验知识）以及失败感知机制，有效解决了现有方法在长程信用分配、数据集上下文注入和无效状态探索方面的三大局限。在74个真实数据集上，FlowPipe相比SOTA方法平均提升11.96%的准确率，并实现12.5倍更快的训练收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据预处理pipeline的自动构建方法（如Multi-DQN）面临三个关键局限：解耦的值估计器削弱长程信用分配、数据集上下文仅被弱注入策略、以及在稀疏搜索空间（存在大量无效状态）中探索效率低下。

Method: FlowPipe将有向无环图上的pipeline合成建模为条件概率流生成问题。核心方法包括：(1) 使用条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）并采用轨迹平衡（Trajectory Balance）目标，将终端验证奖励与早期pipeline决策关联；(2) 通过特征向线性调制（FiLM）引入深度语义调制（Deep Semantic Modulation），使LLM导出的逻辑先验根据数据集语义调节策略的内部激活；(3) 将失败感知（failure awareness）纳入流目标，避免无效状态并聚焦高潜力搜索区域。

Result: 在两个基准套件（包含74个真实数据集）上的实验表明，FlowPipe优于SOTA基线，平均准确率提升11.96%，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。

Conclusion: FlowPipe通过统一的条件生成流网络框架，有效解决了自动化pipeline合成中的三大关键挑战，显著提升了性能与训练效率。代码已开源。

Abstract: Data preparation pipelines improve data quality in machine learning by transforming raw tables into learning-ready data through sequential cleaning and feature transformation operators. However, automatically constructing such pipelines is computationally difficult because operator sequences are combinatorial and end-to-end evaluation is expensive. Existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) Multi-DQN methods still face three key limitations: decoupled value estimators weaken long-horizon credit assignment, dataset context is only weakly injected into the policy, and exploration is inefficient in a sparse search space with many invalid states. To address these issues, we propose FlowPipe, a unified framework that formulates pipeline synthesis as conditional probabilistic flow generation over a directed acyclic graph. FlowPipe uses Conditional Generative Flow Networks (C-GFlowNets) with a Trajectory Balance objective to connect terminal validation rewards with early pipeline decisions. It further introduces Deep Semantic Modulation through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), allowing LLM-derived logical priors to condition the policy's internal activations according to dataset semantics. In addition, FlowPipe incorporates failure awareness into the flow objective to avoid invalid states and concentrate search on high-potential regions. Experiments on two benchmark suites with 74 real-world datasets show that FlowPipe outperforms SOTA baselines, improving accuracy by 11.96% on average and achieving 12.5x faster training convergence. Source code is available at https://github.com/KunyuNi/FlowPipe.

</details>


### [85] [Grad Detect: Gradient-Based Hallucination Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24790)
*Anand Kamat,Daniel Blake,Brent M. Werness*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Grad Detect，一种基于梯度分析的方法，通过在推理时进行一次前向-反向传播，利用层间梯度模式来检测LLM的幻觉和预测模型弃权，在多个Q&A基准上优于置信度和采样基线。层消融实验发现最后5层集中了97%以上的判别梯度信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成内容时容易产生幻觉，现有检测方法主要依赖输出级信号（如置信度、采样一致性），但忽略了模型内部梯度结构中蕴含的丰富信息。需要一种能利用内部信号进行幻觉检测的统一框架。

Method: Grad Detect通过对推理时的单次前向-反向传播，提取每一层的梯度模式作为特征，分析其与输出正确性的关联。在11个模型、4种架构家族上进行全面的层消融实验以定位关键梯度信号层。

Result: 在多个Q&A基准的幻觉检测和模型弃权预测任务上，Grad Detect一致优于基于置信度和基于采样的基线方法。层消融实验表明最后5层集中了超过97%的判别性梯度信号，可大幅降低计算开销。

Conclusion: Grad Detect提供了预测LLM可靠性的统一框架，兼具强预测性能和可解释性，能够揭示模型失败的位置和原因。通过仅关注最后几层可实现高效部署，适合高可靠性要求的应用场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet they remain prone to generating hallucinations. Detecting these hallucinations is critical for deploying LLMs reliably in high-stakes applications. We present Grad Detect, a gradient-based approach for predicting hallucinations by analyzing layer-wise gradient patterns from a single forward-backward pass during inference. Our method shows that the internal gradient structure of a model carries rich information about the correctness of its output. This information is not accessible through output-level signals alone. We evaluate Grad Detect on several Q&A benchmarks across both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction, where it consistently outperforms confidence-based and sampling-based baselines. Through comprehensive layer ablation studies across all eleven models from four architectural families, we find that the final five layers concentrate over 97% of the discriminative gradient signal, enabling efficient deployment with minimal performance loss. Grad Detect provides a unified framework for predicting multiple dimensions of LLM reliability, offering strong predictive performance alongside interpretable insights into where and how model failures originate.

</details>


### [86] [Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24851)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hartley Neural Operator (HNO)，即傅里叶神经算子 (FNO) 的纯实数镜像，用离散 Hartley 变换替代 FFT。理论分析和实验表明：对于自伴椭圆算子（如 Poisson、biharmonic），HNO 因 Green 函数为实对称而占优；对于含相位的时间依赖算子（如波动、对流、Navier-Stokes），FNO 因复数乘法能表示相位而占优。结论是应依据算子的对称性匹配谱基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO 使用复数 FFT 处理实值 PDE 解存在共轭对称带来的表征冗余，作者希望用纯实数变换消除冗余，并探究不同 PDE 算子应匹配何种谱基。

Method: 将 FNO 中的 FFT 替换为纯实数的离散 Hartley 变换 (DHT)，每个保留谱模式只学习一个实数权重，无复数运算。HNO 与 FNO 等参数，区别仅在谱基。

Result: 椭圆算子（Poisson、biharmonic）上 HNO 优于 FNO；含相位的时间依赖算子（波动、对流、Burgers、Navier-Stokes）上 FNO 优于 HNO；热方程（无相位）为临界情况。该分裂单调于算子相位含量。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优算子，应基于 PDE 算子的对称性（Green 函数实对称 vs 含相位）选择相应的谱基（DHT vs FFT）。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.

</details>


### [87] [When Do Conservation Laws Survive Learned Representations? Certified Horizons for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24945)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究物理世界模型中的表征学习问题：提出了一种对解码后的物理不变量进行认证的框架，通过"壳-视界证书"分解表征、读出和潜动力缺陷，证明在状态、学习提升和像素观测下守恒定律的可认证性，并发现硬规范辛结构在已知相坐标中表现最佳但无法跨学习坐标系，而受控Lipschitz对齐的软不变量在表征学习设置中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究物理世界模型中守恒定律在表征学习后是否仍可被认证，以及不同几何先验对表征学习鲁棒性的影响。

Method: 提出"壳-视界证书"框架，将认证预算分解为表征缺陷、读出缺陷和潜动力学缺陷，通过单调对齐桥接软学习见证与解码不变量的认证视界，并在状态、学习提升和像素观测三种观测设置下进行实验验证。

Result: 守恒证书可以在表征学习中存活，但不同几何先验的生存能力不同：硬规范辛结构在已知相坐标中拥有最长视界但无法跨学习坐标系，受控Lipschitz对齐的软不变量在表征学习设置中有效，像素认证可在读出稳定的子管上恢复，开普勒问题暴露了几何边界。

Conclusion: 核心对象不是潜哈密顿量，而是解码后的物理不变量，其对表征学习的鲁棒性可以被度量、认证和证伪。

Abstract: We ask a representation-learning question about physical world models: when does a conservation law remain certifiable after a model learns a latent representation? A certified horizon bounds -- in advance, from measurable model defects -- how many steps a rollout provably stays on a physical invariant's level set. The key design choice is what is certified: not a learned latent Hamiltonian or a learned scalar witness (a model can conserve either while drifting in true energy), but the decoded physical invariant obtained by decoding the latent state and evaluating the known invariant. Around this object we derive shell-horizon certificates whose budget decomposes into representation, readout, and latent-dynamics defects, with a monotone alignment bridge through which a soft learned witness yields a certified horizon for the decoded invariant, and test them across state, learned-lift, and pixel observations on conservative systems. Conservation certificates can survive learned representation, but not all geometric priors survive equally: hard canonical symplectic structure yields the longest horizons in known phase coordinates yet does not cross a learned chart, whereas a controlled-Lipschitz-aligned soft invariant survives in the learned-representation settings we test; pixel certification is recovered on a readout-stable sub-tube; and the Kepler problem exposes a geometric boundary. The central object is therefore not a latent Hamiltonian, but a decoded physical invariant whose robustness to representation learning can be measured, certified, and falsified.

</details>


### [88] [Conformal Orbit-Valid Trust Horizons for Equivariant World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24946)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于群对称性的隐空间世界模型信任视界认证方法，利用分裂共形预测校准原始视界曲线，并证明了精确等变性可沿群轨道传输已校准的信任视界曲线。实验表明该方法在对称2D和3D环境下均能产生保守、无反保守违规的认证结果，且等变模型在非等变基线失效时仍能保持有效认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的世界模型仅在 rollout 误差可控的时间范围内有用，因此需要一种方法来确定模型可被信任的视界（horizon）。现有的信任认证方法缺乏对对称性结构的利用，本文旨在利用已知的群对称性来提升认证的效率和可靠性。

Method: 1) 对隐空间世界模型的一步残差和有限时间展开估计形成原始视界曲线；2) 使用分裂共形预测（split-conformal）乘以一个乘性因子进行校准；3) 理论证明精确等变性（exact equivariance）可将已校准的信任视界曲线沿群轨道传输；4) 在2D和3D环境下进行实证验证。

Result: 可复现审计集上共形因子γ_α=1.0（原始认证已保守）；50次稳定审计零反保守违规（95%上限5.8%）；等变模型轨道传输残差中位数1.1%、最大4.1%；认证非空洞（中位认证/测量视界比0.67）。2D对称基底上等变、普通、增强模型均轨道有效；3D偏航审计中等变模型单扇区安全有效，非等变基线需多扇区或产生违规。

Conclusion: 基于群对称性的共形认证方法能产生保守且非空洞的信任视界认证，等变性保证了认证结果沿轨道的一致性。该方法是一种保守的分布审计而非全局可达性保证，且当前3D CEM-MPC行为层中认证引导的子目标间距未得到确认。

Abstract: Learned world models are useful only over horizons on which their rollout error remains controlled. We study trust-horizon certification for latent world models with known group symmetries. Given a one-step latent residual and a finite-time expansion estimate, we form a raw horizon curve and calibrate it with a split-conformal multiplicative factor. On the reproducible audit set, the conformal factor is $γ_α=1.0$: the raw certificate is already conservative under the audit protocol. Across 50 stable audits, we observe zero anti-conservative violations, corresponding to an exact-binomial 95% upper bound of 5.8% on the violation rate. Our main structural result is that exact equivariance transports a calibrated trust-horizon curve over the group orbit: when the environment dynamics, encoder, predictor, action transform, and latent metric satisfy the stated equivariance/invariance conditions, rollout errors and trust horizons are orbit-constant. Empirically, the implemented models exhibit small orbit-transport residuals, with median 1.1% and maximum 4.1% over 14 orbit audits. The certificate is also non-vacuous (median certified-to-measured horizon ratio 0.67). A certificate-level calibration-cost study shows two complementary regimes. On a symmetric 2D substrate, equivariant, plain, and augmented models are all orbit-valid from a single calibration sector -- no separation, because the substrate already makes non-equivariant baselines approximately orbit-robust. A 3D yaw audit shows the other regime: the equivariant model obtains a one-sector safe and non-vacuous orbit-valid certificate, while healthy non-equivariant baselines pay violation, slack, sharpness, or additional-sector cost. The certificate is a conservative, distributional audit rather than a global reachability guarantee, and certificate-guided subgoal spacing is not confirmed in the current 3D CEM-MPC behavior layer.

</details>


### [89] [Swarm-Inspired Generation of Collective Behaviors in Graph Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24958)
*Ji Chen,Song Chen,Chengzhang Gong,Li Fan,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SIES的图动力学框架，通过可学习的带符号局部交互规则，实现了可控集体行为的泛化，在同步控制、机器人协调和异配图表示学习三个任务上均取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体行为研究主要聚焦于解释涌现现象，缺乏能够设计通用局部交互规则以在跨图结构、动力学和任务上产生期望全局组织的方法。

Method: SIES将每个节点视为带状态和任务线索的智能体单元，引入源-目标-条件加权的带符号注意力作为显式演化模型中的自适应耦合项，将显式动力学引擎与局部智能体智能相结合。

Result: 在同步控制中，SIES无需重训练即可泛化到未训练的网络规模、目标相位关系和内在节点动力学；比三种振荡器基线更快达到步态模式；泛化到不同规模的多足机器人和物理六足机器人。在图表示学习中，在异配节点分类基准上达到最高性能。

Conclusion: SIES是一种通用可学习的图动力学交互框架，在同步控制、自适应机器人协调和异配图表示学习方面具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Collective behavior arises when locally interacting units produce coordinated global organization, from synchronization in dynamical systems to task-relevant information flow on graphs. The central challenge is not only to explain how collective behavior emerges, but to design local interaction rules that can produce desired global organization and generalize across graphs, dynamics and tasks.To address this challenge, we introduce the Swarm-Inspired Emergent Synchronizer (SIES), a graph-dynamical framework that learns generalizable local-interaction laws for controllable collective organization. Each node is an agent-like dynamical unit with a state and task cue, and signed source-target-conditioned attention acts as an adaptive coupling term inside an explicit evolution model. Therefore, SIES combines an explicit dynamical engine with local agent intelligence, similar to biological swarms. For synchronization control, SIES learns a generalizable coupling operator that produces prescribed synchronization patterns for CDSs across untrained network scales, target phase relations, and intrinsic node dynamics without retraining. The learned operator also reaches gait-related modes faster than three oscillator baselines and generalizes synchronization-driven locomotion to simulated multi-legged robots of different scales and a physical hexapod after leg disablement. For graph representation learning, SIES applies the same signed interaction principle to message passing and achieves the highest performance among the compared methods on heterophilous node-classification benchmarks. Together, these results position SIES as a generalizable and learnable graph-dynamical interaction framework with promise for synchronization control, adaptive robot coordination, and heterophilous graph representation learning.

</details>


### [90] [Memory-Efficient Policy Libraries with Low-Rank Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25700)
*Samuel Valland Lyngset,Tor Viljen Raanaas,Gard Sveipe,Eirik Møller Nilsen,Jim Torresen,Kai Olav Ellefsen,Tobias Lømo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了将参数高效微调方法LoRA应用于机器人强化学习中的多任务策略库构建，实验表明相比全参数微调，LoRA可节省90-95%存储空间（内存减少20-160倍），且在选定任务上成功率无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证参数高效微调（PEFT）方法（如LoRA）能否从大语言模型领域迁移到机器人强化学习领域，以降低多任务策略库的内存占用和计算开销。

Method: 基于PPO算法，使用LoRA对基线模型进行多任务微调，构建专家策略库，并与全参数微调进行对比实验。

Result: LoRA在超参数调优下可将内存占用减少20-160倍；部署10-50个专业策略时节省90-95%存储空间；在选定任务上成功率与全参数微调无显著差异。

Conclusion: LoRA可有效迁移至机器人RL领域，在几乎不损失任务成功率的前提下大幅降低多策略库的存储需求，是实际机器人应用中存储整个策略库的关键技术。

Abstract: When fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs), there has been success in minimizing both memory usage and computation with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT), like Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA). In this article, we have explored whether this approach is transferable to the world of robotics and Reinforcement Learning (RL), allowing learning with reduced memory usage and improved computational performance. Specifically, we focused on a version of multi-task robotics, where a library of specialist policies are created. In such a library memory efficiency is especially important. We used a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm and fine-tuned a baseline model to different tasks using LoRA. Our results demonstrate that, depending on the hyperparameters, LoRA can minimize memory usage by a factor of 20-160 compared to full fine-tuning of all layers. This implies a 90-95% storage saving when deploying a library of many (10-50) specialized policies, which can be the differentiating factor between being able to store the entire library in memory or having to use swap-memory in an applied robotics setting. At the same time, our results indicate that there is no significant difference in the success-rate between full fine-tuning and LoRA fine-tuning for the selected tasks.

</details>


### [91] [ROAD-VLA: Robust Online Adaptation via Self-Distillation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25800)
*Kejing Wang,Toan Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Simon Khan,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ROAD-VLA框架，通过优势引导的自蒸馏方法在动作空间构建近端教师，将稀疏奖励转化为稠密令牌级监督，在7个机器人操作环境中优于PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在线适应困难，稀疏奖励对高维自回归动作策略提供弱监督，且基于文本的教师模型存在模态鸿沟，无法有效指导VLA适应。

Method: 通过扰动动作令牌logits并加入校准后的优势估计，在动作空间中构建近端教师，实现优势引导的自蒸馏，推导策略改进下界。

Result: 在7个机器人操作环境的分布内和分布外场景下，ROAD-VLA在几乎所有设置中优于PPO。

Conclusion: ROAD-VLA通过动作空间中的近端教师和优势引导自蒸馏，有效解决了VLA模型在线适应中的稀疏奖励和模态鸿沟问题。

Abstract: Effective online adaptation of vision-language-action (VLA) models remains challenging, as sparse rewards provide weak supervision for high-dimensional autoregressive action policies. Although self-distillation can in principle provide denser training signals, we find that text-based privileged teachers conditioned on demonstrations, retrieved experiences, or high-level plans are ineffective for VLA adaptation, exposing a modality gap between symbolic guidance and low-level robot actions. We propose ROAD-VLA, an advantage-guided self-distillation framework that constructs a proximal teacher directly in action space by perturbing action-token logits with calibrated advantage estimates. This converts sparse rewards into dense token-level supervision while keeping the teacher close to the current policy. We further derive a policy-improvement lower bound under calibrated advantages and accurate teacher matching. Across seven robotic manipulation environments with in-distribution and out-of-distribution shifts, ROADVLA outperforms PPO in nearly all settings, demonstrating robust online VLA adaptation.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Proactive Systems in HCI and AI: Concepts, Challenges, and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25149)
*Nima Zargham,Sharon Ferguson,Jaisie Sin,Cosmin Munteanu,Anastasia Kuzminykh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文指出现有主动系统（Proactive Systems）概念模糊、定义不一致，导致系统设计、比较和评估困难，且现有方法局限于被动交互范式。通过多学科研讨会，旨在建立清晰的概念框架、识别设计评估方法局限，并共同制定以人为中心的指导方针和研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动系统（如智能照明、辅助机器人、智能恒温器等）日益受到关注，但"主动性"概念定义松散且应用不一致，简单提醒与复杂主动行为被混为一谈，阻碍了系统的系统化设计、比较与评估。

Method: 本文为研讨会概述，采用跨学科（人机交互、人工智能及相关领域）的互动讨论与协作活动，旨在形成共享概念化、识别设计评估差距，并共同制定以人为中心的指导方针。

Result: 目前尚无具体实验结果，但预期成果包括：（1）对主动性的共享概念化；（2）当前设计与评估方法的局限与缺口；（3）面向未来主动系统的以人为中心的设计指南与研究路线图。

Conclusion: 主动系统研究亟需建立清晰、严谨的概念基础和方法框架，以应对时机、适宜性、用户控制、透明度和信任等独特挑战，推动主动技术的鲁棒与一致性设计评估。

Abstract: The last few years have seen a significant rise in interest in highly autonomous and proactive systems, fueled by advances in AI. Systems that anticipate user needs, take initiative, and act without explicit user input. Such systems span a wide range of applications, from smart lighting that adapts to user activity to assistive robots that plan actions in advance to intelligent thermostats that learn routines and adjust environments proactively. Despite this breadth, the concept of proactivity remains loosely defined and inconsistently applied across research and practice. Current usage of the term often conflates fundamentally different system behaviors. For instance, simple reminders or recommendation systems are frequently labeled as proactive, even though underlying mechanisms and intentions differ significantly. This conceptual ambiguity limits our ability to systematically design, compare, and evaluate proactive systems. Moreover, existing methodologies for design and evaluation are largely rooted in reactive interaction paradigms, failing to address the unique challenges posed by proactive behavior, including timing, appropriateness, user control, transparency, and trust. This multidisciplinary workshop aims to establish a clearer and more rigorous foundation for understanding proactive systems. We bring together researchers and practitioners from Human-Computer Interaction, AI, and related fields to (1) develop a shared conceptualization of proactivity, (2) identify gaps and limitations in current design and evaluation approaches, and (3) co-create human-centered guidelines and research directions for future systems. Through interactive discussions and collaborative activities, the workshop seeks to map key challenges and opportunities, ultimately advancing robust and consistent frameworks for designing and evaluating proactive technologies.

</details>


### [93] [ARTOO-DARTU: Studying AR-HRC With AR Obstruction Mitigation During a Warehouse Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25202)
*Christian Fronk,Hanting Ye,Zhehan Qu,Maria Gorlatova*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出ARTOO-DARTU系统，通过增强现实（AR）情景分析结合遮挡检测与缓解管线（ODM），在仓库人机协作场景中实现实时机器人状态反馈与控制，同时保持真实世界可见性。34人用户研究表明，ODM激活时任务效率提升46%，真实世界可见性相关子任务速度提升61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协作中需要向用户传达机器人意图和内部状态以提高任务效率和安全性。然而，AR情景分析可能遮挡重要真实世界元素，尤其在移动机器人动态运动的仓库场景中，存在安全性和可用性风险。

Method: 提出ARTOO-DARTU系统，包含遮挡检测与缓解管线（ODM），专为AR-HRC场景设计；开发Pocket MonstARs游戏化抽象任务作为评估平台，34名参与者在有无ODM条件下进行对比实验。

Result: ODM激活时：整体HRC任务效率提升46%；需真实世界可见性的子任务速度提升61%。

Conclusion: AR情景分析与ODM相结合可显著提升AR-HRC仓库场景的效率和用户体验，遮挡缓解是AR-HRC系统设计中不可或缺的关键组件。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) often requires robot intentions and internal states to be conveyed to users for task efficiency and safety. Recently, augmented reality (AR) situated analytics provide such real-time robot feedback in HRC contexts. However, AR situated analytics can obstruct important real-world elements, posing safety and usability risks, especially when content is dynamically positioned relative to movements of mobile robots in a warehouse HRC scenario. In this paper, we introduce the Augmented Reality Technique Of Obstruction Deterrence while Aiding Robotic Teaming for Users (ARTOO-DARTU), an AR system tailored specifically for warehouse HRC that enables real-time robot situated analytics and control while preserving visibility of the real world through an obstruction detection and mitigation pipeline (ODM) that is uniquely suited for AR-HRC. To evaluate ARTOO-DARTU, we developed Pocket MonstARs, a controlled gamified abstraction of HRC warehouse inventory picking in which virtual monsters serve as proxies for pick targets, while labeled and object-marked boxes preserve the real-world identification demands of the picking task. In a 34-participant user study, we found that our designed AR situated analytics yielded a 46% increase in efficiency on the overall HRC task, but only when the ODM was active. Participants with the ODM active were also 61% faster on subtasks requiring visibility of the real world. Our findings demonstrate that, when paired with our developed ODM to prevent real-world obstructions, the situated analytics in ARTOO-DARTU can significantly enhance efficiency and user experience in AR-HRC warehouse scenarios.

</details>


### [94] [FUTO Swipe: Layout-Agnostic Neural Swipe Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25247)
*David Lee Miller,Aleksandras Kostarevas*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与键盘布局无关的神经滑动输入解码器，通过编码器预测用户意图指向的字符位置，在推理时由键盘布局映射为各键逻辑值，并发布了包含100万+滑动轨迹的公开数据集。模型通过几何增强泛化到训练中未见的布局，结合了算法解码器的布局灵活性与神经模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经滑动解码器通常与训练时的键盘布局绑定，更换布局需要重新采集数据和训练模型，缺乏布局通用性。

Method: 编码器沿滑动轨迹预测用户是否指示某字符及其在键盘上的位置，键盘布局在推理时提供而非训练时学习；对滑动轨迹和键盘布局施加几何增强，迫使模型基于手势特征而非训练布局进行预测。

Result: 模型能泛化到训练中未见的布局，某些情况下准确率甚至超过训练布局；发布了包含100万+滑动轨迹的公开数据集swipe.futo.org。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了布局灵活性与神经模型准确性的结合，训练好的模型已公开。

Abstract: Neural swipe decoders are typically tied to the keyboard they were trained on, requiring a new corpus and training run for each layout. In this report, we document our approach toward training models that can function on any contiguous mobile keyboard layout. At each point along the swipe, our encoder predicts whether the user is indicating a character and where on the keyboard that character lies. The keyboard layout is supplied at inference time and used to map the spatial and temporal prediction to a logit at each key, rather than being learned during training.
  Training neural models requires substantial data, but public swipe data is limited, particularly for non-QWERTY layouts. We release swipe.futo.org, the largest MIT-licensed swipe corpus we are aware of, containing over 1M donated swipes from more than 12k donor sessions. To generalize beyond the English QWERTY layout, we apply geometric augmentations to both the swipe trajectory and the keyboard layout at every training step, forcing the model to make predictions based on characteristics of the swipe gesture rather than the training layout. The model generalizes to layouts absent from training, in some cases more accurately than the layout it was trained on. This combines the layout-flexibility of an algorithmic decoder with the accuracy of a neural model. Trained models are publicly available.

</details>


### [95] [Co-designing a Preliminary Repository of Augmented Reality Concepts for Real-Time Emotion Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25271)
*Graciela Camacho-Fidalgo,Edgar Rojas-Muñoz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过两阶段参与式设计（40名焦虑倾向用户+10名心理健康专家），创建了一个包含8个主题集群和106个设计思路的AR设计库，用于支持实时情绪调节，填补了AR心理健康领域缺乏用户中心、专家知情的可复用设计资源的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AR技术支持心理健康的方法存在，但缺乏以用户为中心、专家知情的理解，即如何设计实时AR方案来支持情绪困扰中的人们同时不打断他们的日常活动。这种可复用设计资源的缺失阻碍了AR在心理健康支持领域的采纳。

Method: 采用两阶段参与式设计流程：第一阶段招募40名焦虑倾向个体，使用名义小组技术列出AR支持情绪调节的想法；第二阶段招募10名心理健康专业人员将这些想法组织成主题集群并评估临床可行性。

Result: 生成了一个基于用户视角和临床专业知识的AR设计库，包含8个主题集群和106个设计思路。

Conclusion: 这项工作是朝着开发无缝实时AR心理健康干预措施迈出的第一步。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) can be a positive therapeutic approach to support mental health and emotion regulation. Although AR techniques for therapeutic support exist, there is no user-centered, expert-informed understanding of how real-time AR designs can support people in emotional distress without disengaging them from their ongoing activities. This lack of reusable design resources hinders the adoption of AR for mental health support. This paper addresses this gap by introducing a co-designed collection of AR interventions describing how this technique can support real-time emotion regulation. The repository was created following a two-phase participatory design process. Phase 1 recruited 40 anxiety-prone individuals and used the Nominal Group Technique to list ideas on how AR affordances could support emotion regulation. Phase 2 recruited 10 mental health professionals to organize these ideas into thematic clusters and assess their clinical feasibility. The resulting AR design repository, grounded in user perspective and clinical expertise, identifies eight thematic clusters and 106 design ideas. This work represents a first step towards the development of seamless real-time AR interventions for mental health.

</details>


### [96] [The Digital Pirahã Condition: Ecological Mismatch and the Reconstruction of Recursive Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25287)
*Dhushy Thillaivasana,Samar Shailendrab,Kristina Nichollsc,Deepani Guruged*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"数字皮拉罕条件"模型，解释数字与AI环境如何培养浅层认知模式，导致认知生态与学术推理需求之间的错配，并提出教育机构应通过认知生态重构来应对这一挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字和AI介导环境正在重塑人类推理发展的认知生态，日常活动嵌入数据化基础设施后，认知习惯适应了即时性、碎片化、外部化和算法过滤的条件，但由此产生的适应性认知模式与学术和机构活动系统所需的递归、整合推理存在生态错配，需要理论解释和应对框架。

Method: 采用文化生态模型方法，提出"数字皮拉罕条件"这一理论模型，从文化生态学角度分析数字环境对认知模式的影响，并设计教育干预框架。

Result: 识别出数字环境下培养的浅层认知模式特征：认知扁平化、递归能力降低、对外部支架的依赖增强；发现这些适应与学术推理需求之间存在生态错配；提出以讲师作为"文化企业家"通过模拟庇护所、AI支持的元认知支架和递归课程架构来重构学习认知生态的方案。

Conclusion: 数字皮拉罕条件为理解当代认知变化提供了理论视角，也为AI介导社会中的生态重构提供了框架；认知错配是生态结果而非心理缺陷，教育机构应通过有意的认知生态位构建来应对。

Abstract: Contemporary digital and AI-mediated environments are reshaping the cognitive ecologies within which human reasoning develops. As everyday activity becomes embedded in datafied infrastructures, cognitive habits adapt to conditions of immediacy, fragmentation, externalisation, and algorithmic filtering. This paper introduces the Digital Pirahã Condition, a cultural ecological model explaining how these environments cultivate adaptive but shallow cognitive patterns, epistemic flattening, reduced recursive capacity, and heightened reliance on external scaffolds. While functional within digital systems, these adaptations create an ecological mismatch with the recursive, integrative reasoning required in academic and institutional activity systems. The paper argues that this mismatch is an ecological outcome rather than a psychological deficit, and that addressing it requires intentional cognitive niche construction within educational institutions. The lecturer is conceptualised as a cultural entrepreneur who reconstructs the cognitive ecology of learning through analog sanctuaries, AI-supported metacognitive scaffolds, and recursive curriculum architectures. The Digital Pirahã Condition thus provides a theoretical lens for understanding contemporary cognitive change and a framework for ecological redesign in AI-mediated societies.

</details>


### [97] [When LLM Rationales Become User-Facing: Effects on Trust Perception, Decision-Making, and Gaze Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25489)
*Xin Sun,Ting Pan,Yajing Wang,Shu Wei,Jos A. Bosch,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过两项实验（在线实验N=68和眼动实验N=54）考察大语言模型提供的推理过程是否有助于用户校准对模型答案的信任。结果显示：推理的正确性和确定性框架影响信任与决策，不正确的推理比不提供推理更降低对系统的信任，且眼动数据可预测信任状态。这表明并非越多的推理越好，应设计选择性、可验证、与证据关联的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地展示逐步推理过程，但尚不清楚这些推理过程是帮助用户判断何时值得信任，还是仅仅通过流畅的推理来说服用户。研究旨在通过"可审计的信任校准"框架填补这一空白。

Method: 研究1（在线实验，N=68）操纵推理呈现格式（即时、延迟、按需）、推理正确性（正确、错误）和确定性框架（无、确定、不确定）；研究2（眼动追踪实验，N=54）考察无推理、正确推理和错误推理条件下的信任、决策和眼动模式，并进行事后预测建模分析。

Result: 研究1未发现呈现格式的可靠效应，但推理正确性和确定性框架影响信任和决策信心。研究2中，错误推理吸引更多对证据的注意并导致更大瞳孔直径（反映认知努力），且比不提供推理更降低对系统的信任；眼动特征可预测信任和决策相关用户状态。

Conclusion: 推理并非越多越好，应设计选择性、与证据关联、校准确定性表达且易于验证的推理过程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly show step-by-step reasoning rationales alongside their answers, turning reasoning from an internal model capability into a user-facing interface feature. Yet it is unclear whether such rationales help users judge when trust is warranted or merely persuade through fluent reasoning. We address this gap through the lens of auditable trust calibration: user-facing rationales should help people inspect whether an answer is warranted by evidence. We test this framing in factual verification through two linked studies. Study 1, an online experiment (N=68), manipulated rationale presentation format (instant, delayed, on demand), rationale correctness (correct, incorrect), and certainty framing (none, certain, uncertain). Study 2, a controlled eye-tracking study (N=54), examined how no-, correct-, and incorrect-rationale conditions were associated with users' trust, decision-making, and eye-movement patterns. Study 1 showed no reliable presentation-format effects; instead, rationale correctness and certainty framing influenced the trust in the information, trust in the LLM system, and decision confidence. In Study 2, incorrect rationales drew more attention to the supporting evidence and larger pupil diameter while the rationale was viewed, consistent with greater cognitive effort. Incorrect rationales also lowered trust in LLM system relative to showing no rationale, whereas the no-rationale difference was weaker for trust in information. A post-hoc predictive modeling analysis of gaze data from Study 2 further showed that gaze features carried predictive signal for trust- and decision-related user states. This work challenges the assumption that more reasoning is always better and supports rationale designs that are selective, linked to evidence, calibrated in how they express certainty, and easier to verify.

</details>


### [98] [Dissociable Spatial and Temporal Effects of Interaction Latency in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25681)
*Xiaoye Michael Wang,Catherine M. Sabiston,Timothy N. Welsh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验证明，VR交互延迟会对手部空间定位精度和时间效率产生可分离的影响：空间准确性在最短延迟下就受到损害，而运动时间仅在较长延迟下才显著增加，表明仅用时间或效率指标无法全面评估VR延迟效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究表明交互延迟会损害VR性能，但常见的空间、时间和效率指标是否反映相同的延迟相关干扰尚不明确，缺乏对这些指标敏感性和可分离性的系统比较。

Method: 在VR和物理环境中进行手动指向实验，使用动作捕捉管道控制虚拟手部化身，在参与者手部运动与虚拟手部渲染运动之间施加0-500ms的额外延迟，比较VR内不同延迟条件下的端点误差、运动时间、端点变异性和吞吐量。

Result: 与基线相比，VR性能表现出更大的端点误差、更长的运动时间、更大的端点变异性和更低的吞吐量。在VR内，增加的交互延迟进一步提高了端点误差和变异性、降低了吞吐量并改变了运动时间，但不同指标呈现不同特征：端点误差在最短延迟下即增加，而运动时间在短延迟下保持稳定、仅在长延迟下才显著增加。

Conclusion: 交互延迟在沉浸式VR中产生可分离的空间和时间后果，不能仅用运动时间或效率指标评估延迟敏感的VR交互，HCI评估应同时考察空间和时间性能，特别是涉及视觉引导的手动任务时。

Abstract: Motion-to-photon latency is inherent in immersive virtual reality (VR) systems and can arise from multiple sensorimotor loops, including view-contingent latency between head movement and display update and interaction latency between hand movement and the virtual effector. Although prior work shows that interaction latency can impair VR performance, it remains unclear whether common spatial, temporal, and efficiency measures reveal the same latency-related disruption. This study addressed this question by experimentally imposing delays between the physical and virtual hands during manual pointing in VR. Participants pointed to targets on a horizontal surface in VR and in the physical environment as an unmediated baseline. In VR, pointing was performed with a virtual hand avatar controlled by a motion capture pipeline, and additional delays (0-500 ms) were imposed between the participant's hand movement and the rendered movement of the virtual hand. Relative to the baseline, performance in VR showed greater endpoint error, longer movement time, greater endpoint variability, and lower throughput. Within VR, added interaction latency further increased endpoint error and variability, reduced throughput, and altered movement time, but these effects followed different profiles: endpoint error increased even at the shortest delays, whereas movement time remained stable at short delays and increased primarily at longer delays. These findings show that interaction latency produces dissociable spatial and temporal consequences in immersive VR, such that endpoint accuracy revealed disruption before movement time or throughput. Thus, latency-sensitive VR interactions cannot be fully evaluated using movement time or efficiency measures alone. Instead, HCI evaluations should assess both spatial and temporal performance, particularly when VR tasks involve visually guided manual actions.

</details>


### [99] [Designing Trustworthy LLM-based Wellbeing Recommendation through Controllable Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25809)
*Alan Said,Alexandra Weilenmann*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统级框架，通过显式交互约束（如指导策略、解释风格、直接性程度和用户控制机制）来结构化LLM在健康福祉场景中的推荐行为，以解决隐式适配带来的控制、责任归属和用户影响力不足的问题，并实现透明、可控且与人类福祉对齐的推荐系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在健康福祉推荐中依赖隐式机制（如提示工程和对齐）进行行为适配，这限制了系统对推荐表达方式的控制、责任归属的清晰性以及用户影响力的发挥，在影响用户行为和长期结果的高风险场景中尤为问题严重。

Method: 提出系统级视角，通过显式交互约束结构化对话行为，包括指导策略、解释风格、直接性程度和用户控制机制；基于可解释推荐的工作，构建模块化架构，支持不同配置的系统设计与评估。

Result: 该框架能够应对健康福祉推荐中的三大挑战：信任校准、意图对齐和后果感知，并支持针对自我效能、感知能动性和适当依赖等用户中心结果进行系统设计与评估。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个系统级框架，使得基于LLM的推荐系统在保持适应性的同时，具备透明性、可控性，并与人类福祉对齐。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate personalized guidance in wellbeing contexts such as physical activity, stress management, and mental health support, enabling fluent and context-aware interaction but relying on largely implicit mechanisms that shape how recommendations are expressed and adapted. We argue that this reliance on implicit adaptation through prompting and alignment limits control over guidance, responsibility framing, and user influence, which is particularly problematic in wellbeing settings where recommendations affect users' actions and long-term outcomes. We propose a system-level perspective in which conversational behavior is structured through explicit interaction constraints, including guidance strategies, explanation styles, degrees of directness, and mechanisms for user control. Building on prior work on tangible recommendations, we show how these constraints address key challenges in wellbeing-oriented recommendation, namely trust calibration, intent alignment, and consequence awareness. We outline a modular architecture for controllable LLM-based recommendation and discuss how different configurations can be systematically designed and evaluated in relation to user-centered outcomes such as self-efficacy, perceived agency, and appropriate reliance. This paper contributes a system-level framework for designing LLM-based recommender systems that are adaptive while remaining transparent, controllable, and aligned with human wellbeing.

</details>


### [100] [Explainable Control Framework (XCF) based on Fuzzy Model-Agnostic Explanation and LLM Agent-Supported Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25941)
*Faliang Yin,Hak-Keung Lam,David Watson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释控制框架（XCF），包含模型无关的解释方法HFMAE-C和大语言模型驱动的用户界面，用于揭示控制器决策逻辑和工作机制，在倒立摆和Turtlebot避障案例中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着控制器日益复杂（如数据驱动黑箱模型和数学严谨但复杂的设计），亟需可解释控制方法，以提供人类可理解的控制器行为洞察。

Method: 提出XCF框架，支持模型无关的解释并可结合系统响应动态优化局部解释；提出HFMAE-C方法，利用模糊逻辑系统近似控制器行为与系统动力学，通过IF-THEN规则提供样本、局部、领域和宇宙四级解释，并输出显著性值量化状态贡献；开发基于大语言模型的用户界面，自动分析需求、选择算法、生成自然语言报告并提供交互咨询。

Result: 在倒立摆系统和Turtlebot障碍物规避案例中，通过模拟用户实验和与主流可解释控制方法的定量比较，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: XCF框架及其算法能够有效解释控制器行为，提升控制系统的可理解性和可信度。

Abstract: Increasing demand for precise and reliable control in complex scenarios has led to the development of increasingly sophisticated controllers, including data-driven approaches employing closed box models and mathematically rigorous yet complex designs. This complexity highlights the needs for explainable control that can provide human-understandable insights into controller behavior. In this paper, an explainable control framework (XCF) along with supporting algorithms and user interface are proposed to explain how controllers determine their control actions and their underlying working mechanism. The novel contributions of this work are threefold: First, the XCF is designed to provide model-agnostic explanations for controllers in closed-loop systems and can optionally refine local explanations by system response dynamics. Second, a novel explanation method, hierarchical fuzzy model-agnostic explanation for control systems (HFMAE-C), is proposed based on the designed framework. The HFMAE-C employs a fuzzy logic system to approximate the controller's behavior and system dynamics, providing sample, local, domain and universe level explanations via IF-THEN rules revealing the controller's decision logic and salience values quantifying the contribution of system states to control actions. Third, a large language model agent-supported user interface is developed to automatically analyze user requirements, select appropriate algorithms, interpret the generated explanations to a natural language report, and provide interactive consultation. Case studies on inverted pendulum system and Turtlebot obstacle avoidance demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method through simulated user experiments and quantitative comparisons with mainstream explainable control approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [TurboMPC: Fast, Scalable, and Differentiable Model Predictive Control on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24039)
*Gabriel Bravo-Palacios,Jianghan Zhang,Zachary Pestrikov,Brian Plancher,Thomas Lew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TurboMPC是一个完全在GPU上运行的可微分MPC求解器，支持不等式约束、隐式积分器、跨时间步耦合成本和松弛变量。它结合了SQP、ADMM内层求解器、隐式微分和JAX-CUDA协同实现，在约束规划、人形机器人模仿学习和强化学习中相比CPU和GPU求解器分别实现最高15倍和58倍加速，并在实车最小时间赛车任务中通过贝叶斯优化的批量GPU加速调参显著优于手工调参基线，且可扩展到超过8000个节点的规划时域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人领域日益依赖GPU进行并行仿真、大规模学习和神经网络推理，但现有的MPC求解器难以在GPU上高效运行，同时保持快速、可微分且兼容机器人中使用的表达性MPC公式。

Method: TurboMPC将序列二次规划(SQP)、交替方向乘子法(ADMM)内层求解器、隐式微分和协同设计的JAX-CUDA实现相结合，构建了一个完全在GPU上运行的可微分MPC求解器，支持状态和控制不等式约束、隐式积分器、跨时间步耦合成本和松弛变量。

Result: 在仿真中，TurboMPC在约束规划、人形机器人模仿学习和带神经网络成本函数的强化学习任务中，相比最先进的CPU和GPU可微分求解器分别实现最高15倍和58倍加速。在实车最小时间赛车中，通过贝叶斯优化的批量GPU加速调参显著优于手工调参基线，并可扩展到超过8000个节点的规划时域。

Conclusion: TurboMPC证明了GPU加速的可微分MPC求解器在机器人应用中具有显著的性能优势，能够有效支持大规模并行优化和复杂约束，为机器人学中MPC与GPU计算范式的融合提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotics increasingly relies on GPUs for parallel simulation, large-scale learning, and neural-network inference. For model predictive control (MPC) to scale with this paradigm, solvers must run efficiently on this hardware while remaining fast, differentiable, and compatible with expressive MPC formulations used in robotics. We present TurboMPC, a differentiable MPC solver that runs entirely on the GPU and supports state and control inequality constraints, implicit integrators, cross-time-coupled costs, and slack variables. TurboMPC combines sequential quadratic programming (SQP), an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) inner solver, implicit differentiation, and a co-designed JAX-CUDA implementation for efficiency and ease of use. In simulation, we validate TurboMPC on constrained planning, humanoid imitation learning, and reinforcement learning with neural-network cost function tasks, achieving up to $15\times$ and $58\times$ speedups over state-of-the-art CPU and GPU differentiable solvers, respectively. We deploy TurboMPC on a full-scale car for minimum-time racing and find that batched, GPU-accelerated tuning of MPC parameters via Bayesian optimization yields significantly faster driving than a hand-tuned baseline. TurboMPC also scales to planning horizons of over $8000$ knot points while maintaining control of the vehicle. We open-source TurboMPC at: https://github.com/ToyotaResearchInstitute/turbompc

</details>


### [102] [BFMTrack: Latent Sequence Optimization for Physics-Based Motion Tracking with Behavioral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25056)
*Thomas Rupf,Agon Serifi,David Müller,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在序列优化（LSO）方法，通过策略梯度更新和时序相关噪声建模，扩展行为基础模型（BFMs）的能力，使其能够在不依赖奖励工程的情况下实现精确的运动序列跟踪，并在密集跟踪、稀疏关键帧和真实人形机器人部署中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行为基础模型（BFMs）在时不变任务（如目标到达、状态奖励优化）上表现优异，但其潜在空间不支持时变目标（如运动序列跟踪）。现有的基于滑动窗口平均的启发式方法无法捕捉高度动态运动的细微变化，亟需一种无需奖励工程即可实现精确运动跟踪的方法。

Method: 提出潜在序列优化（LSO），结合仿真rollout与策略梯度更新，对潜在序列进行优化。为引导优化生成平滑连贯的潜在轨迹，使用时序相关噪声对潜在序列进行建模。

Result: 在密集跟踪、稀疏关键帧任务以及真实人形机器人直接部署中均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: LSO方法成功扩展了BFMs的能力，使其能够处理时变目标，实现精确的运动序列跟踪，且无需奖励工程和调参，具备从仿真到真实机器人部署的泛化能力。

Abstract: Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) offer a promising path toward universal physics-based character control by organizing a rich repertoire of physically plausible behaviors into a latent space, guided by a large-scale motion dataset. While these models excel at time-invariant tasks, such as goal-reaching and state-based reward optimization, their latent space does not directly support time-varying objectives, such as tracking a motion sequence. For tracking, existing heuristics rely on moving-window-averaging that fails to capture the nuances of highly dynamic motions. In this work, we propose a novel Latent Sequence Optimization (LSO) to address these shortcomings. Our approach combines simulation rollouts with a policy gradient update to optimize over a sequence of latents, extending the capabilities of BFMs toward precise motion tracking without requiring reward engineering and tuning. To guide the optimization toward smooth, coherent latent trajectories, we model the latent sequence using temporally correlated noise. We validate our approach across dense tracking, sparse keyframing, and direct deployment onto a real humanoid robot.

</details>


### [103] [Invariant Kalman filtering for extended pose estimation in multi-IMU articulated rigid-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25083)
*Sven Goffin,Cédric Schwartz,Silvère Bonnabel,Olivier Brüls,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向IMU标定铰接刚体系统的相对L-扩展位姿李群表示方法，结合迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波(IterIEKF)与无噪声伪观测约束，在UR5e机器人和人腿数据集上验证了该方法相比各类EKF基线在收敛速度、稳定性和RMSE上均有显著提升（降低至少50%）。
