Motivation: 现有VLA模型在机器人操作任务上展现出强泛化能力，但将其应用于全身、接触丰富的人形机器人 locomotion 领域仍面临数据稀缺、缺乏动态一致演示以及难以在基于学习的管线中编码最优性和安全性等挑战。

Method: 构建WOLF-VLA框架：1）利用全身最优控制（OC）合成动态可行的关节轨迹数据集，涵盖六类 locomotion 任务族并参数化环境变化、物体颜色、位置及视觉干扰；2）基于联合轨迹、自我中心视觉观测和自然语言指令训练VLA模型，实现直接由自然语言指令生成 locomotion 策略。

Result: 训练得到的策略展现出强推理能力、对初始条件变异的鲁棒性，并在多种任务和环境设置下表现具有竞争力。系统消融实验证实了每种模态对模型性能的影响。

Conclusion: 数据集、模型检查点和仿真基准将开源释放，为全身人形 locomotion 的VLA控制建立可复现的动态一致基准，推动可扩展的指令驱动 locomotion 策略迁移研究。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently demonstrated strong generalization in robotic manipulation, yet their applicability to whole-body, contact-rich humanoid locomotion remains severely underexplored due to data scarcity, the absence of dynamically consistent demonstrations, and the difficulty of encoding optimality and safety in learning-based pipelines. This work introduces a unified framework WOLF-VLA that integrates whole-body optimal-control (OC) motion synthesis with large-scale multi-modal dataset to train VLAs capable of generating humanoid locomotion policies directly from natural-language instructions. We construct a comprehensive dataset of dynamically feasible humanoid trajectories across six locomotion-related task families, each parameterized by environmental variations, object colors, placements, and visual distractors. We train a VLA model using the collected joint trajectories, ego-centric visual observations and natural language instruction, yielding a policy that exhibits strong reasoning and robustness to initial-condition variability, and competitive performance across several tasks and environment settings. A systematic ablation study demonstrates the impact of each modality on the model performance. The full dataset, model checkpoints, and benchmarking simulation suite will be openly released, establishing a reproducible dynamically consistent benchmark for whole-body humanoid locomotion rich VLA control and enabling future research in scalable transfer of instruction-driven locomotion policies.

</details>


### [140] [1000 Rallies: An Event-Camera Dataset and Real-Time Learned Ball-State Estimation for Robotic Table Tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25620)
*Raphaela Kreiser,Asude Aydin,Yin Bi,Claudio Fanconi,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个大规模乒乓球事件相机数据集（超过1000个回合），并训练了联合估计球位置与速度的CNN，结合卡尔曼滤波将弹跳点预测误差降低36%，最终实现了由事件感知驱动的实时人机乒乓球对打。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧相机在高速乒乓球场景中面临帧率与计算成本的权衡，而事件相机虽具微秒级时间分辨率优势，但缺乏大规模真实场景数据集来开发和评估基于事件的感知方法。

Method: 1) 构建包含超过1000个回合、多种水平球员的事件相机数据集，同步14台200FPS高帧率相机生成1kHz伪真值标签；2) 训练CNN联合估计图像平面中的球位置与速度；3) 将预测速度作为额外观测量融入卡尔曼滤波；4) 将事件感知系统集成到Stäubli机械臂上实现闭环控制。

Result: 在卡尔曼滤波中引入事件驱动的速度观测，将弹跳点预测误差相比仅基于位置的方法降低36%。

Conclusion: 事件相机能够有效解决高速球类运动中的实时感知难题，该数据集和算法为动态场景下的事件视觉研究提供了重要基准，并首次实现了基于事件感知的实时人机乒乓球对打。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis has emerged as a compelling benchmark for real-time robotic perception due to its fast ball dynamics and stringent timing requirements. Accurate, high-frequency, and low-latency ball state estimation is critical for reliable trajectory prediction and timely control. Traditional frame-based cameras face an inherent trade-off: low frame rates leave temporal blind spots that miss fast-moving objects and high frame rates raise data and computational cost. Event cameras instead offer microsecond temporal resolution and, under sufficient illumination, remain largely free of motion blur even at high ball speeds. However, the community lacks large-scale datasets to develop and benchmark event-based perception in realistic sports scenarios. We address this gap by introducing the first large-scale event-camera dataset for table tennis, comprising over 1000 rallies from a diverse group of players ranging from amateurs to elite-level athletes. Each recording captures the event stream alongside 14 synchronized high-speed frame-based cameras at 200 FPS, which we use to produce 1 kHz pseudo ground-truth labels for ball position, velocity, and spin. Building on this dataset, we train a convolutional neural network robust to background player motion that jointly estimates the ball's position and velocity in the image-plane from events. Treating the predicted velocity as an additional measurement in the Kalman filter reduces bounce-point prediction error by 36% relative to a position-only baseline. Finally, we close the perception-action loop by integrating the event-based system with a Stäubli robotic arm, enabling the first real-time human-robot table tennis rallies driven by event-based perception.

</details>


### [141] [Event-Adaptive Motion Planning with Distilled Vision-Language Model in Safety-Critical Situations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25629)
*Zhenwei Huang,Changsheng You,Shuai Wang,Chao Zhou,Wei Xu,Yi Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EAMP框架，通过事件触发机制选择性调用VLM进行语义推理，结合蒸馏优化的SemNav-VLM和语义模型预测控制，在保障实时性的同时提升动态安全裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键场景中机器人导航面临动态代理不可预测行为导致的碰撞风险，而频繁调用大型VLM会引入严重计算延迟，破坏物理执行的稳定性。

Method: 1) 提示可配置语义事件触发器(PC-SET)持续监测短时序片段中的行为异常，选择性激活语义干预；2) 事件触发的蒸馏SemNav-VLM通过物理验证语义蒸馏微调，将异常映射为离散策略决策；3) 语义模型预测控制(SMPC)模块将策略转化为优化目标和几何参考的动态重构。

Result: 在安全关键物流场景中，EAMP有效对齐高层推理与低层控制，相比现有基线显著提升动态安全裕度，同时保持实时效率。

Conclusion: 事件自适应运动规划框架通过选择性VLM调用解决了高层语义推理与低层实时控制的冲突，为VLM在安全关键导航中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robot navigation in safety-critical scenarios faces significant challenges from unforeseen semantic events, where collisions arise primarily from the unpredictable behaviors of dynamic agents rather than unseen objects. While large vision-language models (VLMs) offer remarkable capabilities in commonsense reasoning, frequently invoking them within the continuous control loop introduces severe computational latency, fundamentally destabilizing physical execution. To address these challenges, we propose event-adaptive motion planning (EAMP), an efficient framework for VLM-based robot navigation. Specifically, a prompt-configurable semantic event trigger (PC-SET) selectively activates semantic intervention by continuously monitoring short temporal clips for behavioral anomalies. Upon triggering, an event-triggered distilled SemNav-VLM, fine-tuned via physically verified semantic distillation, maps detected anomalies into discrete strategy-level decisions. Subsequently, a semantic model predictive control (SMPC) module translates these strategies into dynamic reconfigurations of optimization objectives and geometric references. Extensive experiments in safety-critical logistics scenarios demonstrate that EAMP effectively aligns high-level reasoning with low-level control, significantly improving dynamic safety margins over existing baselines while preserving real-time efficiency.

</details>


### [142] [Calousel: Extrinsic Calibration of Non-overlapping Multi-camera Systems from Pure Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25646)
*Gwanhyeong Song,Chaehyeon Song,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅需单个静态标定板和纯旋转运动的多相机系统外参标定方法，无需重叠视场，通过全局优化框架实现高精度标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多相机外参标定方法存在局限性：基于标定靶的方法需要大型靶标或预测量多靶标位姿，部署成本高；基于运动的方法存在漂移误差、尺度模糊和运动退化问题。现有方法难以兼顾精度与易用性。

Method: 利用纯旋转运动（如转台），使所有相机在时间上先后观察同一静态标定板，共享同一几何参考系。引入潜在转台坐标系，将时间分离的观测整合到SE(3)上的全局优化框架中，通过3D误差项进行联合优化。

Result: 在受控相机支架和全尺寸车辆平台（含异构相机）上验证，分析了非理想转台运动下的鲁棒性。实验表明该方法在无需专用精密硬件的情况下保持有竞争力的精度，适用于现场部署。

Conclusion: 该方法在精度和易用性之间取得了良好平衡，为无重叠视场的多相机系统外参标定提供了一种实用且鲁棒的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Extrinsic calibration of multi-camera systems with non-overlapping FOVs has been a challenging problem in the robotics literature. Conventional target-based methods impose substantial target setup overhead, either deploying large calibration targets or requiring pre-measured multi-target poses. Motion-based approaches instead suffer from drift error, scale ambiguity, and motion degeneracy. Securing both accuracy and usability, we propose a novel calibration method that leverages pure rotational motion, requiring only a single static calibration board. The key idea is to make all cameras sequentially observe the same target under a shared geometric reference, even without overlapping views. To integrate these time-separated observations, we formulate the problem using a latent turntable frame and a 3D error on SE(3) within a global optimization framework. We validate the proposed method on both a controlled camera rig and a full-scale vehicle platform with heterogeneous cameras, and analyze robustness under non-ideal turntable motion. Extensive experiments show that our approach maintains competitive accuracy without specialized precision hardware, proving its strong suitability for realistic on-site deployments. Our code is publicly available here.

</details>


### [143] [Learning to Adapt: Reptile-D-Learning for Robust and Efficient Control Under Parametric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25659)
*Haipeng Cao,Zhaolong Shen,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Reptile-D-learning 框架，利用 Reptile 元学习算法捕捉不同参数系统的共享动力学结构，学习可泛化的 Lyapunov 网络初始化和高性能控制器，实验证明该方法在非线性控制系统中显著提升了泛化能力和对未见参数的快速适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的 Lyapunov 控制（LLC）依赖精确系统模型，参数变化和不确定性会导致稳定性约束失效且需要昂贵的重新训练；D-learning虽无需显式模型但受限于单任务动力学，在大参数偏移下性能退化。

Method: 提出 Reptile-D-learning 框架，结合 Reptile 元学习算法，在不同参数的系统间捕获共享动力学结构，学习可泛化的 Lyapunov 网络初始化与高性能控制器。

Result: 在多个非线性控制系统上的实验表明，Reptile-D-learning 显著提升了泛化能力和对未见参数配置的快速适应能力。

Conclusion: Reptile-D-learning 通过元学习有效解决了 LLC 中参数变化导致的泛化瓶颈，实现了跨参数系统的快速自适应。

Abstract: Learning-based Lyapunov Control (LLC) provides formal stability guarantees for nonlinear systems, but its validity relies heavily on accurate system models. Parameter variations and uncertainties may invalidate stability constraints, leading to costly retraining. Although D-learning can estimate Lyapunov derivatives without relying on explicit dynamics models, it remains limited by single-task dynamics and degrades under large parameter shifts. We propose Reptile-D-learning, a framework that leverages the Reptile meta-learning algorithm to capture shared dynamical structures across systems with different parameters, thereby learning a generalizable Lyapunov network initialization and a high-performance controller. Experiments on multiple nonlinear control systems demonstrate that Reptile-D-learning significantly improves both generalization and rapid adaptation to unseen parameter configurations.

</details>


### [144] [SA-LIVO: Efficient LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry with Subspace-Aware Degeneracy Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25699)
*Yinong Cao,Xin He,Yuwei Chen,Shijie Liu,Chunlai Li,Jianyu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SA-LIVO提出了一种子空间感知信息融合（SAIF）框架，通过对联合LiDAR-视觉信息矩阵进行特征分解并在每个特征方向上应用线性-钳位软门控，实现方向选择性融合，解决了紧耦合LIVO系统中LiDAR与视觉退化方向不一致的问题。实验表明其精度与最强基线相当，且在退化场景中具有有界漂移，CPU上每帧仅需12.3ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有紧耦合LIVO系统在外感受传感器面临独立失效模式（LiDAR在扫描几何欠约束时退化，视觉在光照差或无纹理时退化）时，现有对策（如二元退化检测、协方差膨胀、场景级质量门控）仅在模态层面操作，未解决联合信息矩阵的方向依赖结构。导致视觉残差进入LiDAR已充分约束的位姿方向，而退化方向上视觉补偿分散在整个状态空间而非集中在需要的地方。

Method: 提出SA-LIVO系统，核心是子空间感知信息融合（SAIF）框架：对联合LiDAR-视觉信息矩阵进行特征分解，在每个特征方向上应用线性-钳位软门控，衰减退化方向同时保留可观测方向的完整强度。LiDAR和视觉残差在同一个InEKF循环中、在共享线性化点处联合优化。由于视觉信息仅在LiDAR不足处贡献，光度雅可比在循环前组装一次并在迭代中复用，避免常规迭代滤波器的每迭代计算开销。

Result: 在HILTI'22、New College、Oxford Spires三个基准的29个序列以及并发退化场景上进行实验，精度与最强基线相当，在竞争系统发散时SA-LIVO具有有界漂移。在笔记本CPU上平均每帧12.3ms，在嵌入式ARM板上（无GPU）平均26.8ms，峰值内存降低3.6-6.3倍。

Conclusion: SA-LIVO通过方向选择性融合策略有效解决了LIVO系统中多模态传感器退化方向不一致的问题，实现了高效且鲁棒的位姿估计，代码将开源。

Abstract: Tightly coupled LiDAR-visual-inertial odometry (LIVO) fuses precise geometric depth with complementary visual measurements, yet its exteroceptive sensors face independent failure modes: LiDAR degenerates when scan geometry is under-constrained, while visual measurements degrade under adverse illumination or texture absence. Existing countermeasures, including binary degeneracy detection, covariance inflation, and scene-level quality gating, operate at the modality level and leave the direction-dependent structure of the joint information matrix unaddressed. Consequently, visual residuals enter pose directions where LiDAR is well-constrained, while in deficient directions visual compensation disperses across the full state space rather than concentrating where needed. We propose SA-LIVO, a LiDAR-inertial-visual odometry system addressing these limitations through direction-selective fusion and information-efficient processing. The Subspace-Aware Information Fusion (SAIF) framework eigendecomposes the joint LiDAR-visual information matrix and applies a linear-clamp soft gate per eigendirection, attenuating degenerate directions while preserving observable ones at full strength. LiDAR and visual residuals are then jointly optimized in one InEKF loop at a shared linearization point. Since visual information contributes only where LiDAR is deficient, photometric Jacobians are assembled once before the loop and reused across iterations, avoiding the per-iteration cost of conventional iterated filters. Experiments on 29 sequences from three benchmarks (HILTI'22, New College, Oxford Spires) and concurrent-degradation scenarios show accuracy competitive with the strongest baselines and bounded drift where competing systems diverge. SA-LIVO averages 12.3 ms per frame on a laptop CPU and 26.8 ms on an embedded ARM board without GPU, with 3.6-6.3x lower peak memory. The code will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [145] [Learning Asynchronous Upper-body Task-space Trajectory Tracking Policy for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25706)
*Yumeng Liu,Dongqi Wang,Jiyu Yu,Yijun Fan,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种异步上身任务空间跟踪框架，通过教师-学生蒸馏初始化策略、滑动窗口全局奖励和MPC模块，解决人形机器人规划器与控制器之间的时间异步和结构不完整问题，在低更新率下实现更好的跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高层人形规划器输出稀疏、低频率的任务空间轨迹，而全身控制器需要高频运行，导致规划与执行之间存在时间异步和结构不完整问题。

Method: 1) 教师-学生蒸馏初始化学生策略，以完整缓存的未来轨迹和执行时间索引为条件；2) 滑动窗口全局奖励训练以减少帧漂移；3) 任务后训练阶段使用MPC模块将稀疏参考补全为浮基和上身引导，并加入动作级和FK级自引导约束策略漂移。

Result: 在仿真和Unitree G1硬件实验中，该方法在低更新率下跟踪性能优于同步和去耦基线，且对分布外运动具有更安全的适应能力。

Conclusion: 异步上身任务空间跟踪框架有效解决了人形机器人的异步问题，提高了跟踪鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: High-level humanoid planners often output sparse task-space, low-rate trajectories, whereas whole-body controllers run at high frequency. This creates temporal asynchrony between the planning and execution, and structural incompleteness for full-body control. We propose an asynchronous upper body task-space tracking framework for humanoids. A student policy is initialized by teacher-student distillation, conditioned on the full cached future trajectory and an execution-time index, and trained with a sliding-window global reward to reduce frame drift without explicit frame estimation. For task-specific post-training, an MPC module completes sparse references into floating-base and upper-body guidance, while action- and FK level self-guidance constrain policy drift. Simulation and Unitree G1 hardware experiments show improved tracking under low update rates, stronger performance than synchronous and decoupled baselines, and safer adaptation to out-of-distribution motions.

</details>


### [146] [Stage-Aware and Roughness-Constrained Diffusion Policy for Multi-Stage Robotic Polishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25754)
*Shuai Ke,Jiexin Zhang,Huan Zhao,Zhiao Wei,Yikun Guo,Tiange Wu,Guoqiang Guo,Haoyuan Zhou,Jie Pan,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向机器人抛光的阶段感知与粗糙度约束扩散策略（SRDP），通过多模态观测推断过程阶段后验，并引入粗糙度导向的过程约束扩散采样方法，在航天器舱段涂层表面抛光等场景中显著提升了阶段转换稳定性、工艺参数一致性和最终表面质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抛光对表面质量要求极高，而现有机器人模仿学习方法在工业抛光中面临长时程依赖、阶段转换不确定性强以及耦合工艺参数难以建模与调控等挑战，亟需一种能够感知阶段变化并约束工艺一致性的方法。

Method: SRDP方法包含两个核心：一是从多模态观测历史中推断过程阶段后验，并以之条件化共享的反向去噪过程，实现无需显式阶段标签的阶段一致动作生成；二是引入粗糙度导向的过程约束扩散采样方法，在阶段预设主轴转速下生成受约束的进给速度和法向接触力，提升工艺一致性和物理可行性。

Result: 在航天器舱段涂层表面抛光和内腔结构表面精加工两类代表性场景中，通过与先进基线对比、消融实验和真实机器人验证，结果表明SRDP在阶段转换稳定性、工艺参数一致性和最终表面质量方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SRDP通过阶段感知和粗糙度约束的扩散策略，有效解决了机器人抛光中长时程依赖和工艺参数耦合问题，为工业抛光中的模仿学习提供了一种鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Polishing is a critical finishing process in high-end manufacturing fields such as aerospace, where surface quality directly affects the service performance and reliability of components. Robotic imitation learning provides a flexible solution for such tasks, but current methods remain limited in industrial polishing because of long-horizon dependencies, uncertain stage transitions, and the difficulty of modeling and regulating coupled process parameters. To address these issues, this paper proposes a Stage-Aware and Roughness-Constrained Diffusion Policy (SRDP) for robotic polishing. SRDP infers the process-stage posterior from multimodal observation histories and uses it to condition the shared reverse denoising process, enabling stage-consistent action generation without external stage labels during execution. Furthermore, a roughness-oriented process-constrained diffusion sampling method is incorporated to generate constrained feed speed and normal contact force under stage-wise preset spindle speeds, thereby improving process consistency and physical feasibility. Systematic experiments are conducted on two representative scenarios, namely spacecraft cabin coating-surface polishing and inner-cavity structural surface finishing. Comparisons with advanced baselines, ablation studies, and real-robot validations comprehensively evaluate the proposed method. The results show that SRD improves stage-transition stability, process-parameter consistency, and final surface quality across different polishing scenarios.

</details>


### [147] [StairMaster: Learning to Conquer Risky Hollow Stairs for Agile Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25765)
*Xincheng Tang,Youhan Xie,Zhengjie Shu,Wanyu Li,Lai Jiang,Wenkang Hu,Yitong Li,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出StairMaster，一种三阶段强化学习框架，使四足机器人在高达55°倾角的空心楼梯上实现零样本稳定攀爬，这是首个在真实环境中实现如此陡峭空心楼梯攀爬的RL策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人攀爬空心楼梯面临腿部卡陷风险高、深度信息稀疏且噪声大等挑战，现有方法难以解决这些极端不连续地形的稳定 locomotion 问题。

Method: 提出三阶段强化学习框架：1) 交叉注意力机制从噪声深度数据中提取结构特征；2) 空间感知循环单元(SRU)维护鲁棒的时空记忆以缓解感知盲区；3) 高保真sim-to-real深度传感器建模管道；4) 3D路点引导的主动感知奖励、空心间隙运动学与楼梯边缘惩罚。

Result: 在Unitree Go2机器人上零样本迁移成功攀爬倾角高达55°的空心楼梯，是该任务上首个成功的RL策略。

Conclusion: StairMaster通过结合交叉注意力、空间感知记忆、高保真传感器建模和主动感知奖励，有效解决了空心楼梯攀爬中的感知与运动规划难题，实现了RL策略在极端地形上的实际部署。

Abstract: Climbing hollow stairs remains a challenging problem for quadruped robots due to the high risk of leg trapping, severe depth sparsity, and high-frequency depth-sensing noise. In this paper, we propose StairMaster, a novel three-stage reinforcement learning framework for stable locomotion on such extreme discontinuous terrains. Our architecture integrates a Cross-Attention mechanism to extract structural features from noisy depth data, alongside a Spatial-aware Recurrent Unit (SRU) that maintains robust spatio-temporal memory to mitigate perception blind spots. To bridge the sim-to-real gap in depth perception, we propose a high-fidelity sim-to-real depth sensor modeling pipeline that faithfully replicates real-world sensor artifacts. Additionally, we employ a 3D waypoint-guided active perception reward for proactive sensing, alongside hollow gap kinematic and stair edge penalties to ensure precise foothold placement. We successfully deployed StairMaster on a Unitree Go2 robot, demonstrating its ability to conquer hollow stairs with an unprecedented incline of up to 55$^\circ$ through zero-shot transfer. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first RL-based policy to achieve such steep hollow stair climbing in real-world environments.
  Project Website: https://sivan666666.github.io/StairMaster/.

</details>


### [148] [MIL-LC: A Robust Magnetometer-Inertial-LiDAR Fusion Multimodal Localization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25796)
*Qiyang Lyu,Zhenyu Wu,Wei Wang,Hongming Shen,Danwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MIL-LC框架，融合磁力计、惯导和LiDAR的多模态定位方法，解决GNSS拒止、几何重复或无纹理环境下的AMR定位难题，通过自定义传感器套件实现可靠定位，即使LiDAR退化或磁场地图变化时仍能保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单模态定位方法受限于传感器自身缺陷，多模态融合框架仍过度依赖几何/纹理特征或基础设施信标，存在成本高、部署灵活性差的问题。虽然在行人场景中已探索地磁场融合，但面向AMR系统的实用方案仍空白。

Method: 提出MIL-LC框架，设计自定义磁力计-惯导-LiDAR融合传感器套件，利用环境磁场作为互补模态，结合惯导和LiDAR信息进行多模态融合定位，不依赖几何/纹理特征或额外基础设施。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，MIL-LC框架在LiDAR几何退化或长期部署中磁场地图变化的情况下，仍能实现鲁棒且准确的定位性能。

Conclusion: MIL-LC框架有效解决了挑战性环境下AMR的可靠定位问题，地磁场作为一种无需基础设施的互补模态，显著提升了多模态定位的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Localization in challenging environments, such as GNSS-denied, geometrically repetitive, or textureless scenes commonly found in offices, hotels, and underground parking facilities, remains an open problem for reliable autonomous mobile robot (AMR) deployment. Single-modality localization methods are inherently limited by the constraints of individual sensors. Although multimodal fusion frameworks have shown improved robustness, most existing approaches still rely heavily on geometric or texture features, or on infrastructure-based beacons, which increase installation and maintenance costs while reducing deployment flexibility. Recently, ambient magnetic field (AMF)-based localization has attracted growing attention because it does not depend on geometric or texture features, nor does it require additional infrastructure, making it a promising complementary modality for AMR localization. However, existing studies have only explored such fusion in pedestrian scenarios using smartphone-mounted sensor suites, and practical solutions for AMR systems remain largely unexplored. To address this gap, this article proposes a magnetometer-inertial-LiDAR fused multimodal localization framework with a custom-designed sensor suite, termed MIL-LC, which provides reliable localization even when LiDAR suffers from geometric degeneration or when the magnetic map changes during long-term deployment. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that the proposed MIL-LC framework achieves robust and accurate localization performance.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond a Shadow of a Doubt: Close Proximity Geometry Reconstruction Using FMCW Radar Shadow Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25829)
*Felix de Trogoff du Boisguezennec,Benjamin Ramtoula,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用FMCW雷达中车辆底盘遮挡形成的几何阴影这一新线索，通过解析闭式映射从雷达回波边界恢复附近细长直立物体的3D面内倾斜角度，将2D旋转雷达的能力从定位扩展到3D场景重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气和光照条件下，相机和LiDAR性能下降，而毫米波雷达虽鲁棒但存在仰角压缩问题，限制了其对三维几何的推理能力。现有方法缺乏利用雷达阴影来推断物体3D姿态的有效手段。

Method: 观察到车辆底盘会遮挡雷达射线形成独特的几何阴影，且阴影的稳定性可提供有用信息。提出利用目标雷达回波的边界与该阴影的交点，通过解析的闭式映射直接计算目标在面内的3D倾斜角度，无需对场景做额外假设。

Result: 在仿真和Navtech CTS350-X雷达上验证，该方法可在实际条件下估计倾斜角度。主要瓶颈在于雷达扫描中目标的准确分割。

Conclusion: 底盘阴影是一种新颖的几何线索，可将2D旋转雷达的能力从定位扩展到3D场景重建，为恶劣环境下的自主系统感知提供新思路。

Abstract: Reliable perception in adverse conditions remains challenging for autonomous systems, as cameras and LiDAR degrade in poor lighting or weather. Millimetre-wave FMCW radar is robust to such conditions, but its elevation collapse limits geometric reasoning. We observe that vehicle chassis occlude radar rays and form a distinctive geometric shadow, and its consistency can enable us to infer useful information about objects whose returns intersect this shadow. Motivated by this observation, we propose a method to recover the 3D, in-plane inclination of nearby slender vertical objects from this cue. The object inclination is retrieved without assumptions about the wider scene, but through an analytical, closed-form mapping between its radar return boundaries and the opening angle. Validation in simulation and experimentation on a Navtech CTS350-X radar shows that inclinations can be estimated under practical conditions, with segmentation of the object in the radar scan emerging as the main bottleneck. This work highlights chassis shadows as a novel geometric cue, extending the role of 2D rotating radar beyond localisation and toward 3D scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [150] [TacVerse: A Multi-Sensor Dataset and Benchmark for Cross-Sensor Vision-Based Tactile Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25877)
*Lan Wei,Gurmeher Khurana,Sirine Bhouri,Wenhao Hong,Zeyuan Xin,Qingzheng Cong,Wen Fan,Yanzheng Xiang,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacVerse是一个包含106,800张来自七种视觉触觉传感器的触觉图像数据集，支持形状分类、光栅分类和力回归三种下游任务。实验表明，尽管传感器内性能良好，但跨传感器零样本迁移性能显著下降，少样本适应虽能提升但无法达到传感器内上限，MAE预训练在不同任务和传感器上表现最稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉触觉传感器（VBTSs）能通过内部摄像头成像形变来推断接触几何和力信息，但不同传感器设计之间的泛化能力尚不明确，缺乏系统的跨传感器触觉感知研究。

Method: 构建了包含106,800张触觉图像的多传感器数据集TacVerse，涵盖七种VBTSs，设计了形状分类、光栅分类和力回归三个下游任务，在传感器内训练、零样本跨传感器迁移和少样本适应三种设置下进行实验评估。

Result: 传感器内各任务性能良好；直接跨传感器迁移性能大幅下降，形状分类相对鲁棒，光栅分类和力回归对传感器偏移更敏感；少样本适应能持续提升力回归在未见目标传感器上的性能，但无法完全弥合与传感器内上界的差距；MAE预训练在不同任务和传感器上提供最一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: TacVerse为研究触觉感知中的传感器偏移、数据高效适应和自监督学习提供了受控测试平台，揭示了跨传感器泛化面临的关键挑战及MAE预训练的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors (VBTSs) enable robots to infer contact geometry and force-related cues by imaging deformation through an internal camera, yet generalisation across sensor designs remains poorly understood. We present TacVerse, a multi-sensor dataset and benchmark for cross-sensor vision-based tactile perception. The dataset contains 106,800 tactile images from seven VBTSs and supports three downstream tasks: shape classification, grating classification, and force regression. Experiments are conducted under three settings: within-sensor training, zero-shot cross-sensor transfer, and few-shot adaptation. Strong within-sensor performance across all tasks indicates that the collected tactile observations are informative for the target objectives. Direct cross-sensor transfer, however, leads to substantial degradation. Shape classification is comparatively robust, whereas grating classification and force regression are more sensitive to sensor shift. Few-shot adaptation for force regression consistently improves performance on unseen target sensors but does not fully close the gap to within-sensor upper bounds. A representation study further shows that MAE (Masked Autoencoder) pretraining provides the most consistent gains across tasks and sensors. TacVerse provides a controlled testbed for studying sensor shift, data-efficient adaptation, and self-supervised learning in tactile perception.

</details>


### [151] [A 3D-Printable Dataset for Fair Testing and Comparisons of Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25886)
*Dexter R. Shepherd,Nicolas Herzig,Phil Husbands,Andrew Philippides,Chris Johnson,William Kimbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数学定义的3D可打印纹理数据集，通过六种参数化表面模式（正弦波和傅里叶函数组合）实现可控的空间频率、振幅和方向结构变化。实验使用光学TacTip传感器在三种3D打印机和多种耗材上评估纹理复现性，结果表明打印质量（尤其是峰值锐度和拉丝现象）影响触觉方差，高端打印机产生更一致的签名。分类实验显示高质量打印支持良好的打印机内泛化，但跨打印机泛化仍具挑战。该工作建立了首个公开可用的、物理可复现的3D打印纹理基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉纹理数据集主要依赖特定传感器与可用表面/物体的交互数据，而非描述纹理本身，这限制了不同触觉传感器之间的公平比较，阻碍了可重复研究。

Method: 设计六种参数化表面模式（基于正弦波和傅里叶函数），通过三种3D打印机和多种耗材制造，使用光学TacTip传感器在受控接触条件下采集图像，并采用神经网络和PCA模型进行分类实验评估可复现性。

Result: 打印质量（峰值锐度和拉丝现象）显著影响触觉信号方差；高端打印机产生更一致的触觉签名；高质量打印支持良好的打印机内泛化，但跨打印机泛化因几何不一致性而困难。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了首个公开可用、物理可复现的3D打印纹理基准，为触觉传感器的公平比较提供了基础平台。

Abstract: Existing texture datasets for tactile sensing primarily consist of sensor readings from a specific sensor interacting with available surfaces/objects rather than describing the textures themselves, limiting fair comparison between tactile sensors and hindering reproducible research. In this work, we introduce a 3D-printable dataset of mathematically defined textures designed to be fabricated reliably across different printers and filament types. The dataset consists of six parametrically generated surface patterns derived from combinations of sine-wave and Fourier-based functions, giving controlled variation in spatial frequency, amplitude, and directional structure. We evaluate the reproducibility of these textures across three popular 3D printers and multiple filament types by measuring variance in images captured using an optical TacTip sensor under controlled contact conditions. Our results show that print quality, particularly peak sharpness and stringing, affects tactile variance, with higher-end printers producing significantly more consistent signatures. Classification experiments using neural networks and PCA-based models further demonstrate that high-quality prints support strong within-printer generalisation, while cross-printer generalisation remains challenging due to geometric inconsistencies. This work establishes the first openly available, physically reproducible 3D-printed texture benchmark, providing a foundation for fair comparison of tactile sensors.

</details>


### [152] [DeformGen: Dynamics-Based Topology Augmentation for Deformable Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25939)
*Zili Lin,Wenyao Zhang,Yuyang Zhang,Zekun Qi,Junyan Lin,Hanxin Zhu,Jiaolong Yang,Zhibo Chen,Yao Mu,Xiaokang Yang,Xin Jin,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeformGen是一个基于动力学的数据增强框架，通过局部物理扰动和变形场扭曲，为可变形物体操作生成拓扑多样且物理合理的演示数据，有效提升策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演示增强方法在处理可变形物体操作时面临两个根本性挑战：(1)高维状态空间受物理约束，低维位姿扰动难以生成有效构型；(2)轨迹传递非等变性，变形时物质点不再刚性运动。因此需要一种能生成拓扑多样性增强数据的方法。

Method: DeformGen框架包含两个核心组件：(1)针对状态挑战，通过施加局部物理扰动并前向模拟动力学，获得拓扑连贯且物理合理的新变形状态；(2)针对轨迹挑战，利用变形场扭曲将源操作轨迹映射到新状态，将逐粒子位移提升为连续空间函数，使末端执行器轨迹与变形几何一致。

Result: 在高保真可变形操作基准上，DeformGen相比仅使用原始演示训练和刚性风格增强基线，普遍提升了策略学习效果。

Conclusion: DeformGen通过联合增强状态分布及其关联的操作行为，有效解决了可变形物体操作中的数据增强难题，为提升策略泛化能力提供了新范式。

Abstract: Demonstration augmentation is proposed for cost-efficient data acquisition, but existing methods are fundamentally limited in deformable manipulation due to two challenges: (1) the state space is high-dimensional with physics-induced constraints, making valid configurations impossible to reach via low-dimensional pose perturbations; and (2) trajectory transfer is non-equivariant, as material points no longer move rigidly together under deformation. We present DeformGen, a dynamics-based augmentation framework that achieves topological diversity for deformable objects. For the state challenge, DeformGen expands the valid state distribution by applying localized physical disturbances and forward-simulating the dynamics to obtain topology-coherent, physically plausible deformable states. For the trajectory challenge, DeformGen transfers source manipulation trajectories via deformation-field warping, which lifts per-particle displacements into a continuous spatial function to adapt the end-effector trajectory consistently with the deformed geometry. In this way, our method jointly augments the state distribution and its associated manipulation behavior. Experiments on high-fidelity deformable manipulation benchmarks show that DeformGen generally improves policy learning compared with training on the original demonstrations alone and with rigid-style augmentation baselines.

</details>


### [153] [DSP-SLAM++: A Unified Framework for Multi-Class, High-Fidelity Object SLAM in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25953)
*Ahmad Kourani,Ghina Daoud,Daniel Asmar,Imad Elhajj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DSP-SLAM++提出了一种异步建图管道和单目鱼眼-LiDAR传感器融合方案，在保持实时性能的同时，支持多类物体高保真几何重建，将最大物体处理延迟降低70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体感知SLAM系统在实时性、多类支持和高质量物体模型生成三者之间存在难以调和的权衡，亟需一种能兼顾三者的实用化方案。

Method: 在DSP-SLAM基础上引入异步建图管道以解耦前端跟踪与后端物体优化，并针对单目鱼眼相机与LiDAR的传感器套件进行专门的融合适配。

Result: 在25Hz多类数据集上实现鲁棒的实时性能，最大物体处理延迟相比基线降低高达70%，生成细粒度、几何完整的多类物体形状。

Conclusion: 该工作使高保真、多类物体SLAM在自动驾驶和机器人操作等实际应用中更具可行性，尤其适用于常见的鱼眼-LiDAR传感器平台。

Abstract: Existing object-aware SLAM systems force a trade-off between real-time performance, multi-class support, and the generation of high-fidelity, semantically coherent object models. To address this trade-off, we present DSP-SLAM++, which extends the DSP-SLAM framework with an asynchronous mapping pipeline for real-time performance and dedicated sensor fusion adaptations for a monocular fisheye-LiDAR suite. Experiments demonstrate that our system generates fine-grained, geometrically-complete shapes for multiple object classes while eliminating severe mapping thread bottlenecks by reducing maximum object processing latency by up to 70\% compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, enabling robust, real-time performance on a challenging 25 Hz multi-class datasets. This work makes high-fidelity, multi-class object SLAM more practical for real-world applications like autonomous driving and robotic manipulation by enabling its use on platforms with common fisheye-LiDAR sensor setups. The open-source code is available at: [github.com/AUBVRL/DSP-SLAMpp].

</details>


### [154] [From Rubble Simulation to Active Magnetic Mapping: Quantum Sensing for Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25957)
*Samuel Tovey,Stefan Prestel,Hiroshi Yamauchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于无人机的量子磁力计作为建筑坍塌废墟下幸存者定位的补充传感模态，通过Unreal Engine仿真钢筋混凝土停车场坍塌场景，结合每三角形偶极近似计算磁场，并利用高斯过程回归与贝叶斯主动采样进行空间重建。三传感器阵列在梯度分辨率与无人机载荷间取得最优平衡，约100个主动采样点可达结构相关性峰值，验证了量子级传感在无人机结构分析与空洞探测中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑坍塌后72小时内定位幸存者是灾难救援的关键挑战，现有传感模态仅能提供废墟下结构的局部信息，亟需一种能穿透废墟进行结构感知的互补性模态。

Method: 使用Unreal Engine生成钢筋混凝土停车场坍塌场景；基于每三角形偶极近似计算诱导磁场；采用多传感器稀疏采样结合高斯过程回归与贝叶斯主动采样实现空间重建；分析不同数量传感器阵列的性能权衡。

Result: 在屋顶线上方约1米处可恢复亚pT至亚nT范围的磁结构信息；三传感器阵列在梯度分辨率与无人机载荷间最优；主动采样约100个样本可达结构相关性峰值。

Conclusion: 量子级磁传感有望成为无人机结构分析与坍塌建筑空洞探测的有效工具。

Abstract: Locating survivors of building collapses within the first 72 hours is a critical challenge in disaster response, and existing sensing modalities provide only partial information about the structure beneath the rubble.
  This paper proposes drone-based quantum magnetometry as a complementary modality and develops a simulation pipeline spanning rubble physics, sensor-array deployment, and active spatial reconstruction. We use Unreal Engine to generate a steel-reinforced concrete parking-garage collapse and compute the induced magnetic field via a per-triangle dipole approximation, establishing that meaningful magnetic structure is recoverable in the sub-pT to sub-nT range from roughly 1 m above the roofline. Then, we feed sparse multi-sensor samples into a Gaussian Process Regression back-end driven by Bayesian active sampling and validate the pipeline across multiple independent collapse realizations; a three-sensor array optimizes the trade-off between gradient resolution and UAV payload constraints, and active sampling reaches peak structural correlation in roughly $100$ samples. Together, these results indicate that quantum-grade sensing could become a useful tool for drone-based structural analysis and potentially void detection in collapsed buildings.

</details>


### [155] [Mixture-of-Experts RL for Fault-Tolerant Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25965)
*Giulio Turrisi,Ozan Pali,Luca Oneto,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种故障感知模块化控制架构，利用故障诊断信息激活针对不同执行器故障模式的专用控制专家，在行星探测等远程环境中提升腿式机器人的鲁棒运动能力。实验表明，该模块化策略在性能上优于同等规模的 monolithic 策略，且在计算资源受限（如太空应用）场景下仍保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在行星探测等远程环境中的腿式机器人必须具备应对执行器故障和复杂地形的可靠运动能力。传统强化学习的 monolithic 策略难以高效表达应对不同故障条件所需的多样化控制策略。

Method: 提出故障感知模块化控制架构，显式利用故障诊断信息激活与特定执行器故障模式关联的专用控制专家模块。

Result: 显式故障条件化的模块化策略在各类故障场景下的运动性能始终优于同等规模的 monolithic 策略；即使在网络容量大幅降低的情况下，该架构仍保持竞争性能。

Conclusion: 模块化架构适用于计算受限的机器人平台（如太空应用），兼顾了故障鲁棒性与资源效率。

Abstract: Legged robots deployed in planetary exploration and other remote environments must maintain reliable locomotion despite actuator failures and challenging terrain conditions. Although reinforcement learning has achieved strong results in legged locomotion, monolithic policies can struggle to efficiently represent the diverse control strategies required to compensate for different fault conditions. In this work, we propose a fault-aware modular control architecture that explicitly leverages fault-diagnosis information to activate specialized control experts associated with distinct actuator failure modes. Experimental results show that explicit fault-conditioned modular policies consistently outperform monolithic policies of comparable size, achieving higher locomotion performance across failure scenarios. Moreover, the proposed modular architecture retains competitive performance even under significantly reduced network capacity, highlighting its suitability for compute-constrained robotic platforms, such as those typically employed in space applications. The code associated with this work is available at: https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/fault-locomotion-isaaclab.

</details>


### [156] [A Sensorised Lattice Footplate for a Semi-Active Prosthetic Foot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25966)
*Jinze Ge,Jingcheng Sun,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究将磁性足底传感直接嵌入低成本半主动假脚的承重柔性元件中，通过集成传感3D打印晶格足板、伺服可调液压阻尼器和降阶踝关节模型，验证了传感嵌入的可行性，并实现基于传感的半主动阻尼控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否将磁性足底传感直接嵌入假脚的承重柔性元件内部，以在无需外部鞋垫层的情况下实现足底力估计，从而简化半主动假脚的结构并降低成本。

Method: 设计并集成了传感器化的3D打印晶格足板、伺服可调液压阻尼器及降阶踝关节模型；通过实验表征阻尼器调整角度与阻尼系数的关系；进行压缩测试验证晶格刚度可调性；通过循环法向加载测试嵌入传感器的力追踪能力；在静态姿势试验中验证前足和后足载荷分布的可分离性；采用前馈阻尼调度策略跟踪参考踝关节轨迹。

Result: 嵌入传感器能追踪测试机参考力，实现足底力估计；静态试验中前足和后足载荷分布在四种站姿配置下可分离；前馈阻尼调度在早期到中期支撑相近似了参考踝关节背屈趋势，但纯耗散机制无法产生主动蹬伸。

Conclusion: 传感可嵌入假脚承重柔性元件内部并用于驱动半主动阻尼控制，为低成本半主动假脚设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates whether magnetic plantar sensing can be embedded directly inside the load-bearing compliant element of a low-cost semi-active prosthetic foot. We present a prototype integrating a sensorised 3D-printed lattice footplate, a servo-adjustable hydraulic damper, and a reduced-order ankle model. The damper is experimentally characterised to relate adjustment angle to damping coefficient. Controlled compression tests show tunable lattice stiffness, while cyclic normal loading shows that the embedded sensor tracks the testing-machine reference force, supporting plantar-force estimation without an external insole layer. Static-posture trials under approximately body-weight loading show that forefoot and rearfoot loading distributions are separable across four prescribed stance configurations, providing a preliminary check of the sensing pipeline. A feedforward damping schedule approximates the dorsiflexion trend of a reference ankle trajectory through early-to-mid stance, while exposing the expected limitation that a purely dissipative mechanism cannot generate active push-off. Together, these results demonstrate that sensing can be embedded inside the load-bearing compliant element of a prosthetic foot and used to drive semi-active damping.

</details>


### [157] [Action ControlNet: A Lightweight Delay-Aware Adapter for Smooth Asynchronous Control in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25985)
*Tiecheng Guo,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Action ControlNet (ACNet)，一种轻量级延迟感知适配器，通过已执行动作后缀作为残差条件，在不重新训练完整策略或修改架构的前提下，解决VLA模型异步执行中的动作不连续和抖动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在通用机器人操作中展现出强大潜力，但其推理延迟阻碍了稳定高频控制。现有异步执行方法会导致动作片段拼接不连续、抖动及接触丰富操作失败，而现有方案需要完整策略重训练或特定架构运行时逻辑。

Method: 提出Action ControlNet (ACNet)，一种延迟感知适配器，将已执行动作后缀作为残差条件，作用于大部分冻结的动作头上。ACNet保持预训练骨干网络不变，引入极少可训练参数，兼容扩散模型和流匹配等生成式动作头。

Result: 在Kinetix、Meta-World MT50和真实世界SO-ARM101平台上，ACNet在推理延迟下提升了鲁棒性，生成比直接片段拼接更平滑的异步轨迹，且比完整延迟条件重训练更轻量。

Conclusion: ACNet通过轻量级延迟感知适配有效解决了VLA模型异步执行中的动作不连续问题，兼顾了性能提升与计算效率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation, but their inference latency remains a major obstacle to stable high-frequency control. Asynchronous execution mitigates this bottleneck by overlapping policy inference with action execution, yet the next action chunk is still predicted from stale observations while the robot continues to move. Direct chunk stitching therefore introduces handoff discontinuities, action jitter, and failures in contact-rich manipulation. Existing remedies typically require either full-policy retraining or architecture-specific runtime logic. This work proposes Action ControlNet (ACNet), a lightweight delay-aware adapter that uses the executed motion suffix as a residual condition for a mostly frozen action head. ACNet leaves the pretrained backbone unchanged, introduces few trainable parameters, and remains compatible with generative action heads such as diffusion and flow matching. On Kinetix, Meta-World MT50, and a real-world SO-ARM101 platform, ACNet improves robustness under inference delay and yields smoother asynchronous trajectories than direct chunk stitching, while remaining more lightweight than full delay-conditioned retraining.

</details>


### [158] [FORCE: Efficient VLA Reinforcement Fine-Tuning via Value-Calibrated Warm-up and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26006)
*Shuyi Zhang,Yunfan Lou,Hongyang Cheng,Yichen Guo,Chuyao Fu,Yaoxu Lyu,Xiaojie Zhang,Haoran Li,Pengwei Wang,Zhongyuan Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FORCE是一个三阶段框架，通过值校准预热和基于Q函数的动作过滤机制，解决了VLA模型在强化学习微调中的样本效率低下和初始化遗忘问题，在仿真和真实任务中取得了显著的效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型受限于次优数据的模仿天花板，而RL微调虽然能突破这一限制但样本效率极低，主要原因包括Q函数不稳定导致的灾难性初始遗忘，以及低质量探索数据引发的低效策略更新，常需昂贵的人工干预。

Method: FORCE包含三个阶段：(1)值校准预热阶段，利用在策略 rollout 缓解Q函数的分布偏移；(2)在线阶段，使用校准后的Q函数作为过滤器，筛选策略自身的动作提案和专家数据，仅保留高价值动作用于策略更新。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界任务中，FORCE实现了79%的成功率绝对提升，相比先前RL方法高出10%，训练速度提升32.5%，且无需人工干预。

Conclusion: FORCE有效缓解了微调初期成功率下降的问题，在无需人工干预的情况下实现鲁棒的自主性能，为部署自主机器人智能体迈出重要一步。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often constrained by the imitation ceiling imposed by sub-optimal data. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) fine-tuning can surpass this limit, it is notoriously sample inefficient. This challenge arises from two core issues: (1) catastrophic initial unlearning due to an unstable Q-function and (2) inefficient policy updates caused by low-quality exploration data, often forcing a reliance on costly human interventions. We introduce FORCE, a 3-stage framework that stabilizes fine-tuning by tackling both issues. FORCE first incorporates a Value-Calibrated Warm-Up phase, utilizing on-policy rollouts to mitigate the distributional shift of the Q-function. Subsequently, during the online stage, this calibrated Q-function acts as a filter for both the policy's own action proposals and expert data, ensuring only high-value actions are used for the policy update. We evaluate FORCE on various simulation and real-world tasks, and the result shows that FORCE achieves a 79% absolute improvement in success rates and outperform prior RL methods by 10%, while accelerating training by 32.5%. Critically, it mitigates the common success rate drop and achieves this robust performance without human intervention, marking a significant step towards deploying capable and autonomous robotic agents.

</details>


### [159] [Emcar: Embodied Controller for Animating Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26008)
*Carlos Gomez Cubero,Elizabeth Jochum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMCAR是一个利用木偶戏和绘画等艺术实践来编程机器人运动的新型无代码软件工具，旨在让艺术家和非技术背景人员能参与人机交互研究与协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人编程工具对技术门槛要求高，限制了艺术家和非技术背景人员参与人机交互（HRI）研究与创作，缺乏能够利用艺术实践独特优势的直观编程平台。

Method: 开发了名为EMCAR的无代码软件平台，通过引入木偶戏和绘画等艺术实践的独特可供性（affordances），使机器人运动编程变得直观和可访问。

Result: EMCAR扩展了协作机器人的创意应用场景，使机器人直接掌握在艺术家手中，并为缺乏技术背景的个人提供了参与协作和机器人研究的有意义途径。

Conclusion: 该无代码平台成功降低了机器人编程的技术门槛，为跨学科协作和机器人研究提供了更加包容的环境。

Abstract: This chapter describes EMCAR, a novel software tool for programming robot motion that leverages the unique affordances of artistic practices such as puppetry and drawing to conceive, design, and program novel interactions and realize new use cases for HRI. The advantage of this no-code platform is that it expands creative applications for collaborative robots - putting robots directly in the hands of artists - and provides an inclusive environment that enables individuals with little or no technical backgrounds to engage meaningfully in collaborations and robotics research.

</details>


### [160] [Verifiable Foundation Models for Robot Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23754)
*Davide Corsi,Kyungmin Kim,Roy Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FEARL通过将机器人控制策略分解为负责感知与推理的大型控制器(C)和负责低维安全观测的小型安全模块(S)，实现了对基础模型的形式化验证。S接收专用安全传感器数据及C的有界上下文嵌入，使得碰撞避免等安全需求可被现有验证工具处理，同时保留C的表达能力。实验表明该框架在多种仿真环境和实体机器人上均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在机器人控制中具备强大的多模态感知能力，但其黑箱特性使其难以通过现有形式化验证工具进行分析，导致安全关键场景下的部署存在风险。

Method: 提出FEARL架构，将策略分解为大型Controller（高维感知与任务推理）和小型Safety module（基于安全传感器低维观测和有界上下文嵌入输出最终动作），通过将形式化验证限定于S模块而非完整基础模型，实现可处理的验证。

Result: 在三个模拟机器人域中使用多种Controller骨干网络（包括预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型）评估FEARL，并将学到的策略迁移至实体机器人，验证了低维安全接口支持实际的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: FEARL通过架构分解在保留基础模型任务推理能力的同时，使得形式化验证变得可行，是一种保障机器人安全部署的有效框架。

Abstract: Deploying foundation models for robot control raises a central challenge: the expressive power that enables rich, multimodal perception also makes these models opaque and difficult to analyze formally, rendering them intractable for existing verification tools. In this paper, we present FEARL (Foundation-Enabled Assured Robot Learning), a framework that addresses this tension through a modular architectural decomposition. FEARL separates the policy into a large Controller (C) responsible for high-dimensional perception and task reasoning, and a small Safety module (S) that receives low-dimensional observations from dedicated safety sensors together with a bounded context embedding from C and produces the final action. Since many robot safety requirements, such as collision avoidance and workspace boundary constraints, can be expressed over these safety sensor observations, formal verification can be applied to S rather than to the full foundation-model backbone. This makes formal analysis tractable with existing tools while preserving the Controller's expressive power for task reasoning. To show that the decomposed policy remains capable of solving diverse tasks, we evaluate FEARL on three simulated robotic domains using multiple Controller backbones and training procedures, including pretrained off-the-shelf vision-language-action models. We further transfer the learned policy from one of our simulated tasks to a physical robot, suggesting that the low-dimensional safety interface supports practical sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [161] [FAR-LIO: Enabling High-Speed Autonomy through Fast, Accurate, and Robust LiDAR-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26010)
*Maximilian Leitenstern,Marcel Weinmann,Patrick Haft,Tobias Lasser,Dominik Kulmer,Markus Lienkamp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FAR-LIO，一个基于CUDA加速的激光雷达-惯性里程计框架，通过CUDA体素哈希映射、稀疏感知GICP算法和EKF后端的上采样延迟补偿策略，在高速自主赛车场景（最高250 km/h）中实现了比现有方法平均误差降低6.9%、运行时间降低38.4%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度动态运动和传感器噪声环境中（如自主赛车），里程计估计面临挑战，而低延迟里程计对于稳定闭环控制至关重要。现有方法难以同时满足快速、准确和鲁棒的需求。

Method: 1) 基于CUDA的体素哈希映射实现并行化近邻搜索和高效地图更新；2) 稀疏感知GICP算法结合自适应阈值，在CUDA体素哈希映射上实现低延迟高精度；3) EKF后端采用上采样和延迟补偿策略融合激光雷达里程计与高频IMU数据。

Result: 在四种不同传感器设置、公开数据集和两辆最高250 km/h的自主赛车上评估，相比基准方法，位置误差平均降低6.9%，运行时间降低38.4%，且仅需单一参数集。

Conclusion: FAR-LIO在计算效率和广泛适用性方面优于现有方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Robust and accurate odometry estimation is essential in modern robotics. In environments characterized by highly dynamic motion and sensor noise, odometry estimation becomes increasingly challenging. Autonomous racing combines both factors in an unstructured setting, where minimizing odometry latency is essential for stable closed-loop control. This paper introduces FAR-LIO, a highly optimized CUDA-accelerated LiDAR-inertial odometry framework developed for Fast, Accurate, and Robust performance. Our system leverages a novel CUDA-based voxel hashmap to enable parallelized nearest-neighbor search and efficient map updates. We employ a sparsity-aware Generalized Iterative Closest Point algorithm with adaptive thresholding on top of the CUDA-based voxel hashmap with adaptive density to achieve low-latency without compromising accuracy. An Extended Kalman Filter serves as a robust backend. It utilizes an upsampling and delay compensation strategy to fuse the LiDAR odometry with high-frequency IMU data, thereby ensuring a robust and smooth odometry output. We evaluate FAR-LIO across four different sensor setups, using both public datasets and data from two autonomous racecars driving at speeds of up to 250 km/h. FAR-LIO achieves an average 6.9% reduction in the positional error and 38.4% lower runtime compared to state-of-the-art baselines on target hardware using a single parameter set. This demonstrates its computational efficiency and broad applicability. To build upon our work, our code is available open-source on https://github.com/TUMFTM/FAR-LIO.

</details>


### [162] [RE4: Transformation-aware Imitation of Object Interactions Using Manipulation Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24403)
*Arsh Chawla,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RE4框架，通过轻量级自监督位姿估计、操作模式感知的演示检索与变换、重规划及演示执行四个可解释模块，在Push-T和Robomimic基准上实现与端到端方法相当的性能，同时保持可解释性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端模仿学习方法（扩散/流模型）性能优异但缺乏可解释性，而基于物体中心或位姿的方法虽可解释但性能受限。旨在融合经典操作理论的原理性方法，同时保持性能和可解释性。

Method: RE4框架包含四个步骤：(1) 对图像观测进行轻量级自监督无模型目标位姿估计；(2) 基于操作模式感知的演示检索；(3) 模式感知的演示变换；(4) 保持模式约束的重规划连接至检索点，最终执行变换后的演示。

Result: 在Push-T和Robomimic的状态和图像基准上评估，在图像Push-T的稀疏数据对抗基准和低数据实验中展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 使用简单可解释的构建模块学习操作技能具有前景，在性能和可解释性之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Object interaction tasks have been a focus of advances in imitation learning. End-to-end methods, dominated by diffusion and flow-based variants have shown leaps in performance while sacrificing interpretability. Object-centric and pose-informed variants have had a role in learning from demonstration in manipulation tasks. In this paper, we revisit a few modern imitation learning benchmarks for object interactions, with the aim of composing a framework that repurposes principled theories of manipulation, preserving both performance and interpretability. For image observations, lightweight training is proposed for model-free pose estimation of the target object, using self-supervision over the demonstration data available for imitation learning. This information is then used to inform a manipulation mode-aware retrieval of a demonstration, a mode-aware transformation, a replan step that connects to the retrieval point while preserving mode constraints, and finally rolling out the transformed demonstration. These compose four key steps of the proposed RE4 framework, evaluated over state-based and image-based benchmarks in Push-T and Robomimic. An adversarial benchmark that evaluates sparse data regions of image-based Push-T showcases the robustness, further bolstered by indications from low-data regime experiments. The current work shows promise in using simple interpretable building blocks to learn manipulation skills.

</details>


### [163] [G2DP: Diffusion Planning with Spatio-Temporal Grid Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26017)
*Hang Yu,Ye Jin,Alessandro Canevaro,Julian Schmidt,Julian Jordan,Peizheng Li,Marc Kaufeld,Silvan Lindner,Johannes Betz,Wilhelm Stork*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: G2DP是一种基于扩散模型的自动驾驶规划器，通过构建可微的时空代价体积，在去噪过程中注入密集的环境约束梯度，实现了在nuPlan、interPlan和DeepScenario等基准上的最优性能，相较于最强模仿学习基线在反应分数上提升7.2分，碰撞避免提升10.15分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散规划器在稠密交互交通中的随机性导致安全性和路线遵循能力不足，而现有引导方法依赖稀疏几何查询或事后优化，情景感知有限且在交互场景中表现脆弱。

Method: 构建可微分时空代价体积，融合概率性未来占用分布与路线进展地图，将其建模为连续安全能量泛函，在去噪循环中注入密集梯度，主动引导轨迹生成趋向无碰撞和进展最优区域。

Result: 在nuPlan上达到SOTA，反应分数超越最强模仿学习基线7.2分；零样本迁移至interPlan和DeepScenario保持最优，其中在interPlan上碰撞避免比无引导方法提升10.15分。

Conclusion: 时空代价网格是扩散规划中实现鲁棒引导的有效表示方式。

Abstract: In autonomous driving, diffusion-based planners have emerged as a promising paradigm for robust motion planning in dense and interactive traffic, as they can effectively model diverse driving behaviors. However, their inherent stochasticity often requires explicit guidance during denoising to ensure safety and route adherence for robust closed-loop execution. Existing guidance typically relies on sparse, entity-centric geometric queries or post-hoc refinement, yielding limited situational awareness and fragile performance in interactive scenes. To address this issue, we propose G2DP (Grid-Guided Diffusion Planning), a diffusion-based planner that directly enforces dense environmental constraints through inference-time guidance. Specifically, G2DP constructs a differentiable spatio-temporal cost volume by fusing probabilistic future occupancy distributions with a route-progress map. By formulating this volume as a continuous safety energy functional, it injects dense gradients directly into the denoising loop, actively steering trajectory generation toward collision-free and progress-optimal regions. Extensive closed-loop evaluations show that G2DP achieves state-of-the-art performance on nuPlan, outperforming the strongest imitation-learning baseline by +7.2 points in reactive score. It further maintains top scores in zero-shot transfers to interPlan and DeepScenario benchmarks, with collision avoidance improving by +10.15 over the unguided approach on interPlan. These results demonstrate that spatio-temporal cost grids serve as an effective representation for robust guidance in diffusion-based planning.

</details>


### [164] [In-Context World Modeling for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26025)
*Siyin Wang,Junhao Shi,Senyu Fei,Zhaoyang Fu,Li Ji,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ICWM框架，通过上下文窗口处理短暂的自生成任务无关交互序列，使VLA模型自动推断系统变量，实现对新颖相机视角和机器人形态的零样本泛化，无需参数更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型仅以当前观测和语言指令为条件，忽略底层系统配置变量，导致在新场景（如改变相机视角或机器人形态）中泛化能力差，需要大量数据微调。

Method: 将系统辨识建模为上下文自适应问题：模型在任务执行前处理一段短暂的自生成、任务无关交互历史，通过上下文窗口隐式捕获当前系统的世界动力学，从而无需参数更新即可适应新配置。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人平台上的实验表明，ICWM在新颖相机视角下显著优于标准VLA基线方法。

Conclusion: ICWM通过上下文学习实现系统辨识，有效解决了VLA模型在部署环境变化时的泛化瓶颈，为机器人策略的快速自适应提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often fail to generalize to novel setups, such as altered camera viewpoints or robot morphologies, because they are typically conditioned only on current observations and language instructions. By ignoring the underlying system configuration as a variable, these models implicitly assume a fixed execution context encountered during training, necessitating data-intensive fine-tuning for any new environment. In this work, we introduce In-Context World Modeling (ICWM), a framework that treats system identification as an in-context adaptation problem. ICWM enables robot policies to autonomously infer essential system variables from a short history of self-generated, task-agnostic interactions. Unlike traditional In-Context Learning that uses demonstrations to specify what task to perform, ICWM leverages the context window to understand how the system operates. By processing these interactions before task execution, the model implicitly captures the world dynamics of the current system, enabling adaptation to novel configurations without parameter updates. Extensive experiments in simulation and on real-world robot platforms demonstrate that ICWM significantly outperforms standard VLA baselines on novel camera viewpoints.

</details>


### [165] [InSight: Self-Guided Skill Acquisition via Steerable VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24884)
*Maggie Wang,Lars Osterberg,Stephen Tian,Ola Shorinwa,Jiajun Wu,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InSight是一个让VLA模型通过原语级控制实现自主技能获取的框架，包含自动化分割流程和VLM引导的数据飞轮，无需人工演示即可学习新技能并组合执行长时序任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的能力受限于训练数据中的演示技能，无法自主获取训练数据中未包含的新技能。

Method: (1) 自动化分割流程：通过VLM规划分解和末端执行器位姿将演示分割为带标签的原语，实现VLA原语可控性；(2) VLM引导的数据飞轮：识别缺失原语、自主尝试演示、自动标注存储成功演示并整合到训练集。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务（如翻块、关抽屉、清扫、扭转、倾倒）中成功学习新技能，无需人工演示即可组合执行长时序任务。

Conclusion: 原语级可控性为VLA策略的持续技能获取提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can learn manipulation skills from demonstrations, but their capabilities are bounded by the skills in the training data. We present InSight, a framework that unlocks autonomous skill acquisition by rendering VLAs steerable at the primitive-action level (e.g., "move gripper to the bowl", "lift upward", "pour the bottle"). InSight consists of two primary stages: (1) an automated segmentation pipeline that partitions demonstrations into labeled primitives via VLM plan decomposition and end-effector poses to enable VLA primitive steerability, and (2) a VLM-guided data flywheel that identifies missing primitives required to accomplish a novel task, autonomously attempts demonstrations of the missing primitives with VLM-proposed low-level control, and automatically labels, stores, and integrates successful demonstrations into the VLA training set. We evaluate InSight across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, including block flipping, drawer closing, sweeping, twisting, and pouring, without any human demonstrations of these target skills. Once learned, these primitives can be composed to execute novel, long-horizon tasks without additional human demonstrations. Our findings demonstrate that primitive steerability provides a practical foundation for continual skill acquisition in VLA policies. Project website: https://insight-vla.github.io.

</details>


### [166] [RoboAtlas: Contextual Active SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26046)
*Alexander Schperberg,Shivam K. Panda,Abraham P. Vinod,M. K. Jawed,Stefano Di Cairano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAtlas是一个上下文感知的主动SLAM框架，通过可扩展的3D语义映射系统OpenRoboVox，自适应地平衡几何探索与语义推理。它利用上下文多臂赌博机，从探索过渡到语义引导导航。在大型真实环境（超1800m²，约3万个语义实例）中实现100%任务成功率，在GOAT-Bench基准上达到90.6%的SOTA成功率，使用小模型Qwen2.5-VL-7B仍达88.8%，优于所有使用GPT-4o的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动SLAM方法通常分离几何探索和语义推理，缺乏上下文感知的自适应平衡机制，且难以将大规模3D语义地图与大语言模型/视觉语言模型有效结合，导致在复杂真实环境中导航效率低下。

Method: 提出RoboAtlas框架，包含三个核心组件：前沿探索、全局语义地图推理和以自我为中心的VLM推理，通过上下文多臂赌博机（contextual multi-armed bandit）自适应地动态选择策略，从纯探索过渡到语义引导导航。底层基于可扩展的3D语义映射系统OpenRoboVox。

Result: 在超1800m²真实环境（约3万语义实例）中100%任务成功率；GOAT-Bench "Val Unseen"基准上使用GPT-4o达90.6% SR（比最强基线高17.8个百分点），使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B达88.8% SR，优于所有使用GPT-4o的基线方法。

Conclusion: 将基础模型与大尺度3D语义地图相结合，能够实现鲁棒且高效的上下文主动SLAM；语义映射框架带来的信息增益比简单地替换底层基础模型更为重要。

Abstract: We present RoboAtlas, a contextual Active SLAM framework that adaptively balances geometric exploration and semantic reasoning using a scalable 3D semantic mapping system, OpenRoboVox. RoboAtlas integrates frontier exploration, global semantic-map reasoning, and egocentric VLM-based reasoning through a contextual multi-armed bandit that transitions from exploration to semantically guided navigation as scene understanding improves. We evaluate the system in simulation and on a Unitree Go2 robot in large-scale real-world environments exceeding 1800 m2 with approx. 30k mapped semantic instances, achieving a 100% task success rate. On the GOAT-Bench "Val Unseen" benchmark, RoboAtlas achieves state-of-the-art performance with highest reported success rate (SR) of 90.6%, using GPT-4o, improving over the strongest prior baseline by 17.8 percentage points in SR. Using the much smaller Qwen2.5-VL-7B model, it still achieves 88.8% SR, outperforming all baselines using GPT-4o in SR, and revealing the importance of the information gained by our semantic mapping framework over simply replacing the underlying foundation model. The results demonstrate that grounding foundation models with large-scale 3D semantic maps enables robust and efficient contextual Active SLAM.

</details>


### [167] [Learning Robot Visual Navigation in Crowds via Intention-Aware Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26047)
*Han Bao,Bingyi Xia,Hanjing Ye,Yu Zhan,Hao Cheng,Baozhi Jia,Wenjun Xu,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出iCrowdNav，一种基于视觉的人群导航方法，通过意图感知的场景表示（包含时空编码器和Intent-Interact Former模块）从自我中心视觉观察中编码行为与结构上下文，以改进深度强化学习导航策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL导航方法将行人简化为2D点，忽略了丰富的视觉线索，无法有效捕捉人类意图和环境结构约束。

Method: 提出iCrowdNav，包含两个核心组件：1) 时空编码器提取场景占据特征；2) Intent-Interact Former (I² Former) 基于注意力机制编码人体姿态以推断行人运动意图。两者集成到紧凑状态嵌入中支持DRL策略训练。

Result: 在多项实验中对基线方法取得优越性能，并在真实场景部署中验证了基于视觉的人群导航能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入视觉线索和意图感知的场景表示，iCrowdNav有效提升了机器人在人群中的导航性能，验证了从视觉观测中学习行人意图的可行性。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires the ability to infer human intentions while accounting for the structural constraints of the environment. Currently, deep reinforcement learning (DRL) provides a promising method for learning navigation policies that understand human intentions. However, most of them rely on limited scene representations, treating pedestrians as simple 2D points and ignoring rich visual cues from both humans and the environment. To address this issue, we introduce iCrowdNav, a novel visual crowd navigation method with intention-aware scene representations, to encode behavioral and structural context from egocentric visual observations. Our method employs two key components: a spatio-temporal encoder for extracting occupancy features of the scene, and Intent-Interact Former (I$^2$ Former), an attention-based module that encodes human poses to infer pedestrians' motion intentions. These features are integrated into a compact state embedding that supports effective DRL policy training. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves superior performance over baselines, and real-world deployment demonstrates vision-based crowd navigation.

</details>


### [168] [Deep Reinforcement Learning-Enhanced Event-Triggered Data-Driven Predictive Control for a 3D Cable-Driven Soft Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26048)
*Cheng Ouyang,Moeen Ul Islam,Kaixiang Zhang,Zhaojian Li,Xiaobo Tan,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对软体机器人控制中DeePC计算开销大的问题，提出一种基于强化学习的自适应事件触发DeePC框架（RL-ET-DeePC），通过训练无模型RL策略决定何时调用DeePC优化器，在保持闭环性能的同时将优化频率降低66%（仿真）和34%（硬件实验）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人具有非线性和时变动力学特性，难以控制。DeePC作为无模型替代方案，但其滚动时域优化需要在每个采样时刻求解约束优化问题，计算量大，难以在资源受限的机器人平台上实时部署。

Method: 提出RL-ET-DeePC框架，训练一个无模型强化学习策略，根据当前系统状态表示决定何时触发DeePC优化器，从而减少不必要的优化调用。在三维绳驱软体机械臂上进行仿真和硬件实验验证。

Result: 仿真中优化频率降低66%且跟踪精度相当；硬件实验中实现零样本迁移，优化频率降低34%，跟踪精度与周期性DeePC相当，且性能比静态阈值事件触发基线更稳定。

Conclusion: RL-ET-DeePC能有效减少DeePC的优化计算负担，实现资源受限平台上的实时软体机器人控制，且具有零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Soft robots are challenging to control due to their nonlinear and time-varying dynamics. Data-enabled predictive control (DeePC) offers a model-free alternative by directly leveraging measured input-output trajectories to construct a predictive controller. However, its receding-horizon formulation requires solving a constrained optimization problem at every sampling instant, which can be computationally demanding for real-time deployment on resource-limited robotic platforms.To address this limitation, we propose an adaptive reinforcement-learning-based event-triggered DeePC (RL-ET-DeePC) framework for soft robotic control. A model-free RL policy is trained to determine when to invoke the DeePC optimizer based on the current system state representation, thereby reducing unnecessary optimization calls while preserving closed-loop performance.Simulation results show that RL-ET-DeePC reduces optimization frequency by up to 66% compared to periodic DeePC, while maintaining comparable tracking accuracy. Hardware experiments on a three-dimensional cable-driven soft robotic arm demonstrate zero-shot transfer, achieving a 34% reduction in optimization frequency with tracking accuracy comparable to periodic DeePC and more consistent performance than a static threshold-based event-triggered baseline.

</details>


### [169] [ForceBand: Learning Forceful Manipulation with sEMG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26093)
*Botao He,Zhi Wang,Linna Kuang,Ishaan Ghosh,Jitendra Malik,Cornelia Fermuller,Tingfan Wu,Jiayuan Mao,Ruoshi Liu,Haozhi Qi,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ForceBand，一种低成本腕戴式表面肌电（sEMG）系统，通过预测手指力为机器人操作提供力增强的示教数据，在力敏感任务中达到87%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人示教数据来源（如动捕、网络视频）主要捕捉运动与外观信息，缺少关键接触力信息，难以用于力敏感操作任务的策略学习。

Method: 收集10小时多模态数据集（包含第一视角视频、sEMG、IMU、指尖力测量），预先训练EMG2Force模型从sEMG和IMU信号预测每根手指的力；用户使用ForceBand和视频收集示教数据后，由EMG2Force模型标注力的时间序列，生成力增强的演示数据用于机器人策略学习。

Result: ForceBand恢复精细的指尖交互，力预测误差比视觉基线降低50%以上；在需要物体特定力控制的抓取、挤压、放置任务中，对多种形状、尺寸、重量的物体达到87%的成功率。

Conclusion: ForceBand以低成本方式将人类肌电信号转化为富含力的示教数据，显著提升机器人力敏感操作策略的学习效果。

Abstract: Human demonstrations are a scalable data source for learning robot manipulation policies. However, common sources of human demonstration data, such as motion-capture trajectories and internet videos, capture mostly motion and appearance while missing the contact forces that are critical for force-sensitive manipulation. In this paper, we introduce ForceBand, a low-cost wrist-worn sEMG system that turns human muscle activity into force-enriched demonstrations. We first collect a 10-hour multimodal dataset containing egocentric video, sEMG, IMU, and fingertip force measurements across diverse actions and objects. Using this dataset, we pre-train an EMG2Force model that predicts per-finger forces from sEMG and IMU signals. After a short user-specific calibration, users can collect target-task demonstrations using only ForceBand and video; EMG2Force then labels these demonstrations with per-finger force traces, producing force-augmented demonstrations for robot policy learning. Experiments show that ForceBand recovers fine-grained fingertip interactions with over 50% lower force prediction error than vision-based baselines and achieves an 87% success rate on pick, squeeze, and place tasks that require object-specific force control across objects with diverse shapes, sizes, and weights. Project website: https://forceband-emg.github.io

</details>


### [170] [Learning Action Priors for Cross-embodiment Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26095)
*Dong Jing,Tianqi Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Jinman Zhao,Zelong Sun,Li Erran Li,Zhiwu Lu,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段训练框架：先通过流匹配（flow-matching）在动作轨迹上预训练动作模块以获取跨具身时间运动先验，再将其迁移至VLA训练中，通过解码器复用和潜在蒸馏对齐视觉-语言与动作空间，显著提升13项跨具身任务的收敛速度、成功率和数据稀缺场景性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将动作模块附加到VLM骨干上联合优化，继承了视觉与语言先验但动作模块几乎从零学习物理运动，缺乏显式运动先验，导致早期优化需同时探索时序动作动态与跨模态对齐，在跨具身场景中问题更为突出。

Method: 两阶段训练框架：Stage 1使用轻量级流匹配编码器-解码器动作模块，仅在无条件动作轨迹上学习时序运动结构，不处理视觉或语言token；Stage 2通过解码器复用和早期潜在蒸馏将所学先验迁移至VLA训练，对齐视觉-语言特征与动作嵌入空间，同时允许端到端策略微调；训练好的编码器作为紧凑历史压缩器，将状态-动作历史汇总为单一时序上下文token。

Result: 在13项跨具身任务（模拟与真实平台）上验证有效性；相比无动作先验的VLA训练，模型收敛更快、成功率更高，在数据稀缺的真实世界任务上性能显著更强；扩展Stage 1动作数据可得到更泛化的动作先验，直接提升下游VLA性能。

Conclusion: 在VLA对齐前预训练动作模块以注入运动先验，是一种简单有效的范式改进，能加速收敛、提升成功率并增强数据效率，且动作先验可随数据规模扩展而持续获益。

Abstract: Most Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models build on a Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone by attaching an action module and optimizing the full policy jointly. This design inherits strong visual and linguistic priors from the VLM, but leaves the action module to learn physical motion almost from scratch. As a result, the policy lacks an explicit motion prior, forcing early optimization to simultaneously discover temporal action dynamics and cross-modal alignment, a challenge further amplified in cross-embodiment settings. In this work, we propose to pretrain the action module with motion priors before cross-modal VLA alignment. Specifically, we introduce a two-stage training framework that equips the action module with cross-embodiment temporal motion structure before VLA training begins. In Stage~1, a lightweight flow-matching-based encoder-decoder action module efficiently learns temporal motion structure solely from unconditioned action trajectories, without processing visual or language tokens. In Stage~2, this learned prior is transferred to VLA training through decoder reuse and early-stage latent distillation, aligning visual-language features with the action embedding space while still allowing end-to-end policy refinement. In addition, the trained encoder serves as a compact history compressor, summarizing state-action histories into a single temporal context token for history-aware modeling at negligible cost. Extensive experiments across 13 diverse cross-embodiment tasks on both simulated and real-world platforms validate the effectiveness of our approach. Compared with VLA training without action priors, our model achieves faster convergence, higher success rates, and substantially stronger performance on data-scarce real-world tasks. Moreover, scaling up the action data in Stage~1 yields a more generalizable action prior that directly improves downstream VLA performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Unprivileged Topology Certificates for Cloud GPU Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24934)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种纯软件的可信认证方法，通过CUDA探针测量SM-内存区域延迟矩阵、流式摘要提交证书，在不依赖特权访问或厂商密钥的情况下，验证云GPU的身份、型号和大致地理位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云GPU租户只能看到型号和区域，但无法直接检查实际运行的物理加速器，存在身份欺诈、型号降级、物理位置欺骗等安全风险。

Method: 使用CUDA探针测量SM-by-内存区域延迟矩阵（基于物理SM标签和依赖全局加载），通过流式缩减器提交充分统计量、配置、代码哈希、网络证据和压缩原始数据存档到证书中；验证者无需GPU即可检查证书，支持三类声明：每SM延迟图的物理指纹稳定性、缓存旁路HBM扫描的硬件拓扑恢复、公共网络地标绑定位置。

Result: RTX 5090六小时满载运行中位时间抖动0.09周期，留一法分类100.0%准确区分Blackwell芯片；恢复Volta V100统一内存域、Hopper H200双L2分区、Blackwell B200两芯NV-HBI封装（74/74 SM分区，30周期/15.5ns跨芯延迟）；B200运行中169个RIPE Atlas探针将服务器定位在44km内，拒绝全部11个诱饵站点。

Conclusion: 该纯软件方法能够在无特权访问或厂商密钥的条件下，有效验证云GPU的身份、类别和粗略地理位置，为云GPU租户提供了一种实用的可信认证手段。

Abstract: Cloud GPU tenants receive a model name and a region, but cannot directly inspect the physical accelerator that runs their job. We present a software-only attestation primitive for this setting. A CUDA probe measures an SM-by-memory-region latency matrix using physical SM labels and dependent global loads. A streaming reducer commits sufficient statistics, configuration, code hashes, network evidence, and a compressed raw data archive into a certificate that a verifier can check without a GPU. The certificate supports three claims. First, the per-SM latency map is a stable physical fingerprint. Over a six-hour full-load RTX 5090 run, its median temporal jitter is 0.09 cycles, while shape-only leave-one-out classification separates distinct Blackwell dies with 100.0% accuracy. Second, cache-bypassing HBM sweeps recover hardware-class topology across generations, including a unified Volta V100 memory domain, a two-way Hopper H200 L2 split, and a Blackwell B200 two-die NV-HBI package whose 74/74 SM partition carries a 30-cycle, 15.5 ns cross-die penalty. Third, public network landmarks bind the same certificate to a coarse location. In the B200 run, 169 RIPE Atlas probes place the server within 44 km of its claimed datacentre and reject all 11 decoy sites. Together, these measurements check cloud-GPU identity, class, and coarse location without privileged access or a vendor key.

</details>


### [172] [Quantum-Resilient Decentralized AI Economies: Proof-of-Useful-Work and Post-Quantum Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24942)
*Connor Barbaccia,Sudip Vhaduri,Sayanton Dibbo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种去中心化AI经济体系，用机器学习工作（推理和训练）替代传统PoW的哈希计算，通过三层架构和代币经济模型实现共识，并具备量子安全优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PoW区块链的哈希计算产生的只是无外部价值的算力消耗，作者希望将区块链节点的计算资源导向有实际价值的机器学习工作，从而构建去中心化AI经济。

Method: 设计了计算层、验证层和经济协调层的三层架构；形式化了$(θ_c, θ_w, W)$-闭环代币经济模型；推导了诚实参与的充分抵押条件；分析了量子攻击对ML计算和签名层的影响。

Result: Grover算法对哈希谜题仅提供二次加速，但无法加速ML核心的线性代数运算；Shor算法威胁经典区块链签名，但可迁移到格密码和哈希签名标准；有用工作共识在经济和量子安全上均优于经典PoW。

Conclusion: 有用工作共识机制既能产生经济价值（AI推理/训练），又天然抵御量子加速攻击，结合后量子签名迁移，整体上比传统PoW更安全、更有经济意义。

Abstract: Proof-of-Work blockchains secure consensus through hash puzzles, producing no external value. In this research, we propose a decentralized AI economy where nodes are rewarded for useful machine-learning work, i.e., inference and training, instead of ineffective hashing method. Our proposed three-layer architecture separates compute, validation, and economic coordination. We formalize it via a $(θ_c, θ_w, W)$-closed-loop token economy and derive a sufficient-stake condition for honest participation. While existing Grover's algorithm provides only a quadratic speedup against hash puzzles, it does not accelerate ML-native linear algebra. On the other hand, Shor's algorithm threatens classical blockchain signatures. Post-quantum migration to lattice-based and hash-based standards can address the signature layer. Therefore, useful-work consensus thus offers both economic and quantum-security advantages over classical proof-of-work.

</details>


### [173] [What Does It Mean to Break a Distillation Defense?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25059)
*Lena Libon,Pura Peetathawatchai,Michael Aerni,Daniel Paleka,Florian Tramèr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 黑盒LLM（仅通过API访问）容易受到蒸馏攻击，近期提出的输出扰动防御方法缺乏共享威胁模型，导致难以比较和评估。本文提出了一个三维威胁模型框架（查询预算、数据预算、接口特征），并以抗蒸馏采样为例证明防御效果依赖于假设的威胁模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM蒸馏攻击的输出扰动防御方法缺乏统一的威胁模型，导致难以比较不同防御方法、评估其与其他攻击的组合效果以及检验其对现实对抗攻击的鲁棒性。这种威胁模型缺失不仅影响技术评估，还会在防御被用于保护知识产权或合规时造成虚假安全感。

Method: 提出了一个三维威胁模型框架：1) 查询预算（query budget）——攻击者可调用的API查询次数；2) 数据预算（data budget）——攻击者可获取的训练数据量；3) 接口特征（interface profile）——攻击者与API交互的方式。以抗蒸馏采样（antidistillation sampling）为案例，分析了不同威胁模型假设下防御效果的变化。

Result: 通过案例研究表明，同一防御方法在不同威胁模型假设下会被判定为有效或无效，防御效果高度依赖于所假设的攻击者能力。

Conclusion: 未来关于蒸馏防御的研究以及基于这些防御构建的治理或政策框架，应明确指定并压力测试攻击者沿上述三个维度的能力。

Abstract: Black-box LLMs (accessible only via API) are vulnerable to distillation attacks, in which an attacker queries the model and trains a student on its outputs. A recent line of work proposes output perturbation defenses that modify the teacher's output to reduce student performance while preserving utility for legitimate users. As a relatively new family of approaches, output perturbation defenses lack a shared threat model, making it difficult to compare them, reason about composing them with other attacks, or evaluate their robustness against realistic adversaries. This underspecification matters beyond technical evaluation: when defenses are deployed to protect intellectual property or justify regulatory compliance, an imprecise threat model can create a false sense of security. We propose a threat model framework that describes attackers along three dimensions: a query budget, a data budget, and an interface profile that captures how attackers interact with the API. Using antidistillation sampling as a case study, we show that whether the defense is considered effective depends on the assumed threat model. We argue that future work on distillation defenses, along with any governance or policy frameworks built around them, should explicitly specify and stress-test attacker capabilities along our three dimensions.

</details>


### [174] [SoK: AI Secure Code Generation: Progress, Pitfalls, and Paths Forward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25195)
*Rupam Patir,Keyan Guo,Haipeng Cai,Hongxin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化梳理了AI安全代码生成的进展、陷阱与未来方向，提出了一个三层框架（自然语言理解、代码执行、知识-执行差距），并通过实验发现：安全编程原则的理解是代码安全性的强预测因子，但模型仍存在显著的知识-执行差距——能识别安全原则却未能转化为安全且功能正确的代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代码生成系统日益普及，但缺乏对其生成安全代码能力的系统理解。已有研究探索了提示工程、微调、强化学习和智能体工作流等方法，但尚未系统性地分析这些技术如何提升安全性以及为何仍然存在重大失败。

Method: 提出了一个三层评估框架：(1) 模型对安全编程原则的自然语言理解能力；(2) 生成代码时对安全原则的执行能力；(3) 两者之间的知识-执行差距。在函数级安全性和完整Web应用安全性基准上对多个模型和编码智能体进行实证评估。

Result: 安全编程原则的理解是代码层面结果（功能正确性、安全性、联合功能-安全正确性）的统计显著强预测因子。但模型存在显著的知识-执行差距——能识别相关安全原则，却未能将其转化为安全且功能正确的代码。

Conclusion: 提供了以原则为中心的AI安全代码生成现状分析，并指出了具体的前进路径：原则引导的生成、评估、基准测试和智能体工作流。

Abstract: The increasing use of AI systems for code generation raises a central security question: what can today's models and coding agents actually do to produce secure code, where do they still fail, and what would move the field forward? Existing work has explored prompting, fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and agentic workflows for secure code generation, but the field still lacks a systematic understanding of how these techniques improve security and why substantial failures persist. In this SoK, we systematize the progress, pitfalls, and paths forward for AI secure code generation. We introduce a three-level framework that measures models' natural-language understanding of secure coding principles, their code-level actuation of those principles during generation, and the knowledge--actuation gaps between the two. We instantiate this framework across models and coding agents on benchmarks covering both isolated function-level security and full web-application security. Our results show that secure-coding-principle understanding is a statistically strong predictor of code-level outcomes, including functional correctness, security, and joint functional-security correctness. Yet substantial knowledge--actuation gaps remain: models can recognize relevant security principles but still fail to translate them into secure and functional code. These findings offer a principle-centered account of where AI secure code generation stands today and identify concrete paths forward through principle-guided generation, evaluation, benchmarking, and agentic workflows.

</details>


### [175] [Homomorphic Encryptions for Privacy Preserving Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25216)
*Preey Shah,Rohan Virani,Sanjari Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探索了使用全同态加密（FHE）技术实现隐私保护的计算机视觉推理任务，通过在加密数据上直接运行CNN模型进行分类，使用Microsoft SEAL/TenSEAL库，在MNIST、Kuzushiji MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上实现了与明文基线相近的分类准确率，并扩展了多通道输入、多层卷积和平均池化等复杂操作的支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律要求（如医疗和金融数据的隐私法规）阻止组织将敏感数据分享给第三方云MLaaS提供商，因此需要一种隐私保护的解决方案，使得在不暴露原始数据的情况下仍能利用云端的机器学习服务完成推理任务。

Method: 采用全同态加密（FHE）方案对输入图像进行加密，修改传统CNN层的结构以适配同态加密的加法和乘法操作约束，使用Microsoft SEAL库实现加密运算，并基于TenSEAL扩展支持彩色图像（多通道）、多层卷积和平均池化等复杂操作。

Result: 在多个数据集上实现了与明文训练基线相比极小的分类精度下降，加密CNN的推理性能损失被控制在较低水平。MNIST作为基准数据集表现良好，在更复杂的Kuzushiji MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10上也取得了有效结果。

Conclusion: 全同态加密能够有效应用于CNN推理任务，在保护数据隐私的同时保持较高的分类准确率，并且可以扩展到更复杂的视觉任务（如彩色图像、多层结构），为隐私保护的云MLaaS提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Legal requirements might prevent organizations from sharing sensitive data like medical or financial details of consumers which prevents them from leveraging cloud based ML-as-a-service solutions provided by third party providers, which are quickly gaining popularity these days. In this project, we aim to perform inference tasks in Computer Vision in a privacy-preserving manner, i.e, by only looking at encrypted data. Recent advances in fully homomorphic encryption make this possible. A fully homomorphic encryption allows an arbitrary sequence of additive and multiplicative operations to be performed on encrypted data directly. Applying homomorphic encryptions to CNNs requires modifying the conventional CNN layers, so that they adhere to the encryption scheme. Our aim was to explore the best methods to create CNNs which can classify encrypted images directly. We used Microsoft SEAL for performing homomorphic encryption. The performance of these "encryption based CNNs" should be comparable with baseline accuracies of the same CNNs trained on unencrypted data, and the aim was to achieve as low of a hit on inference-time performance as possible. We successfully obtained minimal drop in classification accuracy for various datasets. We used MNIST as our baseline, which is popularly used in related research work and then explored more complex datasets like Kuzushiji MNIST, Fashion-MNIST and CIFAR-10 as a part of our contribution. Additionally, we also added support for more complex operations on top of TenSEAL, like processing colored images (multi-channel input), applying multiple convolutional layers and performing average pooling.

</details>


### [176] [Sponsored Group Signature and its Application to Privacy-preserving Guest Access in Smart Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25248)
*Sepideh Avizheh,Reihaneh Safavi-Naini,Shiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SPGS（赞助式群签名）的两层成员加入群签名方案，其中一级成员可以赞助二级成员加入群组，并引入赞助者问责、可链接性等额外 accountability 机制，形式化定义了安全性，给出了通用构造和具体实例化，并应用于智能建筑中的匿名访客访问令牌（AGAT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能环境等隐私保护访问控制应用中，需要一种灵活的成员加入机制，而传统群签名只有受信任的群管理者才能添加成员，缺乏灵活性。本文旨在通过允许现有成员赞助新成员加入来放松这一限制，同时保持问责性。

Method: 提出SPGS方案的形式化定义与游戏安全模型；使用动态群签名、承诺方案和知识可靠的非交互式零知识证明（NIZKPoK）给出通用构造；基于现有方案给出具体实例化；结合IND-CPA安全公钥加密方案构造AGAT方案。
