Result: 给出了SPGS的通用构造并证明了其安全性；提供了具体实例化；展示了基于SPGS的AGAT方案及其效率分析。

Conclusion: SPGS通过两级成员机制在群签名的匿名性与问责性之间实现了更灵活的平衡，特别适用于需要临时、灵活成员管理的隐私保护场景（如智能建筑访客访问），理论构造和实际应用均验证了其实用性。

Abstract: Group signatures are privacy preserving signature schemes in which a group member can anonymously sign messages on behalf of the group, while providing accountability, by allowing the signature of a misbehaving group member be ``opened'' and the identity of the signer be revealed. In group signature members are admitted to the group by a (trusted) group manager. We motivate the need for a flexible mechanism in applications, such as privacy preserving access in smart environments, and propose a two-level member-join group signature that we call SPonsored Group Signature (SPGS) where group members of level 1 can ``sponsor'' new members, in level 2, to join the group. This relaxation of user join comes with additional accountability mechanisms: we require that the signature of a sponsored member can be opened to the identity of the sponsor (that is sponsor is responsible for the sponsored member), and while all signatures are anonymous, for the sponsored members, the signatures are linkable. This allows a sponsor to efficiently identify an undesirable sponsored member. We formalize SPGS scheme, define its security using a game-based approach, and give a generic construction of SPGS that uses a (dynamic) group signature scheme, a commitment scheme, and a knowledge-sound non-interactive zero knowledge proof of knowledge, and prove its security. We also give an instantiation of our construction. To show applicability of SPGS in practice, we consider the problem of providing guest access in a smart building, and introduce Anonymous Guest Access Token (AGAT) that allows a temporary guest to anonymously access (a subset of) the building resources. We show how SPGS can be used (together with an IND-CPA secure public key encryption scheme) to give a direct construction for AGAT, and show the efficiency of our guest access protocol when it is instantiated with existing schemes.

</details>


### [177] [Decoupling Reconnaissance and Exploitation: Measuring the Capability Boundaries of LLM-Based Web Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25332)
*Liwei Yu,Shuo Li,Ming Zhou,Ge Chu,Yan Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出两阶段解耦评估框架，将利用执行与侦察分离，发现LLM渗透测试代理在准确漏洞上下文中功能成功率可达90.0%，但自主侦察召回率仅约50.0%，揭示了不同架构在漏洞类型上的能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端黑盒评估受错误级联影响严重，前期侦察失败会掩盖代理实际漏洞利用能力，需要更精确的表征方法。

Method: 提出两阶段解耦评估框架，使用ground-truth注入和知识驱动消融实验，在70个高保真Web漏洞测试床上评估五种开源渗透测试代理（多智能体、单体、图驱动架构），基于50个代表性漏洞进行严格对齐。

Result: 准确漏洞上下文下功能成功率达90.0%，自主侦察目标漏洞召回率约50.0%；多智能体架构在长序列交互（反序列化）中更优，单体架构在短链注入中更优，图驱动架构在跨会话访问控制漏洞中更优。

Conclusion: 提供细粒度基准测试协议，为设计下一代自动化攻击安全代理提供实证基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for automated penetration testing, yet existing end-to-end black-box evaluations are highly susceptible to error cascading: failures in early reconnaissance can mask an agent's actual ability to exploit vulnerabilities. To more accurately characterize these capabilities, we propose a two-stage decoupled evaluation framework that separates exploit execution from reconnaissance. Using ground-truth injection and knowledge-driven ablation across 70 high-fidelity web vulnerability testbeds, our framework isolates exploitation performance from reconnaissance noise. We empirically evaluate five open-source penetration-testing agents, covering multiagent, monolithic, and graph-driven architectures, on a strictly aligned subset of 50 representative vulnerabilities. The results reveal a substantial capability gap. With accurate vulnerability context, agents achieve a functional success rate of up to 90.0%, whereas autonomous reconnaissance, measured by targeted vulnerability recall, plateaus at approximately 50.0%, primarily due to failures in parsing unstructured telemetry. Cross-architectural analysis further reveals distinct capability niches: multi-agent isolation is more effective for long-sequence interactions such as de-serialization, while monolithic and graph-driven designs perform better on short-chain injections and cross-session access-control vulnerabilities, respectively. This decoupled evaluation work provides a fine-grained benchmarking protocol and an empirical basis for designing next-generation automated offensive security agents.

</details>


### [178] [General Techniques for Reducing Key-Switching Overhead in Privacy-Preserving Two-Party Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25349)
*Wenshao Yang,Zhenhua Liu,Dongdong Yao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对安全两方Transformer推理中线性层的密钥切换开销问题，本文从与打包策略无关的角度提出了预处理辅助安全注意力计算方法、存储-通信权衡技术以及融合密钥切换技术，在不修改现有打包策略的前提下减少了在线推理阶段的密钥切换开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全两方Transformer推理中，线性层使用FHE进行明文-密文或密文-密文矩阵乘法时，密钥切换（key switching）操作占据主要计算开销。现有优化方法过度依赖特定的打包算法，缺乏通用性，因此需要一种与打包策略无关的通用优化方案来减少密钥切换开销。

Method: 1) 预处理辅助方法：将注意力计算分解为可预计算操作和在线交互，将密钥切换从在线推理阶段转移到离线预处理阶段；2) 存储-通信权衡技术：用适度的在线通信替代大规模预计算密文，在不同资源约束下实现灵活部署；3) 融合密钥切换技术：针对乘法-旋转模式，将重线性化与旋转合并为单一过程，减少计算成本。

Result: 分析评估表明，所提技术显著减少了在线密钥切换开销，提供了存储与通信之间的灵活权衡，且无需修改现有打包策略。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套与打包策略无关的优化技术，有效降低了安全两方Transformer推理中密钥切换的开销，并通过存储-通信权衡实现了不同资源场景下的灵活部署，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: In secure two-party Transformer inference, linear layers are typically evaluated using Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) through plaintext-ciphertext or ciphertext-ciphertext matrix multiplications, where key switching primarily occurs and dominates computational overhead in both FHE-based and hybrid FHE-MPC systems. Existing optimizations rely heavily on packing-specific algorithms, limiting their general applicability.
  Targeting this overhead from a packing-independent perspective, we propose a preprocessing-assisted method for secure attention computation. By decomposing attention into precomputable operations and online interactions, this method reduces online inference-phase key switching without modifying existing packing strategies.
  However, the first method shifting key switching offline introduces additional storage requirements. To address this, we propose storage-communication trade-off techniques that replace large precomputed ciphertexts with modest online communication, enabling flexible deployment under varying resource constraints.
  While ciphertext-ciphertext matrix multiplication is offloaded to the preprocessing phase in hybrid schemes and the first layer of FHE-based schemes, these operations still persist in the offline stage and subsequent FHE layers. To further optimize it, we propose a fused key-switch technique targeting the multiplication-followed-by-rotation pattern, which frequently arises in existing RNS-CKKS matrix multiplication schemes. By combining relinearization and rotation into a single procedure, this technique reduces the associated computation costs.
  Analytical evaluations demonstrate that our proposed techniques significantly reduce online key-switch overhead and provide flexible trade-offs between storage and communication without requiring modifications to existing packing strategies.

</details>


### [179] [Representation Matters: An Empirical Study of Program Representations for LLM Vulnerability Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25356)
*Andrew Stoltman,Johnathan Tang,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过RepBench基准测试挑战了“更多源代码上下文有助于漏洞检测”的假设，发现结构化程序表示（尤其是AST+PDG组合）比原始源代码更有效。最佳方法AST+PDG达到83.2%准确率，而原始源代码仅为53.5%。研究揭示了“上下文稀释效应”：添加原始源代码反而会降低图结构证据的推理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的漏洞检测方法大多直接使用原始源代码作为输入，但程序结构和语义应如何表示以利于LLM推理尚不明确。本文旨在探究不同程序表示对LLM漏洞推理能力的影响，验证是否更多原始上下文总是更好。

Method: 构建RepBench基准，将C/C++漏洞测试用例转换为多种表示（原始源码、AST、CFG、PDG及其组合，以及增强PDG），使用107个Joern测试用例（5个CWE类别），在固定CoT和结构化输出协议下评估10种表示变体，额外包含19个ePDG用例。

Result: AST+PDG组合达到83.2%准确率，原始源码仅53.5%；仅图结构的提示优于仅源码和源码+图组合，且提示开销更小；发现上下文稀释效应：添加原始源码到紧凑结构图证据中会降低推理效果。

Conclusion: 精心选择的结构化表示比给予LLM更多原始输入能提供更好的准确率-开销权衡，静态分析可作为面向安全LLM推理的有效提示构建层。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated vulnerability detection, but it remains unclear how program structure and semantics should be represented for LLM-based reasoning. Most prompting-based approaches provide raw source code, implicitly assuming that more source-level context gives the model better evidence. This paper challenges that assumption through RepBench, an empirical benchmark comparing raw source code with static-analysis-based program representations. RepBench converts real-world C/C++ vulnerability testcases into multiple representations: raw source, Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs), Control-Flow Graphs (CFGs), Program Dependence Graphs (PDGs), their combinations, and an auxiliary track of enriched PDGs (ePDGs). Using a curated PrimeVul-derived corpus of 107 Joern-based testcases across five CWE categories, we evaluate ten representation variants under a fixed Chain-of-Thought and structured-output protocol, plus 19 additional ePDG cases generated through VulChecker/Hector. Representation choice substantially affects LLM vulnerability reasoning. The strongest variant, AST+PDG, achieves 83.2% accuracy, compared with 53.5% for raw source. At the family level, graph-only prompts outperform both source-only and source-plus-graph prompts while requiring far less prompt overhead. These results reveal a context dilution effect: adding raw source code to compact structural graph evidence can degrade reasoning by making vulnerability-relevant evidence less salient. Overall, our findings show that carefully selected structural representations offer a better accuracy-overhead tradeoff than simply giving LLMs more raw input, and suggest that static analysis can serve as an effective prompt-construction layer for security-focused LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [180] [Security and Privacy in Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Architectures, Threats, Defenses, and Future Directions for Building Trustworthy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25533)
*Balamurugan Palanisamy,G S S Chalapathi,Vikas Hassija,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了检索增强生成（RAG）系统在隐私和安全方面面临的挑战，涵盖集中式、设备端（Micro-RAG）、联邦式和混合式部署范式，提出了威胁面统一分类法，并分析了攻击类别与防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通过引入检索机制增强大语言模型，但这一过程引入了超越传统语言模型威胁的新安全与隐私风险，现有研究缺乏系统性的梳理与分类。

Method: 采用综述方法，构建了涵盖检索、上下文构建和生成阶段的威胁面统一分类法，系统分析了成员推理、索引推理、投毒、梯度泄漏和合谋等攻击类别，并梳理了架构、算法和密码学层面的防御手段。

Result: 识别了RAG系统在集中式、设备端（Micro-RAG）、联邦式和混合式部署范式下的隐私与安全威胁，建立了统一的攻击分类体系，并总结了现有防御方法及其隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 构建可信、安全且有韧性的RAG系统仍面临诸多开放挑战，需要进一步研究以推动实际应用落地。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a dominant paradigm for enhancing large language models with external knowledge. By coupling retrieval mechanisms with generative models, RAG systems improve factual grounding and adaptability across domains. However, integrating retrieval pipelines introduces new security and privacy risks that extend beyond conventional language modeling threats. Sensitive information may be exposed through retrieval indices, query logs, context construction, or federated updates, while adversarial manipulation of knowledge bases can undermine trust in generated outputs. This survey provides a comprehensive examination of privacy and security challenges across RAG systems deployed in centralized, on-device (Micro-RAG), federated, and hybrid paradigms. We present a unified taxonomy of threat surfaces spanning the retrieval, context construction, and generation stages and systematically analyze attack classes, including membership inference, index inference, poisoning, gradient leakage, and collusion. We further review architectural, algorithmic, and cryptographic defenses, highlighting privacy-utility trade-offs and deployment considerations. Finally, we outline open research challenges toward building trustworthy, secure, and resilient RAG systems for real-world applications.

</details>


### [181] [CrypFormBench: Benchmarking Formal Analysis Capability of Large Language Models for Cryptographic Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25561)
*Zhaoxuan Li,Qionglu Zhang,Hengyuan Liu,Xiaoyan Gu,Xianhui Lu,Hongbo Liu,Bingzheng Wang,Haihui Fan,Ziming Zhao,Rui Zhang,Li Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CrypFormBench基准，用于评估LLM在符号安全与计算安全形式化分析中的五项核心能力。评估9个主流LLM发现其在解释和补全任务上表现尚可，但在生成、转换和修正任务上表现有限，最高分仅48.7（Claude-3.5）。研究还提供了少样本提示、Pass@K采样和轻量微调等实用建议以提升可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动形式化分析密码方案费时且需专业知识，现有自动化工具仍依赖专家进行方案抽象和工具特定形式化描述。LLM虽具潜力，但因缺乏标准化评估方法，其在该领域的有效性尚未被探索。

Method: 构建CrypFormBench基准，覆盖符号安全与计算安全两大范式，包含700个实例、677个方案、7种主流形式化验证语言和160个安全属性，评估LLM在解释、生成、补全、转换和修正五项核心能力上的表现。

Result: 9个主流LLM在解释和补全任务上表现较好（得益于代码感知优势），但在生成、转换和修正任务上表现有限。Claude-3.5取得最高分48.7/100，整体性能仍然受限。

Conclusion: 该基准和分析提供了当前LLM辅助形式化密码分析的现实进展视图，并指出了可靠LLM辅助形式化密码分析的具体方向。少样本提示、Pass@K采样和轻量微调等方法可缓解可执行性瓶颈，提升工具可用输出。

Abstract: Manual formal analysis of cryptographic schemes is labor-intensive and requires substantial expertise. While model-checking tools (e.g., Scyther and Tamarin) and computational-security tools (e.g., CryptoVerif and EasyCrypt) improve the automation of security proofs, they still rely on experts to abstract schemes and write tool-specific formal descriptions. Large language models (LLMs) are a promising alternative, but their effectiveness in this domain remains unexplored due to the absence of standardized evaluation methodologies. To fill this gap, we introduce CrypFormBench (C.F.B for short), a comprehensive benchmark jointly covering symbolic and computational security to evaluate five core LLM capabilities: interpretation, generation, completion, transformation, and correction. It comprises 700 instances spanning 677 schemes, 7 mainstream formal verifier languages, and 160 security properties. The evaluation of 9 state-of-the-art LLMs reveals that most of them perform well on interpretation and completion, given their code-awareness advantages, but struggle with generation, transformation, and correction. Overall, their performance remains limited, with Claude-3.5 achieving the highest score at 48.7 out of 100. We further provide practical guidance, e.g., few-shot prompting, Pass@K sampling, and lightweight fine-tuning, to mitigate the executability bottleneck and improve tool-usable outputs. Taken together, our benchmark and analyses offer a grounded view of current progress and concrete directions toward reliable LLM-assisted formal cryptographic analysis.

</details>


### [182] [An Approach for a Supporting Multi-LLM System for Automated Certification Based on the German IT-Grundschutz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25608)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多大型语言模型系统（MLS）与混合检索增强生成（HybridRAG）的方法，用于半自动化BSI IT-Grundschutz认证，旨在应对NIS2指令带来的需求增长、专家短缺和高成本挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIS2指令导致对安全认证的需求大幅增加，但面临专家短缺和实施成本高昂的问题，亟需自动化工具提升效率、降低成本并维持认证质量。

Method: 采用多大型语言模型系统（MLS）架构，结合混合检索增强生成（HybridRAG）技术，融合大语言模型（LLMs）与知识图谱（KGs），支持认证流程中的保护需求评估、建模、IT-Grundschutz检查、措施整合及实施验证等多个阶段。

Result: 所提出的系统能够提升认证效率、降低成本，同时帮助认证人员在满足新增企业认证需求的情况下维持安全概念的质量。

Conclusion: 该MLS架构为NIS2引入的数字安全挑战提供了可行解决方案，有效应对了日益增长的安全概念需求。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to perform semi-automated BSI IT-Grundschutz certification using a MultiLarge Language Model system (MLS) with Hybrid RetrievalAugmented Generation (HybridRAG). Facing the challenges of the Network and Information Security Directive 2 (NIS2) directive, a shortage of specialists, and high implementation costs, our MLS architecture aims to increase efficiency, reduce costs, and support certifiers in maintaining the quality of security concepts while meeting the increased demand for certifications of newly affected companies. The system combines Large Language Models (LLMs) and Knowledge Graphs (KGs) to support different phases of the certification process, including protection needs assessment, modeling, IT-Grundschutz check, measure consolidation, and subsequent realization. Our architecture addresses the growing demand for security concepts and offers an approach to handle the digital security challenges introduced by NIS2.

</details>


### [183] [Probabilistic Agents in Deterministic Audits: Evaluating Multi-Agent Systems for Automated Audits Based on the German IT-Grundschutz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25622)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合多智能体系统（MAS）与混合检索增强生成（HybridRAG）的架构，用于部分自动化IT-Grundschutz（IT-GS）认证流程。通过在结构分析阶段引入假设验证循环以减少幻觉，并设计解耦推理管道分离语义提取与确定性保护需求继承。实证评估表明，该系统在语义任务（结构分析与建模）上表现高效，但在需要逻辑推理的保护需求评估与IT-GS检查阶段，由于LLM的概率性本质难以满足IT-GS所需的确定性严谨性，存在明显局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIS-2指令要求大量中小企业实施严格的风险管理，但IT-GS认证资源消耗大、人工工作繁重，难以规模化推广。现有方法缺乏自动化手段来减轻认证过程中的文档编制、验证和修订负担。

Method: 基于先前概念框架，本文实现了MAS与HybridRAG相结合的技术架构。两大核心技术贡献：（1）结构分析阶段的假设验证循环，通过知识图谱交叉验证智能体推断的依赖关系以减少幻觉；（2）解耦推理管道，将智能体驱动的语义提取与确定性保护需求继承分离。使用BSI的"RecPlast GmbH"案例研究作为人工专家参考数据集进行端到端评估。

Result: 系统在语义任务（结构分析和建模）上表现出高效性，显著减少了人工工作量。但在逻辑推理阶段（保护需求评估和IT-GS检查），由于当前LLM的概率性本质与IT-GS所需的确定性严谨性不匹配，定量结果显示出明显的局限性。

Conclusion: MAS结合HybridRAG可有效自动化IT-GS认证中的语义密集型任务，但在需要严格逻辑推理的环节仍需人类专家介入。当前LLM的概率特性是满足IT-GS确定性要求的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: The NIS-2 Directive mandates robust Risk Management from thousands of small and medium enterprises. To ensure compliance, companies rely on established standards such as the German IT-Grundschutz (IT-GS) of the Federal Office for Information Security. However, IT-GS certification is resource-intensive and requires a high level of manual effort for documentation, validation, and revision, making scalable implementation difficult and expensive.
  Building upon our previous conceptual framework, this paper presents the technical implementation and empirical evaluation of a Multi-Agent System (MAS) architecture combined with Hybrid Retrieval Augmented Generation (HybridRAG) for the partial automation of IT-GS certification. We introduce two novel technical contributions to the MAS architecture to enforce the compliance rigor. The Hypothesis-Verification Loop in the Structural Analysis (SA) phase that cross-references agent-inferred dependencies against the Knowledge Graph to reduce hallucinations, and a Decoupled Reasoning Pipeline that separates agent-driven semantic extraction from the deterministic protection need inheritance. We utilize the BSI's "RecPlast GmbH" case study as a human expert-generated reference data set for end-to-end evaluation of the architecture and to quantify Precision, Recall, and F1-scores. The performance of the system is investigated across the phases of SA, Protection Needs Assessment (PNA), Modeling, and IT-GS Check.
  The empirical results reveal noticeable differences throughout the different steps of IT-GS. While the MAS demonstrates high efficacy in semantic tasks (SA and Modeling), significantly reducing manual effort through automated information extraction, quantitative results reveal limitations in logical reasoning phases (PNA and IT-GS Check) as the probabilistic nature of current LLMs struggles to meet the deterministic rigor required by IT-GS.

</details>


### [184] [Taxonomy of Risks on Automated Fact-Checking Systems Considering its Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25645)
*Jun Yajima,Tatsuya Oka,Takao Okubo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对自动化事实核查系统的安全风险进行了系统分类，识别出32种具体风险，并通过DEFAME系统的风险评估验证了所提引导词相比传统STRIDE方法能发现更多风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上虚假新闻泛滥，人工事实核查难以覆盖所有内容，自动化事实核查系统虽必要但存在误判和发布错误结果的风险，可能导致 misinformation 扩散或诽谤，因此需要系统性地识别和分类这些风险。

Method: 采用三阶段风险传播模型（风险因子→危险情境→危害）对自动化事实核查系统进行风险分类，识别出32种具体风险；并将这些风险作为引导词，在DEFAME系统上进行风险评估，与STRIDE方法进行对比。

Result: 识别出自动化事实核查系统存在32种具体风险；使用本文引导词能发现传统STRIDE方法无法导出的风险，验证了所提方法在风险评估上的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为自动化事实核查系统的安全使用迈出了第一步，所提风险分类和引导词能有效补充传统安全风险评估方法，有助于构建更安全的自动化事实核查系统。

Abstract: In recent years, the posting of fake news including disinformation and misinformation on social networking services (SNS) has become a social problem. To combat this fake news, fact-checking that is the process of assessing the veracity of posts on SNS has become increasingly important. While fact-checking is currently performed by fact-checking organizations, it is difficult to fact-check all posts on SNS. Therefore, the use of automated fact-checking systems is effective. Recent automated fact-checking systems utilize artificial intelligence and large language models, so there are risks of incorrect judgments and posting incorrect results on social media which can lead to the spread of misinformation or to engage in defamation. In this paper, as a first step toward enabling the safe use of automated fact-checking systems, we categorize the specific risks on automated fact-checking systems. In this categorizing, we consider a three-stage risk propagation: risk factors, hazardous situations, and harm. Our analysis revealed that 32 specific risks exist in automated fact-checking systems. In this paper, we utilize the categorized risks as analytical cues (guide words) to present the risk assessment of the automated fact-checking system DEFAME. This assessment result indicates that risks that cannot be derived using STRIDE, a conventional IT security risk assessment method can be derived using our guide words.

</details>


### [185] [Tracing Target Answers in Poisoned Retrieval Corpora via Token Influence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25721)
*Yan-Lun Chen,Pin-Yu Chen,Chia-Mu Yu,Ying-Dar Lin,Yu-Sung Wu,Wei-Bin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRACE是一个轻量级的检测框架，通过令牌影响归因来识别RAG系统中的语料投毒攻击，在三个QA基准和六个LLM上展示了强大的检测性能，同时能揭示攻击者指定的目标答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG系统语料投毒攻击检测方法通常依赖辅助分类器或额外的LLM验证，带来大量计算开销，需要更轻量级的检测方案。

Method: TRACE通过令牌影响归因追踪与答案相关的令牌，首先发现检索文档中反复出现的高影响力关键词，然后进行二次验证以确认它们对模型预测的影响。

Result: 在三个QA基准和六个LLM上展示了强大的检测性能，同时能揭示攻击者指定的目标答案。

Conclusion: TRACE提供了一种无需额外分类器或LLM验证的轻量级投毒攻击检测方案，有效检测语料投毒并识别攻击者目标。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to corpus poisoning attacks that manipulate model outputs through malicious retrieved documents. Existing detection methods typically rely on auxiliary classifiers or additional LLM-based verification, introducing substantial computational overhead. We present TRACE, a lightweight detection framework that identifies poisoning attacks by tracing answer-related tokens through token influence attribution. TRACE first discovers recurrent high-influence keywords across retrieved documents and then performs a secondary verification to confirm their influence on model predictions. Experiments on three QA benchmarks and six LLMs demonstrate strong detection performance while simultaneously uncovering attacker-specified target answers.

</details>


### [186] [Shoot the Honey, Cloak the Player: Towards Zero-Runtime-Overhead Proactive Defense and Detection for Visual Game Cheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25734)
*Jianing Wang,Chuqi Zhang,Yuancheng Jiang,Adil Ahmad,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AimTrap是首个针对视觉自瞄作弊的端到端防御系统，通过对抗性伪装纹理（ACT）隐藏真实玩家和对抗性蜜罐纹理（AHT）引诱作弊者锁定假目标，实现了实时防御与赛后检测的完整方案，在实际对抗SOTA视觉自瞄作弊时分别达到85.1%和96.9%的防御成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防作弊方案均无法提供端到端解决方案：事后行为检测器无法实时保护比赛完整性且对模仿人类的作弊器越来越脆弱，而主动运行时防御则缺乏问责机制、开销大或需要侵入式系统集成。

Method: 结合两种对抗性纹理机制：对抗性伪装纹理（ACT）隐藏真实玩家，对抗性蜜罐纹理（AHT）引诱作弊器锁定假目标并收集作弊证据。集成可微渲染与渲染期望方法实现鲁棒3D纹理合成，设计安全纹理管理和蜜罐交互轨迹分析管线。

Result: ACT防御成功率85.1%，AHT防御成功率96.9%，极低的假阳性率，可忽略的运行时开销。

Conclusion: AimTrap提供了实用且有效的端到端视觉自瞄作弊防御方案，兼具实时保护与赛后检测能力。

Abstract: Visual aimbots have emerged as a serious cheating threat in first-person shooter (FPS) games, as they evade existing anti-cheat defenses by operating only on rendered frames rather than game memory. However, existing defenses fail to provide an end-to-end solution: post-hoc behavior detectors cannot protect match integrity in real time and are increasingly fragile against human-mimicking aimbots, while proactive runtime defenses often lack accountability, incur substantial overhead, or require intrusive system integration.
  We present AimTrap, the first end-to-end defense against visual aimbots that combines real-time protection with post-game detection using two adversarial texture mechanisms. Adversarial Camouflage Textures (ACT) hide real players from aimbots, while Adversarial Honeypot Textures (AHT) lure aimbots into locking onto fake targets, yielding strong evidence of cheating. To make this practical, AimTrap integrates differentiable rendering with Expectation over Renderings for robust 3D texture synthesis, secure texture management, and a novel honeypot-interaction trajectory analysis pipeline for accurate cheating attribution.
  In real-game evaluation against a state-of-the-art visual aimbot, ACT achieves 85.1% defense success, AHT achieves 96.9%. Compared with prior baselines, AimTrap attains extremely low false-positive rates, while incurring negligible runtime overhead. These results show that AimTrap provides a practical and effective end-to-end defense against visual aimbots.

</details>


### [187] [RAS: Measuring LLM Safety Through Refusal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25750)
*Chang-Chieh Huang,Yan-Lun Chen,Chia-Mu Yu,Wei-Bin Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeVec提出了一种基于内部表征的白盒LLM安全评估方法，通过计算模型隐状态与拒绝方向的对齐程度（RAS分数）来衡量安全性，无需生成输出，速度更快且能有效区分对齐与未对齐模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估依赖输出层面的不安全/越狱提示查询和策略违规判断，存在成本高、对评判器选择敏感、易受固定题库限制等问题。

Method: SafeVec首先从安全对齐的参考模型中逐层提取拒绝方向，然后选择安全与不安全行为可分离的稳定层窗口，最后通过衡量目标模型隐状态与这些拒绝方向的对齐程度计算RAS分数（0-100校准安全分数）。

Result: 在Llama、Gemma、Qwen模型家族上，RAS能有效区分对齐模型与未审查/消融变体，追踪输出级攻击成功率，且显著快于基于评判器的评估方法。

Conclusion: 拒绝对齐提供了紧凑且高效的白盒LLM安全评估信号。

Abstract: Safety evaluation of large language models (LLMs) is commonly performed by querying models with unsafe or jailbreak prompts and judging whether their outputs violate a safety policy. Although useful, output-level evaluation is expensive, sensitive to judge choice, and easily tied to fixed question banks. We propose **SafeVec**, a white-box evaluation procedure that measures safety from internal representations rather than generated answers. **SafeVec** first extracts layer-wise refusal directions from a safety-aligned reference model, then selects stable layer windows where safe and unsafe behaviors are separable, and finally scores a target model by measuring whether its hidden states align with these refusal directions under unsafe and jailbreak prompts. The resulting metric, **RAS** (**R**efusal **A**lignment **S**core), maps representation-level refusal alignment to a calibrated 0-100 safety score. Across `Llama`, `Gemma`, and `Qwen` model families, RAS separates aligned models from uncensored and abliterated variants, tracks output-level attack success rate, and is substantially faster than judge-based evaluation. These results suggest that refusal alignment provides a compact and efficient signal for white-box LLM safety evaluation.

</details>


### [188] [Color Matters: Trigger Color Affects Success in Federated Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25858)
*Kavindu Herath,Joshua C. Zhao,Saurabh Bagchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习中的语义后门攻击，发现即使触发器语义、位置和投毒预算不变，仅改变触发器颜色就能显著影响攻击成功率。在CelebA发色分类任务中，白色触发器对攻击金发类更有效，黑色触发器对攻击黑发类更有效，且该趋势在鲁棒聚合下依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习易受后门攻击，现有研究多关注触发器的语义和位置，但触发器颜色这一简单属性对攻击效果的影响尚未被充分探索。本文旨在揭示触发器颜色作为语义后门攻击中一个被低估的重要因素。

Method: 采用语义驱动的后门机制，使用口罩、墨镜等自然视觉配件作为触发器，仅改变触发器颜色（黑/白），保持攻击流程不变。恶意客户端通过向源类图像添加触发器并重标为目标类来构建中毒样本，同时使用标准投毒目标函数和更强的SABLE目标函数（结合干净分类损失、触发目标损失、特征分离损失和正则化项）。

Result: 在CelebA四类发色分类任务上，白色触发器对攻击金发类更有效，黑色触发器对攻击黑发类更有效；相同的颜色趋势在鲁棒聚合协议下仍然持续，表明触发器颜色是影响语义后门攻击效果的重要因素。

Conclusion: 触发器颜色是联邦学习语义后门攻击中一个有意义且被低估的因素，影响攻击的操作性、持久性和评估，未来防御策略应考虑触发器颜色这一维度。

Abstract: Federated learning is vulnerable to backdoor attacks in which malicious clients inject poisoned updates while preserving benign-task performance. In this paper, we study a semantics-driven backdoor mechanism in which attackers use natural visual accessories as triggers and manipulate only the trigger color while keeping the attack pipeline fixed. Our framework considers semantic trigger objects such as masks and sunglasses, instantiated in black and white variants, and evaluates their effect in a controlled federated learning setting. Malicious clients construct poisoned samples by applying a trigger to source-class images and relabeling them to an attacker-chosen target class, while benign clients train only on clean data. We analyze this mechanism under both a standard poisoning objective and a stronger SABLE-based objective that combines clean classification loss, triggered target loss, feature-separation loss in the penultimate representation space, and regularization to keep malicious updates close to the global model. This design enables the attack to remain effective while reducing excessive update drift. Experiments on a four-class CelebA hair-color task show that trigger color significantly changes attack success rate even when trigger semantics, placement, and poisoning budget are unchanged. White triggers are more effective for attacks targeting the blond class, whereas black triggers perform better for attacks targeting the black class. The same trend persists under robust aggregation, showing that trigger color is a meaningful factor in the operation, persistence, and evaluation of semantic backdoor mechanisms in federated learning.

</details>


### [189] [A Tattered Cloak of Invisibility: Measuring Anonymity Loss in Railgun on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25926)
*Kanan Huseynov,Ali Shahzaib,István András Seres,János Tapolcai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究加密货币混币器Railgun中用户行为导致的隐私泄露问题，通过五种启发式方法（时序模式、地址重用、交易图邻近性、金额指纹、背包匹配）分析存款与取款交易的关联性，发现即使密码学上安全的隐私系统也会因用户行为显著损失匿名性，最终能唯一关联17.65%的取款交易至存款交易，并造成3.42位的中位匿名性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链金融系统中交易公开可见，现有密码学隐私方案的安全性不足以保障用户实际隐私，用户行为模式（如交易时间、金额模式等）会大幅降低匿名性，因此需要系统性地量化此类行为泄露风险。

Method: 针对以太坊上的混币器Railgun，提出五种启发式关联方法：1) 特征时序模式分析；2) 地址重用检测；3) 基于先前公开交易的交易图邻近性；4) 保留数字模式的金额指纹匹配；5) 背包求解器分析交易金额组之间的可加性匹配。

Result: 五种启发式方法能唯一关联17.65%的Railgun取款交易到对应的存款交易；背包求解算法造成取款交易3.42位的中位匿名性损失。

Conclusion: 即使采用密码学上强大的隐私保护系统，用户行为和交易模式仍会导致实质性的匿名性损失；研究成果有助于理解混币器和匿名池的实际隐私极限，并指导更安全的使用实践和系统设计原则。

Abstract: From a user's perspective, perhaps the most significant difference between traditional banking services and widely used blockchain-based financial systems is that, in the latter, transactions and, either directly or indirectly, account balances and transaction histories are publicly observable. Therefore, a growing number of cryptographic solutions have been proposed to add a privacy layer to such systems. However, the privacy that users actually obtain does not depend solely on the security of the underlying cryptographic protocol: user behavior, transaction amount patterns, and timing decisions can substantially reduce anonymity.
  In this work, we study behavioral leakage in cryptocurrency mixers, focusing on Railgun on Ethereum. We aim to heuristically estimate the probability that a given deposit and withdrawal transaction belong to the same user. We consider five sources of leakage: characteristic timing patterns, address reuse, proximity in the transaction graph induced by prior public transactions, amount fingerprints that preserve distinctive digit patterns across transaction values, and knapsack type matches in which groups of transaction amounts add up in revealing ways. Our results show that even cryptographically strong privacy systems may suffer substantial anonymity loss due to user behavior and transaction patterns. Our five heuristics are able to uniquely link 17.65% of Railgun withdraw transactions to deposit transactions. We also applied a knapsack solver algorithm that was able to produce a 3.42 bit median anonymity loss for withdraw transactions. This work contributes to a better understanding of the practical privacy limits of mixers and anonymity pools, and points toward safer usage practices and design principles.

</details>


### [190] [Do (Not) Tell Me About My Insecurities: Assessing the Status Quo of Coordinated Vulnerability Disclosure in Germany Amid New EU Cybersecurity Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25950)
*Sebastian Neef,Cenk Schlunke,Anne Hennig*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 对德国DAX 40家公司的纵向研究表明，协调漏洞披露（CVD）项目的采纳率从2023年的50%显著提升至2025年的90%以上。法律义务（如NIS2和CRA）是主要驱动力，但缺乏人力资源和报告质量参差不齐是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在互联世界中，良好的IT安全实践对防范漏洞和数据泄露至关重要。提供安全联系渠道（如CVD项目或security.txt文件）是企业促进外部漏洞报告的重要实践，但目前对大型企业的实际采纳情况及挑战缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 纵向研究设计：监测40家德国DAX上市公司的公开CVD信息；2023年和2025年通过电子邮件和邮政发送问卷；回收率为20%。

Result: CVD采纳率从50%（2023年）提升至90%以上（2025年）；新增10个CVD项目和25个security.txt文件；法律义务（NIS2、CRA）是主要推动力；人力资源不足和报告质量参差不齐是主要缺点。

Conclusion: 作为首个对40家德国DAX公司CVD实践的调查研究，结果有助于促进中小企业和其它公司对安全项目的采纳与理解，并为政策制定者提供实践挑战和行业经验方面的见解。

Abstract: In our increasingly interconnected world, good IT security practices are necessary to prevent vulnerabilities and data breaches. Providing security contacts, e.g., via Coordinated Vulnerability Disclosure (CVD) programs or security.txt files, is an important practice for businesses to facilitate vulnerability reporting by external parties. As part of a longitudinal study, we analyzed the adoption of, as well as the challenges and experiences with, CVD programs among the 40 companies listed on Germany's DAX (the country's primary stock market index). In addition to monitoring publicly available information about their CVD programs, we sent out questionnaires via email and postal mail in 2023 and 2025, and received answers from 20\% of the companies. The adoption rates show a significant increase from 50\% (2023) to over 90\% (2025), with ten new CVD programs and 25 new security.txt files now available. The survey answers reveal that, for example, legal obligations (e.g., NIS2 and CRA) drive the adoption of CVD practices, but a lack of (human) resources and varying report quality are considered drawbacks. As the first study to survey 40 German stock market index (DAX) companies on their CVD practices, our results can help foster the adoption and understanding of security programs among SMEs and other companies, and provide policymakers with insights into practical challenges and industry experiences.

</details>


### [191] [BlowLive: Blow-Based Multi-Factor Biometrics with Liveness Detection and Revocability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25998)
*Eyasu Getahun Chekole,Howard Halim,Daniël Reijsbergen,Jianying Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出BlowLive多因素生物特征认证框架，融合吹气声学信号与面部生物特征，采用模糊提取器生成加密密钥，结合多普勒频移活体检测，在50名参与者实验中实现99.56%-100%的认证准确率和99.42%的活体检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理和行为生物特征存在四大局限：易受欺骗攻击、模板泄露隐私、类内变异导致精度下降、难以撤销和长期保护。需要一种更安全的鲁棒多因素认证方案。

Method: 1) 提出BlowLive多因素生物特征框架，融合吹气声学信号（行为模态）和面部生物特征（生理模态）；2) 采用模糊提取器从含噪生物特征输入中导出稳定加密密钥用于认证；3) 引入基于多普勒频移的活体检测机制防御回放、合成和深度伪造攻击；4) 结合高级频谱特征提取与多模态融合技术。

Result: 在50名参与者数据上：吹气声学模态认证准确率99.56%，面部及融合模态100%，活体检测准确率99.42%，同时具备强模板保护、可撤销性、非侵入性和高可用性。

Conclusion: BlowLive框架有效解决了现有生物特征认证的关键安全与隐私问题，通过多模态融合、模糊提取器密钥导出和多普勒活体检测实现了高精度、高安全性和高可用性的认证方案。

Abstract: Biometric authentication systems are increasingly deployed in security-critical applications, yet existing physiological and behavioral biometrics suffer from fundamental limitations: 1) they are vulnerable to spoofing attacks due to unreliable liveness detection, 2) biometric templates may leak privacy-sensitive information 3) intra-user variability results in accuracy degradation, and 4) it is difficult to revoke physiological biometrics and safeguard them over long-term use. To address these challenges, we propose BlowLive, a robust multi-factor biometric (MFB) framework that integrates blow-acoustic signals and facial biometrics as complementary behavioral and physiological modalities. BlowLive incorporates advanced spectral feature extraction and multimodal fusion techniques, achieving high authentication accuracy even for behavioral modalities. Instead of relying on conventional biometric approaches that utilize raw biometric templates for authentication, the proposed framework adopts a fuzzy-extractor-based biometric authentication scheme, wherein stable cryptographic keys are derived from inherently noisy biometric inputs and subsequently used for authentication. To defend against playback, synthetic, and deepfake attacks, BlowLive further integrates a novel Doppler shift-based liveness detection mechanism. We implement the complete BlowLive framework and experimentally evaluate its effectiveness using biometric data collected from 50 participants. The experimental results demonstrate high authentication accuracy (99.56% for blow-acoustics and 100% for facial and fusion modalities), robust liveness detection (99.42% accuracy), strong template protection and revocability, non-invasiveness, and high usability.

</details>


### [192] [Privacy Vulnerabilities of Attention Layers in Tabular Foundation Models and Protection of High-Risk Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26021)
*Tânia Carvalho,Maxime Cordy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了表格基础模型在上下文学习中的隐私泄漏风险，提出了一种基于注意力机制的成员推断攻击（AMIA），并引入了一种基于k-匿名原则的推理阶段防御方法，同时发现微调会进一步加剧隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为表格基础模型预训练于合成数据，隐私风险较低；然而这些模型在推理时通过上下文学习直接使用敏感记录作为标注样本，可能通过注意力机制泄漏成员信息。作者希望揭示这一被忽视的隐私漏洞。

Method: 提出AMIA攻击方法，利用Transformer注意力分布集中性进行无影子模型的成员推断攻击；设计基于k-匿名原则的推理阶段防御，降低上下文键表示的唯一性；通过置信度变化分析微调带来的额外隐私风险。

Result: AMIA在低假阳性率下平均攻击增益为7.7%，超过传统基于置信度的攻击；所提防御使AMIA的成员泄漏降低50%，对置信度攻击降低25%，仅带来3.9%的性能损失；微调后置信度上升的样本更容易遭受成员推断攻击。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型在上下文学习中的注意力机制存在严重的成员信息泄漏风险，微调会放大记忆效应，需采取针对性防御措施。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models are commonly assumed to present limited privacy concerns as they are often pre-trained on large collections of synthetic data. However, these models leverage in-context learning, where sensitive records may be provided directly at inference time as labelled context examples. In this paper, we demonstrate that predictions generated via the attention mechanism leak sufficient information to enable effective Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs). To highlight this vulnerability, we propose AMIA (Attention-based Membership Inference Attack), a shadow-model-free attack that exploits the concentration of transformer attention patterns. Our results show that attention mechanisms reveal strong membership signals, which exceed classical confidence-based attacks, achieving an average gain of 7.7\%, specially in low false-positive regimes. To mitigate this risk, we introduce an inference-time defence inspired by $k$-anonymity principles. This approach reduces the uniqueness of context-key representations without introducing random noise or retraining the model. By targeting only high-risk queries identified through AMIA scores, the defence substantially reduces membership leakage of this attack by an average of 50\% and 25\% against confidence-based attacks, while preserving predictive utility with only 3.9\% performance degradation. Beyond showing that context examples are vulnerable, we further demonstrate that fine-tuning introduces an additional source of privacy risk. In particular, samples whose prediction confidence increases after fine-tuning become more susceptible to MIAs, indicating that fine-tuning can amplify memorisation and expose sensitive training information through confidence shifts.

</details>


### [193] [Can Trustless Agents Be Trusted? An Empirical Study of the ERC-8004 Decentralized AI Agent Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26028)
*Xihan Xiong,Zelin Li,Wei Wei,Qin Wang,William Knottenbelt,Zhipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对ERC-8004协议（AI代理经济体的无需许可信任层）进行了首个实证研究，覆盖Ethereum、BNB Smart Chain和Base三条链。研究发现：大多数身份注册是占位符而非活跃代理；声誉注册表因数值不可通约、反馈缺乏可验证交互基础、可被低成本操纵而无法作为信任信号；大量评审者表现出协同女巫行为。去除女巫标记后，15.5%-89.4%的被评级代理没有有效反馈。基于发现提出了协议修订建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理在跨组织边界交易时面临基础性信任挑战：如何评估未知对手方的可信度。ERC-8004协议虽已被快速采纳，但从未经过实证研究，不清楚其记录的信息是否为决策提供可信基础。

Method: 对ERC-8004在Ethereum、BNB Smart Chain、Base三条链上进行实证研究，覆盖从协议部署到2026年5月13日的数据。爬取链上身份和声誉事件、链下文件以及x402支付交易。

Result: 1) 绝大多数身份注册为占位符，仅3%、4%、15%在三链上存在有效注册文件和至少一个实时服务端点。2) 声誉注册表无法作为信任信号：数值不可通约、反馈记录极少基于可验证交互、声誉可低成本操纵。3) 大量评审者存在协同女巫行为（73.6%、59.2%、90.6%）。4) 去除女巫标记后，15.5%、72.3%、89.4%的被评级代理无有效反馈。

Conclusion: ERC-8004协议当前设计存在重大缺陷，无法提供可靠的信任基础。研究提出了具体的协议修订建议，为AI代理市场研究建立了实证基线。

Abstract: As autonomous AI agents increasingly transact across organizational boundaries, a fundamental trust challenge emerges: how can an agent assess whether an unknown counterpart is trustworthy? The ERC-8004 protocol addresses this challenge with the first permissionless trust layer for AI agent economies, built around three on-chain registries for Identity, Reputation, and Validation. Despite its rapid adoption, the protocol has not been studied empirically, leaving it unclear whether the information it records provides a trustworthy basis for decision-making. To address this gap, we present the first empirical study of ERC-8004 across three chains: Ethereum, BNB Smart Chain (BSC), and Base, covering the period from protocol deployment through May 13, 2026. We crawl on-chain Identity and Reputation events, off-chain files, and x402 payment transactions.
  On the identity side, we find that most registrations are placeholders rather than active agents, with only a small fraction (3%, 4%, and 15% across Ethereum, BSC, and Base) exposing a valid ERC-8004 registration file with at least one live service endpoint. On the reputation side, we show that the Registry, as currently deployed, cannot function as a trust signal: values are not commensurable, feedback records are rarely grounded in verifiable interactions, and reputation can be manipulated at minimal cost. Consistent with these design weaknesses, we find that a substantial fraction of reviewers (73.6%, 59.2%, and 90.6% across Ethereum, BSC, and Base) exhibit coordinated Sybil behavior. After removing Sybil-flagged feedback, 15.5%, 72.3%, and 89.4% of rated agents, respectively, are left with no valid feedback. We then turn these findings into concrete recommendations for future revisions of ERC-8004. Our study yields actionable protocol-design implications and establishes an empirical baseline for research on AI agent markets.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [TabClean: Reusable LLM-Synthesized Programs for Tabular Data Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25388)
*Yibo Wang,Riteng Zhang,Yinghao He,Yongye Su,Bharat Bhargava,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: TabClean是一个无需模型训练的表格数据清洗系统，它利用LLM推理生成可复用的防护型清洗程序，通过证据支持的防护修复子句实现确定性转换，在六个基准测试中达到高精度，显著降低了重复运行时的API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据清洗系统存在不同权衡：基于约束的系统需要专家指定规则，基于学习的系统需要标注或重新训练，而基于LLM的清洗器虽然减少了设置成本，但其对每一行、每个单元格或重复工作步骤调用LLM的方式导致成本随表格规模和批次增长而线性增加。

Method: TabClean包含五个步骤：1) 分析表格证据，2) 诊断修复机制，3) 合成可执行的Python转换，4) 通过单元格级反馈验证候选程序，5) 提交最佳程序供模式兼容批次复用。核心抽象是"证据支持的防护修复子句"（evidence-backed guarded repair clause），只有当脏数据模式、目标-负面条件、证据支持和范围约束同时满足时，确定性转换才会执行。

Result: 在六个基准测试上，TabClean实现了高精度，在五个数据集上F1分数优于代表性的基于规则、基于学习和基于LLM的基线方法，并通过将重复的LLM推理替换为确定性程序执行，大幅降低了重复运行时的API成本。

Conclusion: TabClean成功地将LLM的推理能力编译为可复用的确定性程序，在保持高清洗质量的同时解决了基于LLM的清洗方法成本随数据规模增长的问题，为生产环境中的表格数据清洗提供了一种高效、经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable analytics and machine-learning pipelines depend on clean tabular data, yet production tables often contain missing values, typographical errors, inconsistent formats, violated dependencies, unit mismatches, and ambiguous categorical values. Existing cleaning systems make different trade-offs. Constraint-based systems need experts to specify rules. Learning-based systems need labels or retraining. Recent LLM-based cleaners reduce setup effort, but many call an LLM on rows, cells, or repeated workflow steps, so their cost grows with table size and with every recurring batch.
  We present TabClean, a model-training-free system that compiles LLM reasoning into reusable guarded cleaning programs. Given a dirty table and a small annotated development set, TabClean profiles table evidence, diagnoses repair mechanisms, synthesizes executable Python transformations, validates candidates with cell-level feedback, and commits the best program for reuse on schema-compatible batches. The key abstraction is an evidence-backed guarded repair clause. A deterministic transformation may fire only when its dirty pattern, target-negative condition, evidence support, and scope constraints are satisfied. Across six benchmarks, TabClean achieves high precision, improves F1 over representative rule-based, learning-based, and LLM-based baselines on five datasets, and substantially reduces recurring runtime and API cost by replacing repeated LLM inference with deterministic program execution.

</details>
